# Deuteronomy 32:1-52 derivation — Onkelos + the Sifrei on Deuteronomy as the spine (THE SPINE IN FORCE — thirty-six piskaot 306-341 on 32:1-52, 249 rows read whole in both files, every head present, ONE HEAD MISPRINTED; the ELEVEN rows beyond the spine citing the chapter, found by the union of both files' citations and read whole — eight reread, three fresh, none excluded) — THE LEAN FORM (owner-ruled 2026-09-23) in FOUR RUNS + the tail by the split's byte plan

THE DEUTERONOMY WALK sitting 20 — CHAPTER 32, THE SONG, LEAN, Chapter 32 — the song: give ear, O heavens, and hear, O earth; the Rock whose work is perfect, a perverse and crooked generation; remember the days of old — the Most High divided the nations by the number of the children of Israel, the LORD's portion is His people; He found him in a desert land, as an eagle over her young, the LORD alone led him, honey from the rock, the blood of the grape; Jeshurun grew fat and kicked, forsook the God who made him, sacrificed to demons, to gods they knew not; the LORD saw and spurned, I will hide My face, a perverse generation, I will provoke them with a foolish nation, a fire kindled to the depths of Sheol, arrows spent, the teeth of beasts, the sword without and terror within; I would have said I will blot them out, but for the enemy's boast; a nation void of counsel — how should one chase a thousand unless their Rock had sold them; their vine of Sodom, their wine the venom of asps; vengeance is Mine and recompense, the LORD will judge His people when their power is gone — where are their gods who ate the fat of their sacrifices? See now that I, I am He, I kill and I make alive, I lift My hand to heaven, My glittering sword, vengeance on My adversaries, the blood of His servants — sing, O nations, of His people. Moses and Hoshea son of Nun spoke the song; set your heart to all these words, it is your life; and on that selfsame day: go up to Mount Nebo in the Abarim, see the land and die on the mountain as Aaron died on Hor, because you broke faith at the waters of Meribath-kadesh — you shall see the land from afar but not go there. (2026-09-27; the owner: "Continue" after sitting 19b's tail and the push c4b14ce with no compaction between, "Reread and go" after the compactions, "Continue" for RUN 3 in RUN 2's window — the thirteenth sitting of THE LEAN PASS, its seventh reading and THE FIRST WITH THE SPINE IN FORCE since chapter 26, read in FOUR RUNS under the cost rules' cap: RUN 1 the measurements, the ink and the design without a row; RUN 2 Onkelos, the outside rows and piska 306 whole; RUN 3 piskaot 307-322; RUN 4 piskaot 323-341, this ledger, the manifests, the seat and the chain — the state doc's #231 to #234). THE UNITS: deu_32_haazinu (32:1-43, the song) and deu_32_song_aftermath (32:44-52, the frame, the charge, the summons to Nebo); NO PORTION EDGE INSIDE THE CHAPTER (Haazinu is 32:1-52 whole; Vezot Habrachah opens at 33:1) — the chapter the unit (CHAPTER NUMBERS). DECLARED (THE_STEPS Step 2, the spine default of 2026-08-27 — Deuteronomy = the Sifrei on Deuteronomy + Onkelos — read at the chapter's grain; THE LEAN PASS: the spine and Onkelos whole, no docket, the Mishnah rows the shelf names the compile's cases): Onkelos Deuteronomy 32:1-52 (52 DB verses — the export's rows, the identity, asserted) + THE SIFREI ON DEUTERONOMY IN FORCE — piskaot 306-341 on 32:1-52 (249 rows read whole here and this ledger's source, 357,985 bytes in the split's thirty-six files; the export's longest piska 306 on 32:1 with thirty-seven rows; every head present and a chapter-32 citation, 305 headless before on 31:14-23 and 342 headless after on 33:1; no tail folded, the edges clean — the split's asserts; ## Sifrei Devarim piskaot 306-341 — the spine IN FORCE on 32:1-52 (the heads: 306 on 32:1, 307 on 32:4, 308 on 32:5, 309 on 32:6, 310 on 32:7, 311 on 32:8, 312 on 32:9, 313 on 32:10, 314 on 32:11, 315 on 32:12, 316 on 32:13, 317 on 32:14, 318 on 32:15, 319 on 32:18, 320 on 32:19, 321 on 32:23, 322 on 32:26, 323 on 32:29, 324 on 32:34, 325 on 32:35, 326 on 32:36, 327 on 32:37, 328 on 32:35, 329 on 32:39, 330 on 32:40, 331 on 32:41, 332 on 32:42, 333 on 32:43, 334 on 32:44, 335 on 32:46, 336 on 32:47, 337 on 32:48, 338 on 32:49, 339 on 32:50, 340 on 32:51, 341 on 32:52; 249 rows read whole in both files at the shelf's row grain, none credited, 30 read before and reread whole (computed); the rows in the shelf's order over five row files by the split's byte plan; the rows citing nothing in either file: 306:29, 306:33, 315:5, 320:4, 320:9, 321:5, 322:11, 323:12, 327:2, 328:2, 333:5)
- Sifrei Devarim 306:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_04_vaetchanan_2026-09-16.md). On 32:1. "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS, AND I WILL SPEAK" (32:1) — R. MEIR SAYS: WHEN ISRAEL WERE WORTHY THEY TESTIFIED AGAINST THEMSELVES (Joshua 24:22); WHEN THEY CORRUPTED THEMSELVES (Hosea 12:1) THE TRIBE OF JUDAH AND BENJAMIN TESTIFIED AGAINST THEM (Isaiah 5:3-4); WHEN JUDAH CORRUPTED (Malachi 2:11) THE PROPHETS TESTIFIED (2 Kings 17:13); WHEN THEY MOCKED THE PROPHETS (2 Chronicles 36:16) HE CALLED THE HEAVENS TO WITNESS — "I CALL HEAVEN AND EARTH TO WITNESS AGAINST YOU THIS DAY" (4:26); THEY CORRUPTED WITH THE HEAVENS (Jeremiah 7:17-18, THE QUEEN OF HEAVEN) — THE EARTH (Jeremiah 6:19); THE EARTH (Hosea 12:12) — THE ROADS (Jeremiah 6:16); THE ROADS (Ezekiel 16:25) — THE NATIONS (Jeremiah 6:18); THE NATIONS (Psalm 106:35) — THE MOUNTAINS (Micah 6:2); THE MOUNTAINS (Hosea 4:13) — THE BEAST (Isaiah 1:3); THE BEAST (Psalm 106:20) — THE BIRDS (Jeremiah 8:7); BEAST AND CREEPING THING AND BIRD (Ezekiel 8:10) — THE FISH (Job 12:8); THE FISH (Habakkuk 1:14) — THE ANT (Proverbs 6:6-8); R. SHIMON BEN ELAZAR: WRETCHED IS THE MAN WHO MUST LEARN FROM THE ANT — HAD HE LEARNED AND DONE HE WERE WRETCHED; HE MUST LEARN FROM HER WAYS AND HAS NOT. The head row of the spine's return (כְּשֶׁהָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל זַכָּאִים הֵם הָיוּ מְעִידִים בְעַצְמָם "when Israel were worthy they testified against themselves"; הֵעִיד בָּהֶם אֶת הַנְּמָלָה "He called the ant to witness against them"; עָלוּב הָיָה אָדָם זֶה "wretched was this man"): THE LADDER OF WITNESSES — eleven witnesses in descent from Israel's own mouth to the ant, heaven and earth the fourth and fifth rungs; 4:26's summons the seat that read this row at chapter 4 — the song's "give ear, O heavens" the same summons in the song's mouth (the compile: heaven_and_earth_witness the reused effect, four writes on the tape — 4:26, 30:19, 31:28 and the 19b marker's day; 32:1 the fifth seat at 20b). The Prophets' and the Writings' seats (twenty-two of them) cited, never read. DV32-01. REREAD WHOLE (chapter 4's reading — 4:26).
- Sifrei Devarim 306:2 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_29_31_nitzavim_vayelech_2026-09-27.md). On 32:1. IN TIME TO COME THE COMMUNITY OF ISRAEL WILL SAY BEFORE THE HOLY ONE: MASTER OF THE WORLD, MY WITNESSES STAND — "I CALL HEAVEN AND EARTH TO WITNESS AGAINST YOU THIS DAY" (30:19); HE SAYS: I WILL REMOVE THEM — "I CREATE NEW HEAVENS AND A NEW EARTH" (Isaiah 65:17); I SEE THE PLACES WHERE I SINNED AND AM ASHAMED (Jeremiah 2:23) — I WILL REMOVE THEM, "EVERY VALLEY SHALL BE LIFTED" (Isaiah 40:4); MY NAME STANDS — "YOU SHALL BE CALLED BY A NEW NAME" (Isaiah 62:2); MY NAME IS CALLED AFTER THE BAALS — "I WILL REMOVE THE NAMES OF THE BAALS" (Hosea 2:19); MY HOUSEHOLD STILL MENTIONS IT — "THEY SHALL NO MORE BE REMEMBERED BY NAME"; AGAIN TOMORROW SHE WILL SAY: YOU WROTE "IF A MAN SENDS AWAY HIS WIFE … SHALL HE RETURN TO HER?" (Jeremiah 3:1) — HE SAYS: DID I WRITE ANYTHING BUT "A MAN"? "FOR I AM GOD AND NOT A MAN" (Hosea 11:9); ANOTHER WORD: ARE YOU DIVORCED FROM ME, HOUSE OF ISRAEL? "WHERE IS YOUR MOTHER'S BILL OF DIVORCE?" (Isaiah 50:1). (הֲרֵי עֵדַי קַיָּמִים "behold, my witnesses stand"; הֲרֵינִי מַעֲבִירָם "behold, I remove them"; וְכִי גְרוּשִׁים אַתֶּם לִי בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל "are you divorced from Me, house of Israel"): THE WITNESSES REMOVED — 30:19's heaven and earth outlived by their own abolition (new heavens, a new earth), the marriage never dissolved (Jeremiah 3:1's "a man" excludes God): the seat that read this row at sitting 19 (30:19); here the song's summons of 32:1 is the summons the future dialogue undoes. The Prophets' seats cited, never read. The compile: no law — a retelling ahead of the tape's end (DV32-01). REREAD WHOLE (sitting 19's reading — 30:19).
- Sifrei Devarim 306:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS" — A PARABLE OF A KING WHO HANDED HIS SON TO A PEDAGOGUE TO SIT AND WATCH HIM; THE SON SAID: FATHER THINKS HE GAINED BY THIS — I WILL WATCH HIM TILL HE EATS, DRINKS AND SLEEPS AND GO DO MY OWN AFFAIRS; THE FATHER: I GAVE YOU TO THE PEDAGOGUE ONLY SO HE WOULD NEVER LET YOU MOVE. SO MOSES TO ISRAEL: DO YOU THINK TO FLEE FROM UNDER THE WINGS OF THE SHEKHINAH OR TO MOVE OFF THE EARTH? THE HEAVENS WRITE — "THE HEAVENS REVEAL HIS INIQUITY" (Job 20:27), AND THE EARTH KNOWS — "THE EARTH RISES UP AGAINST HIM". IN TIME TO COME THE COMMUNITY OF ISRAEL WILL STAND IN JUDGMENT: MASTER OF THE WORLD, I KNOW NOT WHO CORRUPTED WHOM, WHO CHANGED TOWARD WHOM — ISRAEL TOWARD THE PLACE OR THE PLACE TOWARD ISRAEL; WHEN IT SAYS "THE HEAVENS DECLARE HIS RIGHTEOUSNESS" (Psalm 50:6) — ISRAEL CORRUPTED, THE PLACE DID NOT CHANGE: "FOR I THE LORD HAVE NOT CHANGED" (Malachi 3:6). (שֶׁמָּא אַתֶּם סְבוּרִים לִבְרֹחַ מִתַּחַת כַּנְפֵי שְׁכִינָה "do you think to flee from under the wings of the Shekhinah"; מִי קִלְקֵל בְּמִי וּמִי שִׁנָּה בְּמִי "who corrupted whom and who changed toward whom"): THE WITNESSES AS WARDERS — heaven and earth the pedagogue no one escapes, and the record-keepers whose testimony settles the future trial (the heavens declare His righteousness — the fault Israel's). The compile: no law — the Speaker's constancy a status (Malachi 3:6 the Prophets' seat, cited). DV32-01. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:4 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_11_ekev_reeh_2026-09-20.md, deu_26_28_ki_tavo_2026-09-26.md). On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS" — R. YEHUDAH SAYS: A PARABLE OF A KING WITH TWO GUARDIANS IN A PROVINCE; HE ENTRUSTED THEM WITH HIS GOODS AND HIS SON: AS LONG AS MY SON DOES MY WILL, PAMPER HIM, FEED HIM, GIVE HIM DRINK; WHEN HE DOES NOT, LET HIM TASTE NOTHING OF MINE. SO WHEN ISRAEL DO THE WILL OF THE PLACE — "THE LORD WILL OPEN TO YOU HIS GOOD TREASURY, THE HEAVENS" (28:12); WHEN THEY DO NOT — "THE LORD'S ANGER WILL BE KINDLED AGAINST YOU AND HE WILL SHUT UP THE HEAVENS, THERE WILL BE NO RAIN AND THE GROUND WILL NOT YIELD ITS PRODUCE" (11:17). (כָּל זְמַן שֶׁבְּנִי עוֹשֶׂה רְצוֹנִי "as long as my son does my will"; אַל יִטְעֹם מִשֶּׁלִּי כְּלוּם "let him taste nothing of mine"): HEAVEN AND EARTH THE TWO GUARDIANS — the rain's two arms (28:12 opened, 11:17 shut) the guardians' orders; the seat that read this row at chapter 11 and again at chapters 26-28 (the blessing's treasury). The compile: the heavens_opened and heavens_shut state on the tape (28:12, 11:17) — the song's witnesses the same two agents (DV32-01). REREAD WHOLE (chapter 11's reading and chapters 26-28's).
- Sifrei Devarim 306:5 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS" — R. NEHEMIAH SAYS: A PARABLE OF A KING WHOSE SON WENT OUT TO EVIL WAYS; HE BEGAN TO COMPLAIN OF HIM TO HIS BROTHERS, HIS FRIENDS, HIS NEIGHBORS, HIS RELATIVES — THE FATHER DID NOT CEASE COMPLAINING UNTIL HE SAID: HEAVEN AND EARTH, TO WHOM SHALL I COMPLAIN OF YOU BUT TO THESE? THEREFORE IT SAYS "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS, AND I WILL SPEAK". (שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ לְמִי אֶקְבֹּל עָלֶיךָ חוּץ מֵאֵלּוּ "heaven and earth — to whom shall I complain of you but to these"): THE SUMMONS AS A FATHER'S LAST COMPLAINT — the witnesses the only hearers left; the compile: no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:6 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_11_ekev_reeh_2026-09-20.md, deu_26_28_ki_tavo_2026-09-26.md). On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS" — R. YEHUDAH SAYS: WERE THERE NO OTHER MERIT OF THE RIGHTEOUS THAN THAT THEY WIDEN THE WORLD THEY ARE IN — WHEN ISRAEL DO THE WILL OF THE PLACE, "THE LORD WILL OPEN TO YOU HIS GOOD TREASURY, THE HEAVENS" (28:12), AND OPENING IS A WORD OF WIDENING — "AND HE OPENED HER WOMB" (Genesis 29:31); AND WERE THERE NO OTHER FAULT OF THE WICKED THAN THAT THEY CRAMP THE WORLD THEY ARE IN — WHEN THEY DO NOT, "THE LORD'S ANGER WILL BE KINDLED AND HE WILL SHUT UP THE HEAVENS" (11:17), AND SHUTTING IS A WORD OF CRAMPING — "FOR THE LORD HAD FAST SHUT UP" (Genesis 20:18). (שֶׁמַּרְוִיחִים אֶת הָעוֹלָם שֶׁהֵם בְּתוֹכוֹ "that they widen the world they are in"; וְאֵין פְּתִיחָה אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן הַרְוָחָה "and opening is only a word of widening"; וְאֵין עֲצִירָה אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן הַדֹּחַק "and shutting is only a word of cramping"): TWO WORDS OF THE RAIN VERSES TAUGHT BY TWO GENESIS SEATS — open = widen (Leah's womb), shut = cramp (Gerar's wombs): the tape's own entries (Genesis 20:18, 29:31) the teachers of 28:12 and 11:17 (the Link Review Law — a verbal analogy the spine itself draws, a teacher named); the seat that read this row at chapters 11 and 26-28. The compile: no law (DV32-01). REREAD WHOLE (chapter 11's reading and chapters 26-28's).
- Sifrei Devarim 306:7 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS, AND I WILL SPEAK" — THE HOLY ONE SAID TO MOSES: TELL ISRAEL, LOOK AT THE HEAVENS I CREATED TO SERVE YOU — HAVE THEY CHANGED THEIR MEASURE? HAS THE SUN'S ORB SAID: I WILL NOT RISE IN THE EAST AND LIGHT THE WHOLE WORLD? RATHER "THE SUN RISES AND THE SUN SETS" (Ecclesiastes 1:5), AND MORE — IT REJOICES TO DO MY WILL: "HE IS AS A BRIDEGROOM COMING OUT OF HIS CHAMBER" (Psalm 19:6). (הִסְתַּכְּלוּ בַּשָּׁמַיִם שֶׁבָּרָאתִי לְשַׁמֶּשְׁכֶם "look at the heavens I created to serve you"; שֶׁמָּא שִׁנּוּ אֶת מִדָּתָם "have they changed their measure"): THE HEAVENS' UNCHANGED MEASURE — the argument from the created order the song's first witness carries (the compile: the sun's course a fixed law of the world, the calendar's clock; no verdict). The Writings' seats cited, never read. DV32-01. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:8 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. "AND LET THE EARTH HEAR THE WORDS OF MY MOUTH" — LOOK AT THE EARTH I CREATED TO SERVE YOU: HAS IT CHANGED ITS MEASURE? DID YOU SOW AND IT DID NOT SPROUT, SOW WHEAT AND IT RAISED BARLEY? HAS THIS COW SAID: I WILL NOT THRESH NOR PLOW TODAY, OR THIS ASS: I WILL NOT CARRY NOR GO? SO OF THE SEA — "WILL YOU NOT FEAR ME … WHO SET THE SAND A BOUND FOR THE SEA" (Jeremiah 5:22): SINCE I DECREED IT HAS IT CHANGED ITS MEASURE AND SAID I WILL RISE AND FLOOD THE WORLD? RATHER "I BROKE MY DECREE UPON IT, SET BAR AND DOORS, AND SAID: THUS FAR YOU SHALL COME AND NO FURTHER" (Job 38:10-11), AND MORE — IT GRIEVES AND KNOWS NOT WHAT TO DO ("THEY TOSS AND CANNOT"). AND IS IT NOT AN A-FORTIORI: THESE, MADE NEITHER FOR REWARD NOR FOR LOSS — WORTHY THEY GET NO REWARD, SINNING THEY GET NO PUNISHMENT, THEY PITY NOT THEIR SONS AND DAUGHTERS — HAVE NOT CHANGED THEIR MEASURE; YOU, WHO ARE REWARDED WHEN WORTHY AND PUNISHED WHEN YOU SIN AND PITY YOUR SONS AND DAUGHTERS — HOW MUCH MORE MUST YOU NOT CHANGE YOUR MEASURES. (הִסְתַּכְּלוּ בָאָרֶץ שֶׁבָּרָאתִי לְשַׁמֶּשְׁכֶם "look at the earth I created to serve you"; שֶׁמָּא זְרַעְתֶּם חִטִּים וְהֶעֱלָת שְׂעוֹרִים "did you sow wheat and it raised barley"; וּמָה אֵלּוּ שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ לֹא לְשָׂכָר וְלֹא לְהֶפְסֵד "if these, made neither for reward nor for loss"): THE EARTH'S AND THE SEA'S UNCHANGED MEASURE and the a-fortiori (I1) to Israel — the unrewarded creation keeps its law, the rewarded people should the more; the compile: the natural order's constancy the frame of every law with reward attached (the blessing and the curse on the tape). The Prophets' and the Writings' seats cited, never read. DV32-01. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:9 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_11_ekev_reeh_2026-09-20.md, deu_17_18_shoftim_2026-09-24.md). On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS" — R. BANAYAH USED TO SAY: WHEN A MAN IS CONDEMNED IN JUDGMENT NONE LAYS HAND ON HIM FIRST BUT HIS WITNESSES — "THE HAND OF THE WITNESSES SHALL BE FIRST UPON HIM" (17:7), AND THEN THE PEOPLE COME — "AND THE HAND OF ALL THE PEOPLE AFTERWARD". SO WHEN ISRAEL DO NOT DO THE WILL OF THE PLACE — "THE LORD'S ANGER WILL BE KINDLED AGAINST YOU AND HE WILL SHUT UP THE HEAVENS" (11:17), AND THEN THE PUNISHMENTS COME — "AND YOU SHALL PERISH QUICKLY"; AND WHEN THEY DO — "ON THAT DAY, SAYS THE LORD, I WILL ANSWER THE HEAVENS" (Hosea 2:23), "AND I WILL SOW HER FOR ME IN THE LAND" (Hosea 2:25). (אֵין פּוֹשְׁטִים בּוֹ יָד תְּחִלָּה אֶלָּא עֵדָיו "none lays hand on him first but his witnesses"; וְאַחַר כָּךְ פֻּרְעָנֻיּוֹת מְמַשְׁמְשׁוֹת וּבָאוֹת "and afterward the punishments come on"): THE COURT'S RULE APPLIED TO THE COSMIC WITNESSES — 17:7's order (the witnesses' hand first, the people's after) the pattern of 11:17's order (the heavens shut first, the plagues after): the compiled court law (the witnesses' hand, refuge_war_family and the court runner on the tape) read as the model of the curse's sequence — a REFERENCE the spine draws between two compiled spans (the teacher named for the compile's edge if one is written); the seat that read this row at chapter 11 and at chapters 17-18. The Prophets' seats cited, never read. DV32-01. REREAD WHOLE (chapter 11's reading and chapters 17-18's — 17:7).
- Sifrei Devarim 306:10 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS, AND I WILL SPEAK" — R. YEHUDAH BEN CHANANYAH USED TO SAY: WHEN MOSES SAID "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS" THE HEAVENS AND THE HEAVENS OF HEAVENS FELL SILENT, AND WHEN HE SAID "LET THE EARTH HEAR THE WORDS OF MY MOUTH" THE EARTH AND ALL UPON IT FELL SILENT; IF YOU WONDER, SEE WHAT IS SAID OF JOSHUA — "SUN, STAND STILL AT GIBEON, AND MOON IN THE VALLEY OF AIJALON; AND THE SUN STOOD STILL AND THE MOON STAYED … THERE WAS NO DAY LIKE IT BEFORE" (Joshua 10:12-14): WE LEARN THAT THE RIGHTEOUS RULE THE WHOLE WORLD. (הָיוּ הַשָּׁמַיִם וּשְׁמֵי הַשָּׁמַיִם דּוֹמְמִים "the heavens and the heavens of heavens were silent"; שֶׁהַצַּדִּיקִים שׁוֹלְטִים בְּכָל הָעוֹלָם כֻּלּוֹ "that the righteous rule the whole world"): THE SUMMONS OBEYED — the silence of heaven and earth at the song's first words, Gibeon's sun the proof (the Prophets' seat cited, never read); the compile: no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:11 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS" — BECAUSE MOSES WAS NEAR THE HEAVENS HE SAID "GIVE EAR (ha'azinu — attend closely), O HEAVENS", AND BECAUSE HE WAS FAR FROM THE EARTH HE SAID "AND LET THE EARTH HEAR (tishma) THE WORDS OF MY MOUTH"; ISAIAH CAME AND SUPPORTED THE MATTER — "HEAR, O HEAVENS" FOR HE WAS FAR FROM THE HEAVENS, "AND GIVE EAR, O EARTH" FOR HE WAS NEAR THE EARTH (Isaiah 1:2). (לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה מֹשֶׁה קָרוֹב לַשָּׁמַיִם "because Moses was near the heavens"; וּלְפִי שֶׁהָיָה רָחוֹק מִן הָאָרֶץ "and because he was far from the earth"; בָּא יְשַׁעְיָה וְסָמַךְ לַדָּבָר "Isaiah came and supported the matter"): THE TWIN TAUGHT — Isaiah 1:2's inverted summons (the measure's twin by sense with no shared token) here paired by the spine itself, the two verbs read by the speakers' distance from their hearers: THE LINK REVIEW LAW satisfied — the teacher names the pair (a REFERENCE between the song and the Prophets' seat, cited, never read). The compile: no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:12 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: BECAUSE THE HEAVENS ARE MANY HE OPENED TO THEM IN THE PLURAL, AND BECAUSE THE EARTH IS ONE HE OPENED TO HER IN THE SINGULAR — "AND LET THE EARTH HEAR THE WORDS OF MY MOUTH"; ISAIAH CAME AND SUPPORTED IT — "HEAR, O HEAVENS, AND GIVE EAR, O EARTH" — THE PLURAL TO THE MANY AND THE SINGULAR TO THE ONE. BUT THE SAGES SAY: NOT SO — WHEN WITNESSES COME AND TESTIFY, IF THEIR WORDS AGREE AS ONE THEIR TESTIMONY STANDS, AND IF NOT IT DOES NOT; SO HAD MOSES SAID "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS" AND STOPPED, THE HEAVENS WOULD SAY WE HEARD ONLY BY ATTENDING, AND HAD HE SAID "LET THE EARTH HEAR" THE EARTH WOULD SAY I HEARD ONLY BY HEARING; ISAIAH CAME AND GAVE ATTENDING AND HEARING TO THE HEAVENS AND ATTENDING AND HEARING TO THE EARTH. (לְפִי שֶׁהַשָּׁמַיִם מְרֻבִּים פָּתַח לָהֶם בְּלָשׁוֹן מְרֻבָּה "because the heavens are many he opened to them in the plural"; וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים אֵין הַדָּבָר כֵּן "but the sages say: the matter is not so"; אִם נִמְצְאוּ דִּבְרֵיהֶם מְכֻוָּנִים כְּאֶחָד עֵדוּתָם קַיֶּמֶת "if their words are found to agree as one their testimony stands"): THE GRAMMAR OF THE TWO VERBS — the plural imperative (ha'azinu) and the singular jussive (tishma) the ink itself carries (the measure's morph codes: HVhv2mp, HVqj3fs); the sages' reading makes Isaiah 1:2 the second deposition that completes the first — THE LAW OF AGREEING WITNESSES (Mishnah Sanhedrin 5:2 the testing shelf, cited; the compiled court law on the tape) applied to heaven and earth. The compile: a reference to the court runner's agreement rule, a teacher named. DV32-01. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:13 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS, AND I WILL SPEAK" — BECAUSE THE TORAH WAS GIVEN FROM THE HEAVENS: "YOU HAVE SEEN THAT I SPOKE WITH YOU FROM THE HEAVENS" (Exodus 20:19); "AND LET THE EARTH HEAR THE WORDS OF MY MOUTH" — ON WHICH ISRAEL STOOD AND SAID "ALL THAT THE LORD HAS SPOKEN WE WILL DO AND HEAR" (Exodus 24:7). (עַל שֵׁם שֶׁנִּתְּנָה תּוֹרָה מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם "because the Torah was given from the heavens"; שֶׁעָלֶיהָ עָמְדוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאָמְרוּ "on which Israel stood and said"): THE TWO WITNESSES AS THE COVENANT'S TWO PLACES — the heavens the Giver's, the earth the receivers' (Exodus 20:19 and 24:7 the tape's own entries — RUN CITATIONS); the compile: no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:14 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in gen_04_lights_calendar_2026-07-30.md). On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS" — BECAUSE ISRAEL DID NOT DO THE COMMANDMENTS GIVEN THEM FROM THE HEAVENS, AND THESE ARE THEY: THE INTERCALATION OF YEARS AND THE FIXING OF MONTHS — "AND THEY SHALL BE FOR SIGNS AND FOR SEASONS AND FOR DAYS AND YEARS" (Genesis 1:14); "AND LET THE EARTH HEAR" — BECAUSE THEY DID NOT DO THE COMMANDMENTS GIVEN THEM ON THE EARTH, AND THESE ARE THEY: GLEANINGS, THE FORGOTTEN SHEAF AND THE CORNER, THE HEAVE-OFFERING AND THE TITHES, THE SABBATICAL YEARS AND THE JUBILEES. (עִבּוּר שָׁנִים וּקְבִיעוּת חֳדָשִׁים "the intercalation of years and the fixing of months"; לֶקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה תְּרוּמָה וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת שְׁמִטִּים וְיוֹבְלוֹת "gleanings, the forgotten sheaf and the corner, the heave-offering and the tithes, sabbaticals and jubilees"): THE TWO WITNESSES' OWN COMMANDMENTS — THE CALENDAR from the heavens (the intercalation and the new moon's fixing, Genesis 1:14's lights: the seat that read this row at the Genesis calendar ledger; the compile: the calendar's parameters on the tape — 16:1's intercalation a law of 'observe', the count era's rows) and THE LAND'S GIFTS from the earth (24:19-21's gleanings and forgotten sheaf, Leviticus 19:9's corner, 18:4's heave-offering, 14:22's and 26:12's tithes, 15:1's release, Leviticus 25's jubilee — every one a compiled span or a cited seat on the tape); the compile: the row a REFERENCE list — the song's witnesses answer for the laws under their names. DV32-01. REREAD WHOLE (the Genesis calendar ledger's reading — Genesis 1:14).
- Sifrei Devarim 306:15 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_29_31_nitzavim_vayelech_2026-09-27.md). On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "GIVE EAR, O HEAVENS" — BECAUSE THEY DID NOT DO ALL THE COMMANDMENTS GIVEN FROM THE HEAVENS, "AND LET THE EARTH HEAR" — ALL THOSE GIVEN ON THE EARTH. MOSES CALLED AGAINST ISRAEL TWO WITNESSES THAT STAND FOREVER AND EVER — "I CALL HEAVEN AND EARTH TO WITNESS AGAINST YOU THIS DAY" (30:19); AND THE HOLY ONE CALLED THE SONG TO WITNESS — "NOW WRITE FOR YOURSELVES THIS SONG" (31:19). WE KNOW NOT WHOSE TESTIMONY STANDS, THE HOLY ONE'S OR MOSES'; WHEN IT SAYS "AND THIS SONG SHALL ANSWER BEFORE HIM AS A WITNESS" (31:21) — THE HOLY ONE'S CONFIRMS MOSES', NOT MOSES' THE HOLY ONE'S. WHY DID MOSES CALL TWO WITNESSES THAT LIVE FOREVER? HE SAID: I AM FLESH AND BLOOD AND TOMORROW I DIE — IF ISRAEL SAY WE NEVER RECEIVED THE TORAH, WHO WILL CONTRADICT THEM? AND THE HOLY ONE CALLED THE SONG: THE SONG SHALL TESTIFY FROM BELOW AND I FROM ABOVE; AND WHENCE THAT THE PLACE IS CALLED A WITNESS — "I WILL BE A SWIFT WITNESS" (Malachi 3:5), "I KNOW AND AM WITNESS, SAYS THE LORD" (Jeremiah 29:23), "THE LORD GOD BE A WITNESS AGAINST YOU FROM HIS HOLY TEMPLE" (Micah 1:2). (מֹשֶׁה הֵעִיד בָּהֶם בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל שְׁנֵי עֵדִים "Moses called two witnesses against Israel"; וְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא הֵעִיד בָּהֶם אֶת הַשִּׁירָה "and the Holy One called the song to witness against them"; אֲנִי בָּשָׂר וָדָם וּלְמָחָר אֲנִי מֵת "I am flesh and blood and tomorrow I die"; הַשִּׁירָה תָּעִיד בָּהֶם מִלְּמַטָּה וַאֲנִי מִלְמַעְלָה "the song shall testify against them from below and I from above"): THE THREE WITNESSES OF THE COVENANT — heaven and earth (Moses', 30:19), the song (the Holy One's, 31:19-21), and the Place Himself: the song's own office as WITNESS stated inside the song's spine — the seat that read this row at sitting 19 (30:19, 31:19, 31:21; 19b compiled the song_commanded and song_written effects at 31:19-22); the compile at 20b: the song's witness office a status, the two eternal witnesses the reused effect (DV32-01). Moses' "tomorrow I die" the marker's day (40, 12, 7) in the spine's own words. The Prophets' seats cited, never read. REREAD WHOLE (sitting 19's reading — 30:19 and 31:19-21).
- Sifrei Devarim 306:16 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_05_vaetchanan_2026-09-16.md). On 32:1. "MY DOCTRINE SHALL DROP AS THE RAIN" (32:2) — MY DOCTRINE (likchi) IS ONLY WORDS OF TORAH: "FOR I GIVE YOU GOOD DOCTRINE, FORSAKE NOT MY TORAH" (Proverbs 4:2); "TAKE MY INSTRUCTION AND NOT SILVER" (Proverbs 8:10) — AND INSTRUCTION IS ONLY WORDS OF TORAH: "HEAR, MY SON, YOUR FATHER'S INSTRUCTION AND FORSAKE NOT YOUR MOTHER'S TORAH" (Proverbs 1:8), "HEAR INSTRUCTION AND BE WISE" (Proverbs 8:33), "HOLD FAST TO INSTRUCTION, LET IT NOT GO" (Proverbs 4:13); "TAKE WITH YOU WORDS AND RETURN" (Hosea 14:3) — AND WORDS ARE ONLY WORDS OF TORAH: "THESE WORDS THE LORD SPOKE TO ALL YOUR ASSEMBLY" (5:19). (אֵין לִקְחִי אֶלָּא דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה "my doctrine is only words of Torah"; וְאֵין דְּבָרִים אֶלָּא דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה "and words are only words of Torah"): THE RAIN VERSE OPENED — its first noun fixed as Torah by a chain of Proverbs (the Writings' seats cited, never read) closing on 5:19's "these words" — the Decalogue's words on the tape (the seat that read this row at chapter 5). The compile: the doctrine = the Torah, a definition the song's own word carries; no law (DV32-01). REREAD WHOLE (chapter 5's reading — 5:19).
- Sifrei Devarim 306:17 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. "AS THE RAIN" — AS THE RAIN IS LIFE TO THE WORLD SO WORDS OF TORAH ARE LIFE TO THE WORLD; OR AS THE RAIN — PART OF THE WORLD REJOICES IN IT AND PART GRIEVES (HE WHOSE CISTERN IS FULL OF WINE, WHOSE THRESHING FLOOR IS BEFORE HIM) — COULD WORDS OF TORAH BE SO? IT SAYS "MY SPEECH SHALL DISTIL AS THE DEW": AS ALL THE WORLD REJOICES IN THE DEW SO ALL THE WORLD REJOICES IN WORDS OF TORAH. (מַה מָּטָר חַיִּים לָעוֹלָם אַף דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה "as the rain is life to the world so words of Torah"; מַה טַּל כָּל הָעוֹלָם שְׂמֵחִים בּוֹ "as all the world rejoices in the dew"): RAIN AND DEW — the second clause limits the first (the dew's universal welcome corrects the rain's mixed one): the verse read as a pair whose second member restricts the first (the compile's form: an inclusion then its guard; no law here). DV32-01. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:18 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. "AS THE SMALL RAIN ON THE TENDER GRASS" — AS THESE SHOWERS COME DOWN ON THE HERBS AND RAISE THEM AND MAKE THEM GROW, SO WORDS OF TORAH RAISE YOU AND MAKE YOU GROW — "EXALT HER AND SHE WILL LIFT YOU UP" (Proverbs 4:8). (כָּךְ דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה מַעֲלִים אוֹתְךָ וּמְגַדְּלִים אוֹתְךָ "so words of Torah raise you and make you grow"): the third rain (se'irim) read as growth; the Writings' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:19 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. "AND AS THE SHOWERS ON THE HERB" — AS THESE SHOWERS COME DOWN ON THE HERBS AND DELIGHT THEM AND PAMPER THEM, SO WORDS OF TORAH DELIGHT YOU AND PAMPER YOU — "FOR THEY ARE A GARLAND OF GRACE TO YOUR HEAD" (Proverbs 1:9), "SHE WILL GIVE TO YOUR HEAD A GARLAND OF GRACE" (Proverbs 4:9). (כָּךְ דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה מְעַדְּנִים אוֹתְךָ וּמְפַנְּקִים אוֹתְךָ "so words of Torah delight you and pamper you"): the fourth rain (revivim) read as delight — the four rains of 32:2 each a work of Torah on the student (306:16-19 the first cycle); the Writings' seats cited, never read; no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:20 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "MY DOCTRINE SHALL DROP AS THE RAIN" — R. NEHEMIAH USED TO SAY: ALWAYS GATHER WORDS OF TORAH AS GENERAL RULES; COULD YOU BRING THEM OUT AS GENERAL RULES AS YOU GATHERED THEM? IT SAYS "YA'AROF AS THE RAIN" — AND YA'AROF IS ONLY THE MERCHANT'S TONGUE (leshon kena'ani): A MAN DOES NOT SAY TO HIS FELLOW "CHANGE ME THIS SELA" BUT "AROF (BREAK) ME THIS SELA" — SO GATHER WORDS OF TORAH AS GENERAL RULES AND BREAK THEM OUT AS THESE DROPS OF DEW, NOT AS THESE DROPS OF RAIN WHICH ARE LARGE, BUT AS THE DROPS OF DEW WHICH ARE SMALL. (לְעוֹלָם הֱוֵי כּוֹנֵס דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה כְּלָלִים "always gather words of Torah as general rules"; וְאֵין יַעֲרֹף אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן כְּנַעֲנִי "and ya'arof is only the merchant's tongue"; ״עֲרוֹף לִי סֶלַע זֶה״ "break me this sela"): THE RARE VERB READ BY THE MARKETPLACE — ya'arof (drop) as 'break into small change': learn in generals, teach in particulars, small as dew (the compile's own method named by the spine: the general rule compiled, the cases printed one by one). The translator's note on the merchant sense of kena'ani (Zechariah 14:21, Proverbs 31:24 cited). DV32-01. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:21 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. "AS THE SMALL RAIN ON THE TENDER GRASS" — AS THESE SHOWERS COME DOWN ON THE HERBS AND SEARCH THROUGH THEM SO THEY DO NOT GET WORMY, SO SEARCH THROUGH WORDS OF TORAH SO YOU DO NOT FORGET THEM; SO R. YAAKOV SON OF R. CHANILAI SAID TO RABBI: COME, LET US SEARCH THROUGH THE LAWS SO THEY DO NOT RUST. (כָּךְ הֱוֵי מְפַשְׁפֵּשׁ בְּדִבְרֵי תוֹרָה כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא "so search through words of Torah so that you do not"; בּוֹא וּנְפַשְׁפֵּשׁ בַּהֲלָכוֹת כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲלוּ חֲלוּדָה "come, let us search the laws so they do not rust"): review against forgetting — the second cycle (306:20-22) on the four rains as the method of study; the compile: the sweep's own duty in the tradition's words (the gates rerun so the laws do not rust). DV32-01. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:22 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. "AND AS THE SHOWERS ON THE HERB" — AS THESE SHOWERS COME DOWN ON THE HERBS AND CLEANSE THEM AND FATTEN THEM, SO FATTEN WORDS OF TORAH — REPEAT, AND A THIRD TIME, AND A FOURTH. (כָּךְ הֱוֵי מְפַטֵּם דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה וְשׁוֹנֶה וּמְשַׁלֵּשׁ "so fatten words of Torah — repeat, a third time"): repetition to the fourth — the count of reviews a parameter the tradition sets (the Babylonian Talmud Eruvin 54b's four the testing shelf, cited); no law (DV32-01). - Sifrei Devarim 306:23 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_19_21_shoftim_ki_teitzei_2026-09-24.md). On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "MY DOCTRINE SHALL DROP (ya'arof) AS THE RAIN" — R. FOR BLOODSHED; SO WORDS OF TORAH ATONE FOR ALL TRANSGRESSIONS. - Sifrei Devarim 306:24 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. "AS THE SMALL RAIN (se'irim) ON THE TENDER GRASS" — FOR WHAT DO THE GOATS (se'irim) ATONE? FOR SINS (chataot); SO WORDS OF TORAH ATONE FOR SINS; "AND AS THE SHOWERS ON THE HERB" — AS THE SHOWERS COME WHOLE (temimim) AND ATONE, SO WORDS OF TORAH ATONE FOR ALL INIQUITIES AND TRANSGRESSIONS. (וְעַל מַה שְׂעִירִים מְכַפְּרִים עַל חֲטָאוֹת "and for what do goats atone — for sins"; מַה רְבִיבִים בָּאִים תְּמִימִים וּמְכַפְּרִים "as the showers come whole and atone"): THE HOMOGRAPH READ — se'irim the small rains and se'irim the goats (the sin-offering goats of Numbers 28:15 — the translator's seat; the compile: the goat's atonement on the tape at the festivals' offerings, a homograph the parser must not follow: the rain is not the goat); the pun carried, the law not moved. DV32-01. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:25 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_09_ekev_2026-09-19.md). On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "MY DOCTRINE SHALL DROP AS THE RAIN" — THE SAGES SAY: MOSES SAID TO ISRAEL, DO YOU NOT KNOW HOW MUCH I SUFFERED FOR THE TORAH, HOW MUCH I LABORED AND WEARIED IN IT — "AND HE WAS THERE WITH THE LORD FORTY DAYS AND FORTY NIGHTS" (Exodus 34:28), "AND I SAT ON THE MOUNTAIN FORTY DAYS AND FORTY NIGHTS" (9:9); I WENT IN AMONG THE ANGELS, AMONG THE LIVING CREATURES, AMONG THE SERAPHIM ONE OF WHOM COULD BURN THE WHOLE WORLD (Isaiah 6:2); MY SOUL I GAVE FOR IT, MY BLOOD I GAVE FOR IT — AS I LEARNED IT IN PAIN SHALL YOU LEARN IT IN PAIN? OR AS YOU LEARN IT IN PAIN SHALL YOU TEACH IT IN PAIN? IT SAYS "MY SPEECH SHALL DISTIL (tizal) AS THE DEW" — SEE IT AS THOUGH IT WERE CHEAP (bezol): ONE OF THREE OR FOUR SEAHS FOR A SELA. (כַּמָּה צַעַר נִצְטַעַרְתִּי עַל הַתּוֹרָה "how much I suffered for the Torah"; נַפְשִׁי נָתַתִּי עָלֶיהָ דָּמִי נָתַתִּי עָלֶיהָ "my soul I gave for it, my blood I gave for it"; תְּהוּ רוֹאִים אוֹתָהּ כְּאִלּוּ הִיא בְּזוֹל "see it as though it were cheap"): MOSES' FORTY DAYS as the price and the dew as the cheapness — 9:9's forty days the seat that read this row at chapter 9 (the tape's own count, a RUN CITATION with Exodus 34:28); the pun tizal / bezol (distil / cheap) the translator's note; the compile: no law (DV32-01). REREAD WHOLE (chapter 9's reading — 9:9).
- Sifrei Devarim 306:26 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. "AS THE SMALL RAIN (se'irim) ON THE TENDER GRASS" — WHEN A MAN GOES TO LEARN TORAH AT FIRST IT FALLS ON HIM LIKE A SE'IR, AND SE'IR IS ONLY A DEMON — "AND THE SATYR SHALL CRY TO HIS FELLOW" (Isaiah 34:14), "AND SATYRS SHALL DANCE THERE" (Isaiah 13:21).
