- Sifrei Devarim 306:27 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_19_21_shoftim_ki_teitzei_2026-09-24.md). On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "MY DOCTRINE SHALL DROP AS THE RAIN" — R. BANAYAH USED TO SAY: IF YOU DO WORDS OF TORAH FOR THEIR OWN SAKE THEY ARE LIFE TO YOU — "FOR THEY ARE LIFE TO THOSE WHO FIND THEM" (Proverbs 4:22); IF NOT FOR THEIR OWN SAKE THEY KILL YOU — "YA'AROF AS THE RAIN", AND ARIFAH IS ONLY A WORD OF KILLING: "AND THEY SHALL BREAK THE NECK OF THE HEIFER THERE IN THE VALLEY" (21:4), "FOR MANY ARE THE SLAIN SHE HAS FELLED, AND MIGHTY ALL HER KILLED" (Proverbs 7:26). (אִם עָשִׂיתָ דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה לִשְׁמָם "if you do words of Torah for their own sake"; הֵם מְמִיתִים אוֹתְךָ "they kill you"): THE MOTIVE AS THE SWITCH — the same Torah life or death by its intent (21:4's neck-breaking the second time in the piska — the seat that read this row at chapters 19-21); the compile: no law — a condition on the learner's heart the parser cannot read (DV32-01). REREAD WHOLE (chapters 19-21's reading — 21:4).
- Sifrei Devarim 306:28 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "MY DOCTRINE SHALL DROP AS THE RAIN" — R. DOSTAI SON OF YEHUDAH SAYS: IF YOU GATHER WORDS OF TORAH AS WATER IS GATHERED INTO A CISTERN, IN THE END YOU MERIT TO SEE YOUR MISHNAH — "DRINK WATER FROM YOUR OWN CISTERN" (Proverbs 5:15); IF YOU GATHER THEM AS RAIN IS GATHERED INTO CISTERNS, DITCHES AND CAVES, IN THE END YOU FLOW AND WATER OTHERS — "AND RUNNING WATERS FROM YOUR OWN WELL", "LET YOUR SPRINGS SPREAD ABROAD" (Proverbs 5:16). (כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁכּוֹנְסִים הַמַּיִם בַּבּוֹר "as water is gathered into a cistern"; לַסּוֹף שֶׁאַתָּה מְנַזֵּל וּמַשְׁקֶה אֲחֵרִים "in the end you flow and water others"): the cistern and the springs — one's own learning and the teaching of others; the Writings' seats cited, never read; no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:29 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "MY DOCTRINE SHALL DROP AS THE RAIN" — R. MEIR USED TO SAY: ALWAYS GATHER WORDS OF TORAH AS GENERAL RULES, FOR IF YOU GATHER THEM AS PARTICULARS THEY WEARY YOU AND YOU KNOW NOT WHAT TO DO — A PARABLE OF A MAN GOING TO CAESAREA NEEDING A HUNDRED OR TWO HUNDRED ZUZ FOR EXPENSES: IF HE TAKES THEM IN SMALL COIN THEY WEARY HIM, BUT HE COMBINES THEM INTO SELAS AND BREAKS THEM WHEREVER HE WISHES; AND ONE GOING TO THE MARKET OF BEIT ILYIS NEEDING A HUNDRED MANEH OR TWO MYRIADS: IF HE COMBINES THEM INTO SELAS THEY WEARY HIM, BUT HE MAKES THEM GOLD DINARS AND BREAKS THEM WHEREVER HE WISHES. (שֶׁאִם אַתָּה כוֹנְסָם פְּרָטִים מְיַגְּעִים אוֹתְךָ "for if you gather them as particulars they weary you"; אֲבָל מְצָרְפָם וְעוֹשֶׂה אוֹתָם סְלָעִים "but he combines them and makes them selas"; וְעוֹשֶׂה אוֹתָם דִּינְרֵי זָהָב "and makes them gold dinars"): R. MEIR'S COINAGE — the general rule the gold dinar that carries a thousand particulars; THE ENGLISH ROW CORRUPTED IN THE EXPORT ("with all the creatures in the world" and a run of stray letters — a fragment from another row), the verdict from the Hebrew alone (sitting 18's lesson 4: a translator's misprint is judged by the Hebrew). The compile: the method's own picture again (306:20); no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:30 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. "AS THE SMALL RAIN ON THE TENDER GRASS" — WHEN A MAN GOES TO LEARN TORAH AT FIRST HE KNOWS NOT WHAT TO DO UNTIL HE HAS REPEATED TWO BOOKS OR TWO ORDERS, AND THEN IT DRAWS AFTER HIM LIKE SHOWERS — THEREFORE IT SAYS "AS THE SHOWERS ON THE HERB". (עַד שֶׁשּׁוֹנֶה שְׁנֵי סְפָרִים אוֹ שְׁנֵי סְדָרִים "until he has repeated two books or two orders"): the threshold of fluency — two books (of Scripture) or two orders (of the Mishnah): the Mishnah's orders named inside the spine (the testing shelf's own shape); no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:31 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "MY DOCTRINE SHALL DROP AS THE RAIN" — AS THIS RAIN COMES DOWN ON THE TREES AND GIVES EACH ITS TASTE ACCORDING TO WHAT IT IS — THE VINE, THE OLIVE, THE FIG — SO WORDS OF TORAH ARE ALL ONE, AND THERE IS IN THEM SCRIPTURE AND MISHNAH, TALMUD, LAWS AND LORE. (כָּךְ דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה כֻּלָּהּ אַחַת "so words of Torah are all one"; מִקְרָא וּמִשְׁנָה תַּלְמוּד הֲלָכוֹת וְהַגָּדוֹת "Scripture and Mishnah, Talmud, laws and lore"): THE ONE TORAH IN FIVE FORMS — the spine's own map of the shelves (Scripture the program, the Mishnah the answer sheet, the Talmud the compile rules, the laws and the lore — the compiler law's frame in the tradition's words; 48:10 outside the same triad); no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:32 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. "AS THE SMALL RAIN ON THE TENDER GRASS" — AS THESE SHOWERS COME DOWN ON THE HERBS AND RAISE THEM, AND SOME ARE RED, SOME GREEN, SOME BLACK, SOME WHITE, SO IN WORDS OF TORAH THERE ARE WISE MEN, FIT MEN, RIGHTEOUS MEN, PIOUS MEN. (בְּנֵי אָדָם חֲכָמִים בְּנֵי אָדָם כְּשֵׁרִים "wise men, fit men"; בְּנֵי אָדָם צַדִּיקִים בְּנֵי אָדָם חֲסִידִים "righteous men, pious men"): the four colors and the four ranks of students; no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:33 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: AS THIS RAIN YOU DO NOT SEE UNTIL IT COMES — "AND IT CAME TO PASS IN A LITTLE WHILE THAT THE HEAVENS GREW BLACK WITH CLOUDS" (1 Kings 18:45) — SO A DISCIPLE OF THE SAGES YOU KNOW NOT WHAT HE IS UNTIL HE REPEATS MIDRASH (SCRIPTURAL EXPOSITION), LAWS AND LORE, OR UNTIL HE IS APPOINTED ADMINISTRATOR OVER THE COMMUNITY. (כָּךְ תַּלְמִיד חֲכָמִים אֵין אַתָּה יוֹדֵעַ מַה "so a disciple of the sages — you know not what"; עַד שֶׁיִּתְמַנֶּה פַּרְנָס עַל הַצִּבּוּר "until he is appointed administrator over the community"): the scholar known by his output or his office; the Prophets' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:34 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "MY DOCTRINE SHALL DROP AS THE RAIN" — NOT AS THE RAIN THAT COMES FROM THE SOUTH, ALL BLIGHT AND MILDEW AND CURSE, BUT AS THE RAIN THAT COMES FROM THE WEST, ALL BLESSING. R. SIMAI USED TO SAY: WHENCE THAT AS MOSES CALLED HEAVEN AND EARTH TO WITNESS AGAINST ISRAEL SO HE CALLED THE FOUR WINDS OF HEAVEN? "YA'AROF AS THE RAIN MY DOCTRINE" — THE WEST WIND, THE BACK (oref) OF THE WORLD, ALL BLESSING; "MY SPEECH SHALL DISTIL AS THE DEW" — THE NORTH WIND THAT MAKES THE SKY CLEAN AS GOLD; "AS THE SMALL RAIN (se'irim) ON THE TENDER GRASS" — THE EAST WIND THAT BLACKENS THE SKY LIKE GOATS; "AND AS THE SHOWERS (revivim) ON THE HERB" — THE SOUTH WIND THAT WEAVES THE SKY LIKE A CLOUD. (לֹא כְּמָטָר זֶה שֶׁבָּא מִן הַדָּרוֹם "not as the rain that comes from the south"; כָּךְ הֵעִיד בָּהֶם אַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת הַשָּׁמַיִם "so he called the four winds of heaven to witness against them"; זוֹ רוּחַ מַעֲרָבִית שֶׁהִיא עׇרְפּוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם "this is the west wind, the back of the world"): THE FOUR RAINS AS THE FOUR WINDS — a third reading of ya'arof (the west is the world's nape); the four winds joined to heaven and earth as witnesses (six witnesses in R. Simai's count); the compile: the winds' seasons the land's clock (the calendar's parameters), no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:35 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. ANOTHER WORD: "MY DOCTRINE SHALL DROP AS THE RAIN" — AND SO R. SIMAI USED TO SAY: THESE FOUR WINDS WERE SAID ONLY AGAINST THE FOUR WINDS OF HEAVEN — THE NORTH FAIR IN THE SUNNY DAYS AND HARSH IN THE RAINY, THE SOUTH HARSH IN THE SUNNY AND FAIR IN THE RAINY, THE EAST ALWAYS HARSH, THE WEST ALWAYS FAIR; THE NORTH FAIR FOR THE WHEAT AT A THIRD AND HARSH FOR THE OLIVES AT BUDDING, THE SOUTH THE REVERSE. AND SO R. SIMAI USED TO SAY: ALL CREATURES CREATED FROM THE HEAVENS — THEIR SOUL AND BODY ARE FROM THE HEAVENS; ALL CREATED FROM THE EARTH — FROM THE EARTH; EXCEPT MAN, WHOSE SOUL IS FROM THE HEAVENS AND BODY FROM THE EARTH: IF HE DOES TORAH AND HIS FATHER'S WILL IN HEAVEN HE IS AS THE CREATURES ABOVE — "I SAID: YOU ARE GODS, SONS OF THE MOST HIGH ALL OF YOU" (Psalm 82:6); IF NOT, AS THE CREATURES BELOW — "SURELY AS ADAM YOU SHALL DIE" (Psalm 82:7). AND SO R. SIMAI USED TO SAY: THERE IS NO SECTION WITHOUT THE RESURRECTION OF THE DEAD, ONLY WE LACK THE STRENGTH TO EXPOUND — "HE CALLS TO THE HEAVENS ABOVE AND TO THE EARTH TO JUDGE HIS PEOPLE" (Psalm 50:4): TO THE HEAVENS ABOVE — THE SOUL; TO THE EARTH — THE BODY, TO BE JUDGED WITH IT; AND WHENCE THAT IT SPEAKS OF THE RESURRECTION — "COME FROM THE FOUR WINDS, O BREATH, AND BREATHE ON THESE SLAIN" (Ezekiel 37:9). (מִזְרָחִית לְעוֹלָם קָשָׁה מַעֲרָבִית לְעוֹלָם יָפָה "the east always harsh, the west always fair"; חוּץ מֵאָדָם זֶה שֶׁנַּפְשׁוֹ מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם "except man, whose soul is from the heavens"; אֵין לְךָ פָּרָשָׁה שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ תְּחִיַּת מֵתִים "there is no section without the resurrection of the dead"): THE WINDS' AGRONOMY (the Mekhilta on Exodus 14 the parallel, cited), MAN OF HEAVEN AND EARTH (the two witnesses his two parts — the song's summons a summons of the hearer's own soul and body), and THE RESURRECTION IN EVERY SECTION (Psalm 50:4 revoweled to 'to be judged with it' — the translator's note): the compile: no law — the resurrection a declared future outside the tape's clock (the Writings' and the Prophets' seats cited, never read). DV32-01. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:36 — MATERIAL. On 32:1. "FOR I WILL PROCLAIM THE NAME OF THE LORD" (32:3) — WE FIND THAT MOSES DID NOT MENTION THE NAME OF THE HOLY ONE UNTIL AFTER TWENTY-ONE WORDS; FROM WHOM DID HE LEARN? FROM THE MINISTERING ANGELS, WHO MENTION THE NAME ONLY AFTER THREE SANCTIFICATIONS — "AND ONE CALLED TO ANOTHER AND SAID: HOLY, HOLY, HOLY IS THE LORD OF HOSTS" (Isaiah 6:3); MOSES SAID: IT IS ENOUGH THAT I BE AT SEVEN TIMES THE MINISTERING ANGELS. AND IS IT NOT AN A-FORTIORI: IF MOSES, THE WISEST OF THE WISE, THE GREATEST OF THE GREAT, THE FATHER OF THE PROPHETS, DID NOT MENTION THE NAME OF THE PLACE UNTIL AFTER TWENTY-ONE WORDS, HE WHO MENTIONS THE NAME OF THE PLACE IN VAIN, HOW MUCH MORE. R. SHIMON BEN YOCHAI SAYS: WHENCE THAT A MAN SHALL NOT SAY "TO THE LORD A BURNT OFFERING", "TO THE LORD A MEAL OFFERING", "TO THE LORD PEACE OFFERINGS", BUT "A BURNT OFFERING TO THE LORD", "A MEAL OFFERING TO THE LORD", "PEACE OFFERINGS TO THE LORD"? IT SAYS "AN OFFERING TO THE LORD" (Leviticus 1:2); AND IS IT NOT AN A-FORTIORI: IF OF THESE, CONSECRATED TO HEAVEN, THE HOLY ONE SAID MY NAME SHALL NOT REST ON THEM UNTIL THEY ARE CONSECRATED, HE WHO MENTIONS THE NAME OF THE HOLY ONE IN VAIN AND IN A PLACE OF CONTEMPT, HOW MUCH MORE. (שֶׁלֹּא הִזְכִּיר מֹשֶׁה שְׁמוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ "that Moses did not mention the name of the Holy One, blessed"; אֶלָּא לְאַחַר עֶשְׂרִים וְאֶחָד דָּבָר "until after twenty-one words"; הַמַּזְכִּיר שְׁמוֹ שֶׁל מָקוֹם בְּחִנָּם "he who mentions the name of the Place in vain"): THE NAME VERSE OPENED — TWENTY-ONE WORDS before the Name in 32:1-3 (the count the ink can compute at the compile: the Name's first seat at 32:3's third token; the measure counts the Name seven times bare in the chapter), seven times the angels' three (Isaiah 6:3); and THE OFFERING FORMULA'S WORD ORDER from Leviticus 1:2 (the Sifra on Leviticus 1:2 the parallel, cited — the Sifra's rule compiled at the offerings' span on the tape; the Mishnah Nedarim 1:2's vow formulas the testing shelf, cited): the compile: the Name uttered after praise a rule of speech, no verdict; the formula's order a parameter of the offering's declaration (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 306:37 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_06_vaetchanan_2026-09-17.md). On 32:1. "FOR I WILL PROCLAIM THE NAME OF THE LORD" (32:3) — R. YOSE SAYS: WHENCE THAT WHEN THOSE STANDING IN THE SYNAGOGUE SAY "BLESS THE LORD WHO IS BLESSED" THE OTHERS ANSWER "BLESSED IS THE LORD WHO IS BLESSED FOR EVER AND EVER"? "FOR I WILL PROCLAIM THE NAME OF THE LORD — ASCRIBE GREATNESS TO OUR GOD". R. NEHORAI SAID TO HIM: BY HEAVEN! IT IS THE WAY OF THE WORLD — THE FOOT SOLDIERS PROVOKE THE BATTLE AND THE MIGHTY WIN. AND WHENCE THAT THE BLESSING AFTER THE MEAL IS CONVENED ONLY WITH THREE? "FOR I WILL PROCLAIM … ASCRIBE (plural) GREATNESS TO OUR GOD"; AND WHENCE THAT ONE ANSWERS AMEN AFTER THE ONE WHO BLESSES? "ASCRIBE GREATNESS TO OUR GOD"; AND WHENCE THAT ONE SAYS "BLESSED BE THE NAME OF HIS GLORIOUS KINGDOM FOR EVER AND EVER"? "FOR I WILL PROCLAIM THE NAME OF THE LORD"; AND WHENCE THAT TO THOSE WHO SAY "MAY HIS GREAT NAME BE BLESSED" ONE ANSWERS "FOR EVER AND TO ALL ETERNITY"? "ASCRIBE GREATNESS TO OUR GOD". AND WHENCE THAT OUR FATHERS WENT DOWN TO EGYPT ONLY THAT THE HOLY ONE MIGHT DO THEM MIRACLES AND MIGHTY DEEDS TO SANCTIFY HIS GREAT NAME IN THE WORLD (Exodus 2:23-24)? AND THE TEN PLAGUES ON PHARAOH AND EGYPT ONLY TO SANCTIFY HIS NAME — "WHO IS THE LORD THAT I SHOULD HEED HIM" (Exodus 5:2) AT THE START, "THE LORD IS RIGHTEOUS AND I AND MY PEOPLE ARE WICKED" (Exodus 9:27) AT THE END; AND THE MIRACLES AT THE SEA, THE JORDAN AND THE VALLEYS OF ARNON (Joshua 5:1; Rahab, Joshua 2:10); AND DANIEL INTO THE LIONS' DEN (Daniel 6:27) AND CHANANIAH, MISHAEL AND AZARIAH INTO THE FURNACE (Daniel 3:32-33) — ALL TO SANCTIFY HIS GREAT NAME; AND WHENCE THAT THE MINISTERING ANGELS DO NOT MENTION THE NAME ABOVE UNTIL ISRAEL MENTION IT BELOW? "HEAR, O ISRAEL, THE LORD OUR GOD, THE LORD IS ONE" (6:4), AND "WHEN THE MORNING STARS SANG TOGETHER" — ISRAEL, LIKENED TO THE STARS (Genesis 22:17) — AND THEN "ALL THE SONS OF GOD SHOUTED" (Job 38:7) — THE MINISTERING ANGELS (Job 1:6). (וּמִנַּיִן שֶׁאֵין מְזַמְּנִים אֶלָּא בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה "and whence that the meal blessing is convened only with three"; וּמִנַּיִן שֶׁעוֹנִים אָמֵן אַחַר הַמְּבָרֵךְ "and whence that one answers Amen after the one who blesses"; שֶׁאֵין מַלְאֲכֵי הַשָּׁרֵת מַזְכִּירִים שְׁמוֹ "that the ministering angels do not mention His name"): THE SYNAGOGUE'S RESPONSES FROM ONE VERSE — Barekhu and its answer, THE ZIMMUN OF THREE (Mishnah Berakhot 7:1 — the translator's seat), Amen after a blessing (Berakhot 8:8), "Blessed be the name of His glorious kingdom" inside the Shema (Berakhot 2:2 — the Shema's paragraphs; 6:4 the seat that read this row at chapter 6), the Kaddish's answer — the answer sheet's rows the compile takes as cases (the testing shelf, cited); THE NAME SANCTIFIED BY HISTORY — Egypt, the plagues, the sea, the Jordan, the Arnon (the tape's own entries, RUN CITATIONS), Daniel and the three (the Writings' seats cited, never read); THE ANGELS WAIT FOR ISRAEL (6:4 the trigger). The compile at 20b: the response formulas parameters of the liturgy's law (a status on the assembly of three), the Name's sanctification a status the tape's miracles write (DV32-01). REREAD WHOLE (chapter 6's reading — 6:4).
- Sifrei Devarim 307:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in gen_08_toledot_garden_first_rule_2026-08-24.md). On 32:4. "THE ROCK" (32:4) — THE DESIGNER (tzayyar), WHO DESIGNED THE WORLD FIRST AND FORMED (yatzar) MAN IN IT: "AND THE LORD GOD FORMED MAN" (Genesis 2:7). The head row of piska 307 (הַצַּיָּר שֶׁהוּא צָר אֶת הָעוֹלָם תְּחִלָּה "the designer who designed the world first"): THE ROCK BY ITS ROOT — tzur read as tzayyar (the designer) and yatzar (formed), Genesis 2:7 the seat that read this row at the Genesis garden ledger; the compile: the Rock a title of the Maker (Onkelos: the Mighty One), no law (DV32-01). REREAD WHOLE (the Genesis ledger gen_08_toledot_garden_first_rule's reading — Genesis 2:7).
- Sifrei Devarim 307:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. "HIS WORK IS PERFECT" — HIS WORK IS COMPLETE WITH ALL WHO COME INTO THE WORLD; NONE MAY QUESTION HIS DEEDS FOR THE SLIGHTEST CAUSE; NONE LOOKS AND SAYS: HAD I THREE EYES, THREE HANDS, THREE FEET, WALKED ON MY HEAD, HAD MY FACE TURNED BACKWARD — HOW FINE IT WOULD BE; IT SAYS "FOR ALL HIS WAYS ARE JUSTICE" — HE SITS WITH EACH ONE IN JUDGMENT AND GIVES HIM WHAT IS FIT FOR HIM. (פְּעֻלָּתוֹ שְׁלֵמָה עִם כָּל בָּאֵי הָעוֹלָם "His work is complete with all who come into the world"; יוֹשֵׁב עִם כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד בַּדִּין "He sits with each and every one in judgment"): the body's design as the first justice — each creature given what fits it; no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 307:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. "A GOD OF FAITHFULNESS" — WHO BELIEVED IN THE WORLD AND CREATED IT. (שֶׁהֶאֱמִין בָּעוֹלָם וּבְרָאוֹ "who believed in the world and created it"): emunah (faithfulness) read by its root as he'emin (believed, affirmed) — the creation an act of trust; no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 307:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. "AND WITHOUT INIQUITY" — FOR MEN WERE NOT CREATED TO BE WICKED BUT RIGHTEOUS: "GOD MADE MAN UPRIGHT, BUT THEY SOUGHT OUT MANY DEVICES" (Ecclesiastes 7:29). (נִבְרְאוּ בְּנֵי אָדָם לִהְיוֹת רְשָׁעִים אֶלָּא צַדִּיקִים "men were created not to be wicked but righteous"): the default of the creature upright — the Writings' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 307:5 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. "JUST AND RIGHT IS HE" — HE CONDUCTS HIMSELF WITH UPRIGHTNESS TOWARD ALL WHO COME INTO THE WORLD. (מִתְנַהֵג בְּיַשְׁרוּת עִם כָּל בָּאֵי הָעוֹלָם "He conducts Himself in uprightness with all who come into the world"): the verse's five titles read one by one (307:1-5 the first cycle); no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 307:6 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. ANOTHER WORD: "THE ROCK" — THE MIGHTY ONE (takif). "HIS WORK IS PERFECT" — COMPLETE WITH ALL WHO COME INTO THE WORLD; NONE LOOKS AND SAYS: WHAT DID THE GENERATION OF THE FLOOD SEE TO BE SWEPT AWAY BY WATER, THE MEN OF THE TOWER TO BE SCATTERED FROM ONE END OF THE WORLD TO THE OTHER, THE MEN OF SODOM TO BE SWEPT AWAY BY FIRE AND BRIMSTONE, WHAT DID AARON SEE TO TAKE THE PRIESTHOOD, DAVID THE KINGSHIP, KORAH AND HIS BAND TO BE SWALLOWED BY THE EARTH? IT SAYS "FOR ALL HIS WAYS ARE JUSTICE" — HE SITS WITH EACH IN JUDGMENT AND GIVES HIM WHAT IS FIT. "A GOD OF FAITHFULNESS" — THE KEEPER OF THE DEPOSIT. (הַצּוּר הַתַּקִּיף "the Rock — the Mighty One"; מָה רָאוּ אַנְשֵׁי דוֹר הַמַּבּוּל לְהִשָּׁטֵף בַּמַּיִם "what did the men of the generation of the Flood see to be swept away by water"; אֵל אֱמוּנָה בַּעַל הַפִּקָּדוֹן "a God of faithfulness — the keeper of the deposit"): THE MIGHTY ONE — the spine's own word for the Rock is Onkelos's (takifa at 32:4 — the measure); the six judgments of the tape's own entries (the Flood, Babel, Sodom, Aaron's priesthood, David ahead cited, Korah's swallowing — RUN CITATIONS but David) each "what is fit"; the deposit the faithful God keeps; no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 307:7 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. "AND WITHOUT INIQUITY" — HE COLLECTS HIS OWN LAST; THE HOLY ONE'S MEASURE IS NOT FLESH AND BLOOD'S: A MAN DEPOSITS WITH HIS FELLOW A PURSE OF TWO HUNDRED AND OWES HIM A MANEH — WHEN HE COMES FOR HIS OWN THE FELLOW SAYS: TAKE OUT THE MANEH YOU OWE ME AND HERE IS THE REST; AND A LABORER WHO WORKED FOR A HOUSEHOLDER AND OWES HIM A DINAR — WHEN HE COMES FOR HIS WAGE: TAKE OUT THE DINAR AND HERE IS THE REST; BUT HE WHO SPOKE AND THE WORLD WAS IS NOT SO — "A GOD OF FAITHFULNESS", THE KEEPER OF THE DEPOSIT, "AND WITHOUT INIQUITY" — HE COLLECTS HIS OWN LAST. (גּוֹבֶה שֶׁלּוֹ בָּאַחֲרוֹנָה "He collects His own last"; שֶׁלֹּא כְּמִדַּת בָּשָׂר וָדָם מִדַּת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ "not as the measure of flesh and blood is the measure of the Holy One, blessed"): THE DEPOSIT AND THE WAGE — two cases of the compiled bailment and hire laws (Exodus 22's keeper on the tape; 24:15's wage) turned into the figure: God pays the deposit whole before He collects the debt; the compile: no law, the answer sheet's set-off (Mishnah Shevuot 7:5 the testing shelf, cited) the human measure the row contrasts (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 307:8 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. "JUST AND RIGHT IS HE" — AS IT SAYS "FOR THE LORD IS RIGHTEOUS, HE LOVES RIGHTEOUS DEEDS" (Psalm 11:7). (צַדִּיק וְיָשָׁר הוּא "just and right is He"): the second cycle closed (307:6-8); the Writings' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 307:9 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. ANOTHER WORD: "THE ROCK" — THE MIGHTY ONE. (הַצּוּר הַתַּקִּיף "the Rock — the Mighty One"): the third cycle opens with the same word; THE ENGLISH MISPRINTS THE VERSE (it cites 32:41 where the head is 32:4 — the Hebrew's marker at 307:1 governs the piska); no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 307:10 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. "HIS WORK IS PERFECT" — THE WORK OF ALL WHO COME INTO THE WORLD IS COMPLETE BEFORE HIM: THE REWARD OF THE RIGHTEOUS AND THE PUNISHMENT OF THE WICKED — THESE TOOK NOTHING OF THEIRS IN THIS WORLD AND THOSE TOOK NOTHING OF THEIRS IN THIS WORLD; THE RIGHTEOUS — "HOW GREAT IS YOUR GOODNESS WHICH YOU HAVE LAID UP FOR THOSE WHO FEAR YOU" (Psalm 31:20); THE WICKED — "IS IT NOT LAID UP IN STORE WITH ME, SEALED IN MY TREASURIES" (32:34); WHEN DO BOTH TAKE? "FOR ALL HIS WAYS ARE JUSTICE" — TOMORROW, WHEN HE SITS ON THE THRONE OF JUDGMENT, HE SITS WITH EACH IN JUDGMENT AND GIVES HIM WHAT IS FIT. (מַתַּן שְׂכָרָם שֶׁל צַדִּיקִים וּמַחַת פֻּרְעָנוּתָם שֶׁל "the giving of the righteous's reward and the blow of the wicked's punishment"; לְמָחָר כְּשֶׁהוּא יוֹשֵׁב עַל כִּסֵּא דִין "tomorrow, when He sits on the throne of judgment"): THE SONG CITES ITSELF — 32:34's treasuries (Onkelos: the day of judgment) the proof that the wicked's account is deferred; the compile: the ledger's balance carried to a day outside the tape's clock, no verdict here (DV32-01, DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 307:11 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. "A GOD OF FAITHFULNESS" — AS HE PAYS THE WHOLLY RIGHTEOUS THE REWARD OF A COMMANDMENT DONE IN THIS WORLD IN THE WORLD TO COME, SO HE PAYS THE WHOLLY WICKED THE REWARD OF A LIGHT COMMANDMENT DONE IN THIS WORLD IN THIS WORLD; AND AS HE EXACTS FROM THE WHOLLY WICKED FOR A TRANSGRESSION DONE IN THIS WORLD IN THE WORLD TO COME, SO HE EXACTS FROM THE WHOLLY RIGHTEOUS FOR A LIGHT TRANSGRESSION DONE IN THIS WORLD IN THIS WORLD. (כְּשֵׁם שֶׁמְּשַׁלֵּם לְצַדִּיק גָּמוּר שְׂכַר מִצְוָה "as He pays the wholly righteous the reward of a commandment"; כָּךְ נִפְרָע מִצַּדִּיק גָּמוּר עַל עֲבֵרָה קַלָּה "so He exacts from the wholly righteous for a light transgression"): THE TWO ACCOUNTS — the wicked paid here for their little good, the righteous charged here for their little wrong (the Babylonian Talmud Taanit 11a the testing shelf, cited); the compile: no law — the ledger's two columns closed in two worlds (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 307:12 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. "AND WITHOUT INIQUITY" — WHEN A MAN DEPARTS FROM THE WORLD ALL HIS DEEDS COME AND ARE DETAILED BEFORE HIM: THUS AND THUS YOU DID ON SUCH A DAY, THUS AND THUS ON SUCH A DAY — DO YOU BELIEVE THESE THINGS? HE SAYS: YES. THEY SAY: SIGN — "HE SEALS UP THE HAND OF EVERY MAN, THAT ALL MEN MAY KNOW HIS WORK" (Job 37:7). (בָּאִים כָּל מַעֲשָׂיו וְנִפְרָטִים לְפָנָיו "all his deeds come and are detailed before him"; אוֹמְרִים לוֹ חֲתוֹם "they say to him: sign"): THE RECORD READ BACK BY DAY AND SIGNED — the tradition's picture of the ledger (the tape's own form: every deed at its day, the readback a signature); no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 307:13 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. "JUST AND RIGHT IS HE" — AND HE JUSTIFIES THE JUDGMENT AND SAYS: I HAVE BEEN WELL JUDGED; "THAT YOU MAY BE JUSTIFIED WHEN YOU SPEAK" (Psalm 51:6). (וְהוּא מַצְדִּיק אֶת הַדִּין וְאוֹמֵר יָפֶה נִדַּנְתִּי "and he justifies the judgment and says: I have been well judged"): the third cycle closed — the judged man's own acknowledgment (tzidduk ha-din, the justification of the judgment); no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 307:14 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. ANOTHER WORD: "THE ROCK, HIS WORK IS PERFECT" — WHEN THEY SEIZED R. CHANINA BEN TERADYON THE DECREE WAS TO BURN HIM WITH HIS SCROLL; HE READ THIS VERSE: "THE ROCK, HIS WORK IS PERFECT"; THEY SAID TO HIS WIFE: YOUR HUSBAND TO BE BURNED AND YOU TO BE KILLED — SHE READ "A GOD OF FAITHFULNESS AND WITHOUT INIQUITY"; TO HIS DAUGHTER: YOUR FATHER TO BE BURNED, YOUR MOTHER KILLED, AND YOU TO DO LABOR — SHE READ "GREAT IN COUNSEL AND MIGHTY IN DEED, WHOSE EYES ARE OPEN" (Jeremiah 32:19). RABBI SAID: HOW GREAT ARE THESE RIGHTEOUS, WHO IN THE HOUR OF THEIR TROUBLE SUMMONED THREE VERSES OF JUSTIFYING THE JUDGMENT, AS NOWHERE ELSE IN SCRIPTURE — ALL THREE DIRECTED THEIR HEART AND JUSTIFIED THE JUDGMENT UPON THEMSELVES. A PHILOSOPHER STOOD BEFORE HIS GOVERNOR: MY LORD, BE NOT PROUD THAT YOU BURNED THE TORAH — FROM THE PLACE IT WENT OUT IT HAS RETURNED, TO ITS FATHER'S HOUSE; HE SAID: TOMORROW YOUR SENTENCE IS AS THEIRS; HE SAID: YOU HAVE BROUGHT ME GOOD NEWS — TOMORROW MY PORTION IS WITH THEM IN THE WORLD TO COME. (כְּשֶׁתָּפְסוּ אֶת רַבִּי חֲנִינָה בֶּן תְּרַדְיוֹן "when they seized R. Chanina ben Teradyon"; כַּמָּה גְּדוֹלִים צַדִּיקִים אֵלּוּ "how great are these righteous"; שֶׁמִּמָּקוֹם שֶׁיָּצָאת חָזְרָה לָהּ לְבֵית אָבִיהָ "from the place it went out it has returned to its Father's house"): THE THREE VERSES OF THE JUSTIFICATION — 32:4's two halves and Jeremiah 32:19, the martyr's liturgy (the Babylonian Talmud Avodah Zarah 18a the parallel, cited; the four deaths' "killed" Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1 — the translator's note; Mishnah Avodah Zarah 3:4's philosopher); the compile: no law — the verse's liturgical office a status (DV32-01); the Prophets' seat cited, never read. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 307:15 — MATERIAL. On 32:4. ANOTHER WORD: "THE ROCK, HIS WORK IS PERFECT" — WHEN MOSES CAME DOWN FROM MOUNT SINAI ISRAEL GATHERED TO HIM: OUR MASTER MOSES, TELL US WHAT THE MEASURE OF JUDGMENT IS ABOVE; HE SAID: I DO NOT TELL YOU IT ACQUITS THE INNOCENT AND CONVICTS THE GUILTY — EVEN TO EXCHANGE THE MATTER — "A GOD OF FAITHFULNESS AND WITHOUT INIQUITY". (כְּשֶׁיָּרַד מֹשֶׁה מֵהַר סִינַי "when Moses came down from Mount Sinai"; מַה הִיא מִדַּת הַדִּין לְמַעְלָה "what is the measure of judgment above"; אֲפִלּוּ לְהַחֲלִיף בַּדָּבָר "even to exchange the matter"): the last row of the piska — the heavenly court's measure beyond acquittal and conviction (the text terse, the translator supplying "it also works in reverse"); the compile: the court's two verdicts (the compiled court law) the human measure; no law (DV32-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 308:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:5. "IS CORRUPTION HIS? NO; HIS CHILDREN'S IS THE BLEMISH" (32:5) — THOUGH THEY ARE FULL OF BLEMISHES THEY ARE CALLED CHILDREN — THE WORDS OF R. MEIR, AS IT SAYS "HIS CHILDREN — THEIR BLEMISH"; R. YEHUDAH SAYS: THEY HAVE NO BLEMISH, AS IT SAYS "NOT HIS CHILDREN — THEIR BLEMISH"; AND SO "SEED OF EVILDOERS, CHILDREN THAT DEAL CORRUPTLY" (Isaiah 1:4) — IF WHEN THEY DEAL CORRUPTLY THEY ARE CALLED CHILDREN, HAD THEY NOT, HOW MUCH MORE; "THEY ARE WISE TO DO EVIL" (Jeremiah 4:22) — IF WHEN THEY DO EVIL THEY ARE CALLED WISE, HAD THEY DONE GOOD, HOW MUCH MORE; "THEY ARE FOOLISH CHILDREN" — IF WHEN FOOLISH THEY ARE CALLED CHILDREN, HAD THEY BEEN UNDERSTANDING, HOW MUCH MORE; "MY PEOPLE SIT BEFORE YOU AND HEAR YOUR WORDS" (Ezekiel 33:31) — COULD THEY HEAR AND DO? "BUT THEY DO NOT DO THEM" — IF WHEN THEY HEAR AND DO NOT DO THEY ARE CALLED MY PEOPLE, HAD THEY HEARD AND DONE, HOW MUCH MORE. The head row of piska 308 (אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵם מְלֵאִים מוּמִים קְרוּיִים "though they are full of blemishes they are called"; אֵין בָּהֶם מוּמִים "they have no blemish"; אִם כְּשֶׁמַּשְׁחִיתִים קְרוּיִים בָּנִים "if when they deal corruptly they are called children"): THE BROKEN SYNTAX READ TWO WAYS — R. Meir attaches "not" to the corruption (blemished sons still sons), R. Yehudah to the blemish (no blemish in His sons): the verse the parser cannot parse read by two masters (the ink counts it among the eleven verses with no kin); then FOUR A-FORTIORI ARGUMENTS (I1) from the Prophets' seats (cited, never read) — the name "children" held through the fault; the compile: Israel's sonship a status that survives the verdict (DV32-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 308:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:5. IN THE NAME OF ABBA HADORES THEY SAID: ISRAEL CORRUPTED THEMSELVES IN EVERY PROHIBITION OF THE TORAH — WHY SO MUCH? SO AS NOT TO GIVE THE WICKED AN OPENING TO SAY: WHENEVER WE SIN BEFORE HIM WE GRIEVE BEFORE HIM. A PARABLE: ONE GOING OUT TO BE CRUCIFIED, HIS FATHER WEEPING, HIS MOTHER BEATING HERSELF — THIS ONE SAYS WOE IS ME AND THAT ONE WOE IS ME, BUT THE WOE IS ONLY ON HIM WHO GOES OUT TO BE CRUCIFIED: "WOE TO THEIR SOUL, FOR THEY HAVE WROUGHT EVIL TO THEMSELVES" (Isaiah 3:9). (שִׁחֲתוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּכָל לָאוִים שֶׁבַּתּוֹרָה "Israel corrupted themselves in every prohibition of the Torah"; לְאֶחָד שֶׁיָּצָא לִצָּלֵב וְאָבִיו בּוֹכֶה עָלָיו "one who went out to be crucified, his father weeping over him"): the corruption named as the breach of every negative commandment (the compiled prohibitions on the tape) and the grief the sinner's own; the Prophets' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 308:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:5. "A PERVERSE AND CROOKED GENERATION" — MOSES SAID TO ISRAEL: YOU ARE CROOKED, YOU ARE TWISTED, AND YOU GO ONLY TO THE FIRE. A PARABLE: ONE WITH A BENT STAFF GIVES IT TO A CRAFTSMAN TO MEND — HE MENDS IT BY FIRE, IF NOT HE STRAIGHTENS IT IN THE PRESS, IF NOT HE HEWS IT WITH THE ADZE AND THROWS IT TO THE FIRE: "I WILL GIVE YOU INTO THE HAND OF BRUTISH MEN, SKILLED TO DESTROY" (Ezekiel 21:36). (עַקְמָנִים אַתֶּם פְּתַלְתָּנִים אַתֶּם "crooked are you, twisted are you"; מְתַקְּנוֹ בָּאוּר "he mends it by fire"): the three tools in order — fire, the press, the adze — the escalation of chastisement (the curse's sequence on the tape); the Prophets' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 308:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:5. ANOTHER WORD: MOSES SAID TO ISRAEL — BY THE MEASURE YOU MEASURED I HAVE MEASURED TO YOU: "WITH THE PURE YOU SHOW YOURSELF PURE AND WITH THE CROOKED YOU SHOW YOURSELF WRESTLING" (2 Samuel 22:27) — "A PERVERSE AND CROOKED GENERATION". (בְּמִדָּה שֶׁמְּדַדְתֶּם בָּהּ מָדַדְתִּי לָכֶם "by the measure you measured I have measured to you"): MEASURE FOR MEASURE — the song's crookedness God's answering crookedness (Mishnah Sotah 1:7 the testing shelf's rule, cited); the Prophets' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 309:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:6. "DO YOU THUS REQUITE THE LORD?" (32:6) — A PARABLE: ONE STOOD RAILING AT A COUNCILMAN IN THE MARKET; THE HEARERS SAID: FOOL OF THE WORLD, YOU STAND RAILING AT A COUNCILMAN? IF HE WISHED TO BEAT YOU OR TEAR YOUR GARMENT OR IMPRISON YOU, COULD YOU PREVAIL? IF A CENTURION GREATER THAN HE, HOW MUCH MORE; IF THE PREFECT GREATER THAN BOTH, HOW MUCH MORE. The head row of piska 309 (לְאֶחָד שֶׁהָיָה עוֹמֵד וְצוֹהֵב כְּנֶגֶד בּוּלְיוּטוֹס בַּשּׁוּק "one who stood railing at a councilman in the market"; שֶׁמָּא אַתְּ יָכוֹל לוֹ "could you prevail over him"): the a-fortiori of ranks (I1) — councilman, centurion, prefect — the Roman ladder the figure of the folly; no law (DV32-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 309:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:6. ANOTHER WORD: "DO YOU THUS REQUITE THE LORD?" — ONE STOOD RAILING AT HIS FATHER IN THE MARKET; THE HEARERS SAID: FOOL OF THE WORLD, AT WHOM DO YOU RAIL — YOUR FATHER! HEAR HOW MUCH HE LABORED FOR YOU AND WEARIED FOR YOU; IF YOU DID NOT HONOR HIM FOR THE PAST, HONOR HIM NOW, LEST HE WRITE ALL HIS GOODS TO OTHERS. SO MOSES TO ISRAEL: IF YOU DO NOT REMEMBER THE MIRACLES AND MIGHTY DEEDS THE HOLY ONE DID FOR YOU IN EGYPT, REMEMBER HOW MUCH GOOD HE WILL GIVE YOU IN THE WORLD TO COME. (שְׁמַע כַּמָּה עָמָל עָמַל בְּךָ "hear how much he labored for you"; הִזָּכְרוּ כַּמָּה טוֹבוֹת עָתִיד לִתֵּן לָכֶם לָעוֹלָם "remember how much good He will give you in the world"): the father who may disinherit — honor for the past or for the future; the compile: the father's honor (5:16's command on the tape) the figure, no law (DV32-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 309:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:6. "O FOOLISH PEOPLE AND UNWISE" — FOOLISH FOR THE PAST, UNWISE FOR THE FUTURE; LIKEWISE "ISRAEL DOES NOT KNOW" (Isaiah 1:3) FOR THE PAST, "MY PEOPLE DOES NOT CONSIDER" FOR THE FUTURE; WHAT MADE ISRAEL FOOLISH AND STUPID? THAT THEY DID NOT GROW WISE IN WORDS OF TORAH: "IS NOT THEIR CORD PLUCKED UP? THEY DIE, AND WITHOUT WISDOM" (Job 4:21). (נָבָל לְשֶׁעָבַר וְלֹא חָכָם לֶעָתִיד לָבוֹא "foolish for the past, unwise for the future"; שֶׁלֹּא הֻחְכְּמוּ בְּדִבְרֵי תוֹרָה "that they did not grow wise in words of Torah"): the two adjectives the two tenses; the Prophets' and the Writings' seats cited, never read; no law (DV32-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 309:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:6. "IS HE NOT YOUR FATHER WHO ACQUIRED YOU?" — SHIMON BEN CHALAFTA SAYS: IF THE WEAK WERE ABOVE AND THE STRONG BELOW, WHO WINS? COULD YOU PREVAIL? HOW MUCH MORE WHEN THE STRONG IS ABOVE AND THE WEAK BELOW; "BE NOT RASH WITH YOUR MOUTH … FOR GOD IS IN HEAVEN AND YOU ON EARTH" (Ecclesiastes 5:1). (שֶׁאִם הָיָה חַלָּשׁ מִלְּמַעְלָה וְגִבּוֹר מִלְּמַטָּה "if the weak were above and the strong below"; מִי נוֹצֵחַ "who wins"): the wrestlers' a-fortiori (I1); the Writings' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 309:5 — MATERIAL. On 32:6. "IS HE NOT YOUR FATHER WHO ACQUIRED YOU?" — MOSES SAID TO ISRAEL: YOU ARE BELOVED TO HIM, YOU ARE HIS ACQUISITION, YOU ARE NOT HIS INHERITANCE. A PARABLE: ONE WHOSE FATHER LEFT HIM TEN FIELDS, AND HE BOUGHT ONE FIELD OF HIS OWN AND LOVED IT MORE THAN ALL HIS FATHER LEFT HIM; SO ONE LEFT TEN PALACES WHO BOUGHT ONE OF HIS OWN. SO MOSES TO ISRAEL: YOU ARE BELOVED, HIS ACQUISITION, NOT HIS INHERITANCE. (חֲבִיבִים אַתֶּם לוֹ קִנְיָן אַתֶּם לוֹ "you are beloved to Him, you are His acquisition"; וְאֵי אַתֶּם יְרוּשָּׁה לוֹ "and you are not His inheritance"): ACQUISITION AGAINST INHERITANCE — the bought field loved above the inherited ten (the compiled inheritance law's two titles on the tape); the compile: Israel's status as acquired (kinyan), a reference to 32:9's portion; no law (DV32-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 309:6 — MATERIAL. On 32:6. "WHO ACQUIRED YOU" — ONE OF THREE CALLED THE ACQUISITION OF THE PLACE: THE TORAH — "THE LORD ACQUIRED ME AT THE BEGINNING OF HIS WAY" (Proverbs 8:22); ISRAEL — "IS HE NOT YOUR FATHER WHO ACQUIRED YOU"; THE TEMPLE — "THIS MOUNTAIN WHICH HIS RIGHT HAND ACQUIRED" (Psalm 78:54). (זֶה אֶחָד מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה שֶׁנִּקְרְאוּ קִנְיָן לַמָּקוֹם "this is one of three called the acquisition of the Place"; בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ נִקְרָא קִנְיָן לַמָּקוֹם "the Temple is called the acquisition of the Place"): THE THREE ACQUISITIONS (the Torah, Israel, the Temple — Mishnah Avot 6:10 the parallel with five, cited; the Mekhilta on Exodus 15:16 the parallel); the Writings' seats cited, never read; the compile: a word's three seats, no law (DV32-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 309:7 — MATERIAL. On 32:6. "HE MADE YOU AND ESTABLISHED YOU" — R. MEIR USED TO SAY: A CITY THAT HAS EVERYTHING IN IT — PRIESTS FROM IT, PROPHETS FROM IT, SAGES FROM IT, SCRIBES FROM IT: "OUT OF HIM THE CORNERSTONE, OUT OF HIM THE PEG" (Zechariah 10:4); R. YEHUDAH SAYS: HE MADE YOU HOLLOWS UPON HOLLOWS (kavin); R. SHIMON BEN YEHUDAH SAYS: HE SET YOU ON YOUR BASE, FED YOU THE SPOIL OF SEVEN NATIONS, GAVE YOU WHAT HE SWORE TO YOU, MADE YOU INHERIT WHAT HE PROMISED; R. DOSTAI BEN YEHUDAH SAYS: HE MADE YOU CHAMBERS UPON CHAMBERS WITHIN — IF ONE ROSE ON ITS FELLOW YOU COULD NOT STAND. (כְּרַכָּא דְּכוֹלָּא בֵּיהּ "a city that has everything in it — the Aramaic saying"; כֹּהֲנִים מִתּוֹכוֹ נְבִיאִים מִתּוֹכוֹ חֲכָמִים מִתּוֹכוֹ סוֹפְרִים "priests from it, prophets from it, sages from it, scribes"; הִלְעִיטְךָ בִּזַּת שִׁבְעָה עֲמָמִים "fed you the spoil of seven nations"): FOUR READINGS OF "ESTABLISHED" — the whole city, the body's conduits, the seven nations' spoil (7:1's seven on the tape, the oath and the promise kept — a RUN CITATION of the tape's statuses), the body's chambers (the Prophets' seat cited, never read); no law (DV32-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 310:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:7. "REMEMBER THE DAYS OF OLD" (32:7) — HE SAID TO THEM: REMEMBER WHAT I DID TO THE FIRST GENERATIONS — TO THE GENERATION OF THE FLOOD, TO THE GENERATION OF THE DISPERSION, TO THE MEN OF SODOM. The head row of piska 310 (הִזָּכְרוּ מַה שֶּׁעָשִׂיתִי לַדּוֹרוֹת הָרִאשׁוֹנִים "remember what I did to the first generations"): the three judgments named (the tape's own entries — RUN CITATIONS: Genesis 6-8, 11, 19); the compile: the song's "remember" a call to the recorded past — no law (DV32-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 310:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:7. "CONSIDER THE YEARS OF GENERATION AND GENERATION" — THERE IS NO GENERATION WITHOUT MEN LIKE THE GENERATION OF THE FLOOD, NONE WITHOUT MEN LIKE SODOM — BUT EACH ONE IS JUDGED BY HIS DEEDS. (אֵין לְךָ דוֹר שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ כְּאַנְשֵׁי דוֹר "there is no generation without men like the generation"; שֶׁנִּדּוֹן כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד לְפִי מַעֲשָׂיו "that each and every one is judged by his deeds"): judgment by the person, not the generation (24:16's rule on the tape); no law (DV32-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 310:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:7. "ASK YOUR FATHER AND HE WILL DECLARE TO YOU" — THESE ARE THE PROPHETS: "AND ELISHA SAW AND CRIED: MY FATHER, MY FATHER" (2 Kings 2:12). (אֵלּוּ נְבִיאִים "these are the prophets"): the father the prophet; the Prophets' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 310:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:7. "YOUR ELDERS AND THEY WILL TELL YOU" — THESE ARE THE ELDERS: "GATHER ME SEVENTY MEN OF THE ELDERS OF ISRAEL" (Numbers 11:16). (אֵלּוּ זְקֵנִים "these are the elders"): the elders the seventy (the tape's own count at Numbers 11:16 — a RUN CITATION; the Sanhedrin the translator's gloss); no law (DV32-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 310:5 — MATERIAL. On 32:7. ANOTHER WORD: "REMEMBER THE DAYS OF OLD" — HE SAID TO THEM: WHENEVER THE HOLY ONE BRINGS SUFFERINGS UPON YOU, REMEMBER HOW MUCH GOOD AND CONSOLATION HE WILL GIVE YOU IN THE WORLD TO COME. (כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מֵבִיא עֲלֵיכֶם "whenever the Holy One, blessed be He, brings upon you"): the days of old read as the days of the world to come (olam both); no law (DV32-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 310:6 — MATERIAL. On 32:7. "CONSIDER THE YEARS OF GENERATION AND GENERATION" — THIS IS THE GENERATION OF THE MESSIAH, WHICH HAS THREE GENERATIONS: "THEY SHALL FEAR YOU WITH THE SUN AND BEFORE THE MOON, GENERATION OF GENERATIONS" (Psalm 72:5). (זֶה דּוֹרוֹ שֶׁל מָשִׁיחַ שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה "this is the generation of the Messiah, which has three"): the plural counted as three (the Mekhilta on Amalek the parallel, cited); the Writings' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 310:7 — MATERIAL. On 32:7. "ASK YOUR FATHER AND HE WILL DECLARE TO YOU" — TOMORROW ISRAEL WILL SEE AND HEAR AS THOSE WHO HEAR FROM THE MOUTH OF THE HOLY ONE: "YOUR EARS SHALL HEAR A WORD BEHIND YOU, SAYING" (Isaiah 30:21), "YOUR TEACHER SHALL NO MORE BE HIDDEN, YOUR EYES SHALL SEE YOUR TEACHER" (Isaiah 30:20). (לְמָחָר עֲתִידִים יִשְׂרָאֵל לִהְיוֹת רוֹאִים וְשׁוֹמְעִים "tomorrow Israel will see and hear"): the father the Holy One in the future; the Prophets' seats cited, never read; no law (DV32-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 310:8 — MATERIAL. On 32:7. "YOUR ELDERS AND THEY WILL TELL YOU" — WHAT I SHOWED THE ELDERS ON THE MOUNTAIN: "AND TO MOSES HE SAID: COME UP TO THE LORD" (Exodus 24:1). (מַה שֶּׁהֶרְאֵיתִי לַזְּקֵנִים בָּהָר "what I showed the elders on the mountain"): the elders' vision at Sinai (Exodus 24:1, 9-11 the tape's own entry — a RUN CITATION); no law (DV32-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 311:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in gen_27_the_call_2026-08-25.md). On 32:8. "WHEN THE MOST HIGH GAVE THE NATIONS THEIR INHERITANCE" (32:8) — BEFORE ABRAHAM OUR FATHER CAME THE HOLY ONE, AS IT WERE, JUDGED THE WORLD BY THE MEASURE OF CRUELTY: THE MEN OF THE FLOOD SINNED — HE FLOATED THEM LIKE SPARKS ON THE WATER; THE MEN OF THE TOWER — HE SCATTERED THEM FROM ONE END OF THE WORLD TO THE OTHER; THE MEN OF SODOM — HE SWEPT THEM WITH BRIMSTONE AND FIRE; BUT WHEN ABRAHAM CAME INTO THE WORLD HE MERITED TO RECEIVE SUFFERINGS, AND THEY BEGAN TO COME ON: "AND THERE WAS A FAMINE IN THE LAND, AND ABRAM WENT DOWN TO EGYPT" (Genesis 12:10); AND IF YOU SAY, WHY DO SUFFERINGS COME? FOR THE LOVE OF ISRAEL — "HE SET THE BORDERS OF THE PEOPLES BY THE NUMBER OF THE CHILDREN OF ISRAEL". The head row of piska 311 (הָיָה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא דָּן אֶת הָעוֹלָם "the Holy One, blessed be He, judged the world"; בְּמִדַּת אַכְזָרִיּוּת "by the measure of cruelty"; מִפְּנֵי חִבָּתָם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל "for the love of Israel"): THE TURN AT ABRAHAM — catastrophe before him, chastisement after (Genesis 12:10's famine the seat that read this row at the Genesis ledger of the call); the compile: the world's judgment mode a status that changes at Abraham's entry (the tape's own entries — the Flood, Babel, Sodom, the famine: RUN CITATIONS); no law (DV32-03). REREAD WHOLE (the Genesis ledger gen_27_the_call's reading — Genesis 12:10).
- Sifrei Devarim 311:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:8. ANOTHER WORD: "WHEN THE MOST HIGH GAVE THE NATIONS THEIR INHERITANCE" — WHEN THE HOLY ONE GAVE THE WORLD TO THE NATIONS HE MARKED THE BORDER OF EACH NATION SO THEY WOULD NOT BE MIXED: HE SENT THE SONS OF GOMER TO GOMER, OF MAGOG TO MAGOG, OF MADAI TO MADAI, OF JAVAN TO JAVAN, OF TUBAL TO TUBAL; HE MARKED THE NATIONS' BORDERS SO THEY WOULD NOT ENTER THE LAND OF ISRAEL — "HE SET THE BORDERS OF THE PEOPLES". (פֵּרֵשׁ תְּחוּמָהּ שֶׁל כָּל אֻמָּה וְאֻמָּה "He marked the border of each and every nation"; כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִכָּנְסוּ לְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל "so that they would not enter the land of Israel"): Genesis 10:2's sons of Japheth as the boundary list (the tape's own table of nations — a RUN CITATION); the compile: the nations' borders a status of the world, no law (DV32-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 311:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:8. ANOTHER WORD: "WHEN THE MOST HIGH GAVE THE NATIONS THEIR INHERITANCE" — WHEN THE HOLY ONE GAVE THE TORAH TO ISRAEL HE STOOD AND LOOKED AND CONSIDERED: "HE STOOD AND MEASURED THE EARTH, HE LOOKED AND MADE THE NATIONS TREMBLE" (Habakkuk 3:6) — AND THERE WAS NO NATION FIT TO RECEIVE THE TORAH BUT ISRAEL — "HE SET THE BORDERS OF THE PEOPLES". (וְלֹא הָיְתָה אֻמָּה בָּאֻמּוֹת שֶׁהָיְתָה רְאוּיָה לְקַבֵּל "and there was no nation among the nations fit to receive"): the Torah offered and the nations measured (the Prophets' seat cited, never read); no law (DV32-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 311:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:8. ANOTHER WORD: "WHEN THE MOST HIGH GAVE THE NATIONS THEIR INHERITANCE" — WHEN HE GAVE THE WORLD TO THE NATIONS HE ALLOTTED THEM GEHENNA: "THERE IS ASSYRIA AND ALL HER COMPANY" (Ezekiel 32:22), "THERE THE PRINCES OF THE NORTH AND ALL THE SIDONIANS" (32:30), "THERE EDOM, HER KINGS" (32:29); AND IF YOU SAY, WHO TAKES THEIR WEALTH AND HONOR? SAY: ISRAEL — "HE SET THE BORDERS OF THE PEOPLES". (חִלְּקָם בְּגֵיהִנֹּם "He allotted them Gehenna"; מִי נוֹטֵל עָשְׁרָם וּכְבוֹדָם שֶׁל אֵלּוּ "who takes the wealth and honor of these"): the nations' portion below and Israel's above (the Prophets' seats cited, never read); no law (DV32-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 311:5 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_10_ekev_2026-09-19.md, gen_25_babel_2026-08-25.md, gen_29_separation_promise_2026-08-25.md). On 32:8. ANOTHER WORD: "WHEN THE MOST HIGH GAVE THE NATIONS THEIR INHERITANCE" — WHEN THE HOLY ONE GAVE OF THE NATIONS THEIR SIN-FEARING AND FIT ONES; "WHEN HE SEPARATED THE CHILDREN OF MEN" — THIS IS THE GENERATION OF THE DISPERSION: "AND FROM THERE THE LORD SCATTERED THEM" (Genesis 11:9); "HE SET THE BORDERS OF THE PEOPLES" — R. ELIEZER SON OF R. YOSE THE GALILEAN SAYS: IT SAYS "SIXTY ARE THE QUEENS AND EIGHTY THE CONCUBINES" (Song of Songs 6:8) — SIXTY AND EIGHTY ARE A HUNDRED AND FORTY; OUR FATHERS WENT DOWN TO EGYPT WITH ONLY SEVENTY SOULS: "WITH SEVENTY SOULS YOUR FATHERS WENT DOWN" (10:22); AND IT SAYS "BORDERS (gevulot) OF THE PEOPLES" — NOT "BORDER OF THE PEOPLES" IS WRITTEN BUT "BORDERS": THE NATIONS MERITED TO TAKE TWO PORTIONS BY THE NUMBER OF THE CHILDREN OF ISRAEL. (זֶה דּוֹר הַפַּלָּגָה "this is the generation of the Dispersion"; ״שִׁשִּׁים וּשְׁמֹנִים הֲרֵי מֵאָה וְאַרְבָּעִים "sixty and eighty — behold a hundred and forty"; גְּבוּלֵי עַמִּים אֵין כָּתוּב כָּאן אֶלָּא גְּבֻלֹת "border of the peoples is not written here but borders"; זָכוּ אֻמּוֹת לִטּוֹל שְׁנֵי חֲלָקִים "the nations merited to take two portions"): THE NUMBER OF THE CHILDREN OF ISRAEL COMPUTED — seventy (10:22 the seat that read this row at chapter 10; Genesis 46:27 and Exodus 1:5 the tape's own count) against a hundred and forty nations (60 + 80, the Writings' seat cited): the plural "borders" read as two each — an arithmetic the compile can check on the tape's own figures (the seventy on the ledger; the hundred and forty the shelf's); Genesis 11:9's scattering the Genesis seats that read this row (Babel, the separation); no law (DV32-03). REREAD WHOLE (chapter 10's reading — 10:22; the Genesis ledgers gen_25_babel and gen_29_separation_promise — Genesis 11:9).
- Sifrei Devarim 312:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_14_reeh_2026-09-21.md). On 32:9. "FOR THE PORTION OF THE LORD IS HIS PEOPLE" (32:9) — A PARABLE OF A KING WHO HAD A FIELD AND GAVE IT TO TENANTS; THE TENANTS BEGAN TO STEAL FROM IT — HE TOOK IT FROM THEM AND GAVE IT TO THEIR SONS; THEY WERE WORSE THAN THE FIRST — HE GAVE IT TO THEIR SONS' SONS; THEY WERE WORSE STILL; A SON WAS BORN TO HIM — HE SAID: GO OUT OF MINE, I WILL NOT HAVE YOU IN IT; GIVE ME MY PORTION THAT I MAY KNOW IT. SO WHEN ABRAHAM CAME, DROSS WENT OUT OF HIM — ISHMAEL AND THE SONS OF KETURAH; ISAAC — ESAU AND THE CHIEFS OF EDOM; WORSE THAN THE FIRST; BUT WHEN JACOB CAME NO DROSS WENT OUT OF HIM — ALL HIS SONS WERE BORN FIT: "AND JACOB WAS A PERFECT MAN, DWELLING IN TENTS" (Genesis 25:27). FROM WHERE DOES THE PLACE KNOW HIS PORTION? FROM JACOB — "JACOB THE LOT OF HIS INHERITANCE", "FOR THE LORD CHOSE JACOB FOR HIMSELF" (Psalm 135:4); STILL THE MATTER HANGS — DID THE HOLY ONE CHOOSE JACOB OR JACOB THE HOLY ONE? "ISRAEL FOR HIS TREASURE"; STILL IT HANGS — "AND THE LORD YOUR GOD CHOSE YOU TO BE A TREASURED PEOPLE" (14:2); AND WHENCE THAT JACOB TOO CHOSE HIM? "NOT LIKE THESE IS THE PORTION OF JACOB" (Jeremiah 10:16). The head row of piska 312 (מָשָׁל לְמֶלֶךְ שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ שָׂדֶה וּנְתָנָהּ לַעֲרִיסִים "a parable of a king who had a field and gave it to tenants"; מֵהֵיכָן הַמָּקוֹם מַכִּיר אֶת חֶלְקוֹ "from where does the Place know His portion"; וַעֲדַיִן הַדָּבָר תָּלוּי בִּדְלָא תָּלוּי "and still the matter hangs on what is not settled"): THE PORTION TRACED TO JACOB — three generations of tenants (Abraham's and Isaac's dross, Jacob's twelve fit sons: the tape's own genealogy, RUN CITATIONS), then the chooser settled by 14:2 (the seat that read this row at chapter 14 — the treasured people compiled there) and Jacob's own choice by Jeremiah; the compile: the_lords_people status seated at Jacob, both directions of the choice (DV32-03); the Writings' and the Prophets' seats cited, never read. REREAD WHOLE (chapter 14's reading — 14:2).
- Sifrei Devarim 312:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:9. "JACOB THE CORD (chevel) OF HIS INHERITANCE" — CORD IS ONLY LOT: "THE LOTS HAVE FALLEN TO ME IN PLEASANT PLACES" (Psalm 16:6), "AND THE LOTS OF MANASSEH FELL TEN" (Joshua 17:5), "OUT OF THE LOT OF THE CHILDREN OF JUDAH THE INHERITANCE OF SIMEON" (Joshua 19:9). ANOTHER WORD: AS THIS CORD IS THREEFOLD, SO JACOB WAS THE THIRD OF THE FATHERS AND RECEIVED THE REWARD OF ALL: WHEN ABRAHAM WAS BORN — "A BROTHER IS BORN FOR ADVERSITY" (Proverbs 17:17); ISAAC — "TWO ARE BETTER THAN ONE" (Ecclesiastes 4:9); JACOB — "THE THREEFOLD CORD IS NOT QUICKLY BROKEN" (Ecclesiastes 4:12). (אֵין חֶבֶל אֶלָּא גּוֹרָל "cord is only lot"; מַה חֶבֶל זֶה מְשֻׁלָּשׁ "as this cord is threefold"; כָּךְ הָיָה יַעֲקֹב שְׁלִישִׁי לָאָבוֹת "so Jacob was the third of the fathers"): the cord as the lot (Onkelos: adav, the lot — the measure) and as the three strands; the Hebrew carries a bracketed "and it says" (an editor's supplement in the export); the Prophets' and the Writings' seats cited, never read; no law (DV32-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 313:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in gen_31_covenant_pieces_2026-08-25.md). On 32:10. "HE FOUND HIM IN A DESERT LAND" (32:10) — THIS IS ABRAHAM. A PARABLE OF A KING WHO WENT OUT WITH HIS TROOPS TO THE DESERT; HIS TROOPS LEFT HIM IN A PLACE OF TROUBLES, OF RAIDERS, OF BANDITS, AND WENT; ONE WARRIOR WAS APPOINTED TO HIM: MY LORD KING, LET NOT YOUR HEART FAIL, FEAR NOTHING — BY YOUR LIFE I WILL NOT LEAVE YOU UNTIL YOU ENTER YOUR PALACE AND SLEEP IN YOUR BED — "AND HE SAID TO HIM: I AM THE LORD WHO BROUGHT YOU OUT OF UR OF THE CHALDEES" (Genesis 15:7). The head row of piska 313 (זֶה אַבְרָהָם "this is Abraham"; חַיֶּיךָ שֶׁאֵינִי מְנִיחֲךָ עַד שֶׁתִּכָּנֵס לְפַלְטֵרִים שֶׁלְּךָ "by your life, I will not leave you until you enter your palace"): THE FIRST OF FOUR READINGS OF THE VERSE — Abraham found (313:1-4), Israel at Sinai (313:5-9), Israel in the desert (313:10-13), the future (313:14-16); Genesis 15:7 the seat that read this row at the Genesis ledger of the covenant of the pieces; THE ENGLISH MISPRINTS THE SEAT (Genesis 16:7 for the Hebrew's 15:7 — the Hebrew's marker true); the compile: no law (DV32-04). REREAD WHOLE (the Genesis ledger gen_31_covenant_pieces' reading — Genesis 15:7).
- Sifrei Devarim 313:2 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in gen_27_the_call_2026-08-25.md). On 32:10. "HE COMPASSED HIM ABOUT" — AS IT SAYS "AND THE LORD SAID TO ABRAM: GO FROM YOUR LAND" (Genesis 12:1). (יְסֹבְבֶנְהוּ כָּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר "He compassed him about — as it is said"): the compassing Abraham's journey (Genesis 12:1 the seat that read this row at the Genesis ledger of the call — a RUN CITATION); no law (DV32-04). REREAD WHOLE (the Genesis ledger gen_27_the_call's reading — Genesis 12:1).
- Sifrei Devarim 313:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:10. "HE CARED FOR HIM (yevonenehu — gave him understanding)" — BEFORE ABRAHAM OUR FATHER CAME THE HOLY ONE, AS IT WERE, WAS KING OVER THE HEAVENS ALONE: "THE LORD, GOD OF HEAVEN, WHO TOOK ME" (Genesis 24:7); BUT WHEN ABRAHAM CAME HE MADE HIM KING OVER HEAVEN AND EARTH: "I WILL MAKE YOU SWEAR BY THE LORD, GOD OF HEAVEN AND GOD OF EARTH" (Genesis 24:3). (לֹא הָיָה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מֶלֶךְ אֶלָּא "the Holy One, blessed be He, was king only"; הִמְלִיכוֹ עַל הַשָּׁמַיִם וְעַל הָאָרֶץ "he made Him king over the heavens and over the earth"): THE TWO TITLES OF GENESIS 24 read as before and after Abraham (the tape's own entries — RUN CITATIONS); the compile: no law — a status of the Name's reach (DV32-04). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 313:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:10. "HE KEPT HIM AS THE APPLE OF HIS EYE" — HAD THE HOLY ONE ASKED ABRAHAM FOR HIS EYEBALL HE WOULD HAVE GIVEN IT, AND NOT HIS EYEBALL ONLY BUT HIS SOUL, DEAREST OF ALL: "TAKE NOW YOUR SON, YOUR ONLY ONE (yechidekha), ISAAC" (Genesis 22:2) — IS IT NOT KNOWN HE WAS HIS ONLY SON? RATHER THIS IS THE SOUL CALLED THE ONLY ONE: "DELIVER MY SOUL FROM THE SWORD, MY ONLY ONE FROM THE DOG" (Psalm 22:21). (אֲפִלּוּ בִּקֵּשׁ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מֵאַבְרָהָם אָבִינוּ "even had the Holy One, blessed be He, asked of Abraham our father"; אֶלָּא זוֹ נֶפֶשׁ שֶׁנִּקְרֵאת יְחִידָה "rather this is the soul that is called the only one"): the binding's "only one" read as the soul (Genesis 22:2 the tape's own entry — a RUN CITATION; the Writings' seat cited); no law (DV32-04). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 313:5 — MATERIAL. On 32:10. ANOTHER WORD: "HE FOUND HIM IN A DESERT LAND" — THESE ARE ISRAEL: "LIKE GRAPES IN THE WILDERNESS I FOUND ISRAEL" (Hosea 9:10). (אֵלּוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל "these are Israel"): the second reading opens — Israel found (the Prophets' seat cited, never read); no law (DV32-04). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 313:6 — MATERIAL. On 32:10. "AND IN THE WASTE, A HOWLING WILDERNESS" — IN A PLACE OF TROUBLES, OF RAIDERS, OF BANDITS. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 313:7 — MATERIAL. On 32:10. "HE COMPASSED HIM ABOUT" — BEFORE MOUNT SINAI: "AND YOU SHALL SET BOUNDS FOR THE PEOPLE ROUND ABOUT, SAYING" (Exodus 19:12). - Sifrei Devarim 313:8 — MATERIAL. On 32:10. "HE CARED FOR HIM" — WITH THE TEN UTTERANCES: IT TEACHES THAT WHEN THE UTTERANCE WENT OUT FROM THE MOUTH OF THE HOLY ONE ISRAEL SAW IT AND UNDERSTOOD IT, AND KNEW HOW MUCH MIDRASH (EXPOSITION) IS IN IT, HOW MUCH LAW, HOW MANY A-FORTIORI ARGUMENTS, HOW MANY VERBAL ANALOGIES. (בַּעֲשֶׂרֶת הַדִּבְּרוֹת "with the ten utterances"; וְכַמָּה קַלִּים וַחֲמוּרִים יֵשׁ בּוֹ "and how many a-fortiori arguments are in it"; וְכַמָּה גְּזֵרוֹת שָׁווֹת יֵשׁ בּוֹ "and how many verbal analogies are in it"): THE MIDDOT SEEN IN THE UTTERANCE — the a-fortiori (I1) and the verbal analogy (I2) NAMED as contents of each of the ten words (the Mekhilta on Exodus 20:15 the parallel, cited): the compile rules present in the program at its giving — the tradition's own statement of THE COMPILER LAW; no argument by a middah here (the codes named, not typed as an argument); no law (DV32-04). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 313:9 — MATERIAL. On 32:10. "HE KEPT HIM AS THE APPLE OF HIS EYE" — THEY WENT BACK TWELVE MIL AND RETURNED TWELVE MIL AT EVERY UTTERANCE, AND WERE NOT DAUNTED BY THE VOICES NOR BY THE TORCHES. (הוֹלְכִים שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר מִיל וְחוֹזְרִים שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר "they went twelve mil and returned twelve mil"): the twelve mil of the camp (Tosefta Arakhin 1:10 the testing shelf, cited by the translator; the camp's size the answer sheet's number); no law (DV32-04). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 313:10 — MATERIAL. On 32:10. ANOTHER WORD: "HE FOUND HIM IN A DESERT LAND" — EVERYTHING WAS FOUND AND SUPPLIED TO THEM IN THE DESERT: THE WELL RISING FOR THEM, THE MANNA COMING DOWN, THE QUAIL AT HAND, THE CLOUDS OF GLORY ROUND ABOUT; "AND IN THE WASTE, A HOWLING WILDERNESS" — IN A PLACE OF TROUBLES, OF RAIDERS, OF BANDITS. (הַכֹּל מָצוּי וּמְסֻפָּק לָהֶם בַּמִּדְבָּר "everything found and supplied to them in the desert"; בְּאֵר עוֹלָה לָהֶם מָן יוֹרֵד לָהֶם "the well rising for them, the manna coming down for them"): THE DESERT'S GIFTS — the well, the manna, the quail, the clouds (305:3's three gifts of the three righteous, sitting 19; the daemon of the three gifts the debt carried to 34:5): Onkelos's "He supplied their needs" (32:10) is this row's reading (the measure); the tape's own entries (Exodus 16, Numbers 11, 20 — RUN CITATIONS); no law (DV32-04). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 313:11 — MATERIAL. On 32:10. "HE COMPASSED HIM ABOUT" — WITH BANNERS: THREE FROM THE NORTH, THREE FROM THE SOUTH, THREE FROM THE EAST, THREE FROM THE WEST. (בִּדְגָלִים שְׁלֹשָׁה מִן הַצָּפוֹן שְׁלֹשָׁה מִן הַדָּרוֹם "with banners: three from the north, three from the south"): the camp of Numbers 2 (three tribes a side — the tape's own count, a RUN CITATION); no law (DV32-04). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 313:12 — MATERIAL. On 32:10. "HE CARED FOR HIM" — WITH TWO GIFTS: WHEN ONE OF THE NATIONS STRETCHED HIS HAND TO GATHER OF THE MANNA NOTHING CAME UP IN HIS HAND; TO DRAW WATER FROM THE WELL — NOTHING CAME UP IN HIS HAND. (בִּשְׁתֵּי מַתָּנוֹת "with two gifts"; לֹא הָיָה עוֹלֶה בְּיָדוֹ כְּלוּם "nothing came up in his hand"): the manna and the well Israel's alone; no law (DV32-04). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 313:13 — MATERIAL. On 32:10. "HE KEPT HIM AS THE APPLE OF HIS EYE" — AS IT SAYS "RISE UP, O LORD, AND LET YOUR ENEMIES BE SCATTERED, LET THOSE WHO HATE YOU FLEE BEFORE YOU" (Numbers 10:35). (יִצְּרֶנְהוּ כְּאִישׁוֹן עֵינוֹ "He kept him as the apple of His eye"): the ark's song the guard (Numbers 10:35 the tape's own entry — a RUN CITATION); no law (DV32-04). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 313:14 — MATERIAL. On 32:10. ANOTHER WORD: "HE FOUND HIM IN A DESERT LAND" — THIS IS THE TIME TO COME: "THEREFORE, BEHOLD, I WILL ALLURE HER AND BRING HER INTO THE WILDERNESS AND SPEAK TO HER HEART" (Hosea 2:16). "BUT JESHURUN GREW FAT AND KICKED" (32:15) — FROM SATIETY THEY REBEL; SO YOU FIND THE GENERATION OF THE FLOOD REBELLED ONLY OUT OF FOOD AND DRINK AND EASE — "THEIR HOUSES ARE SAFE FROM FEAR" (Job 21:9) (THE BARAITA AS WRITTEN AT PISKA 43); THE MEN OF THE TOWER ONLY OUT OF EASE — "THE WHOLE EARTH WAS OF ONE LANGUAGE" (Genesis 11:1); THE MEN OF SODOM ONLY OUT OF FOOD — "A LAND FROM WHICH BREAD COMES" (Job 28:5) (THE MISHNAH ABRIDGED), "AS I LIVE, SAYS THE LORD, SODOM HAS NOT DONE" (Ezekiel 16:48); AND RABBAN GAMLIEL, R. YEHOSHUA, R. ELAZAR BEN AZARIAH AND R. AKIVA ENTERING ROME (THE BARAITA AT PISKA 43); AND THE MEN OF THE DESERT ONLY OUT OF FOOD AND DRINK — "THE PEOPLE SAT TO EAT AND DRINK AND ROSE TO PLAY" (Exodus 32:6), "THEY HAVE QUICKLY TURNED FROM THE WAY" (Exodus 32:8). THE HOLY ONE SAID TO MOSES: TELL ISRAEL, WHEN YOU ENTER THE LAND YOU WILL REBEL ONLY OUT OF EATING AND DRINKING AND EASE — "WHEN I BRING HIM TO THE LAND … FLOWING WITH MILK AND HONEY, AND HE EATS AND IS FULL AND GROWS FAT AND TURNS TO OTHER GODS" (31:20); MOSES SAID TO ISRAEL THE SAME — "LEST YOU EAT AND BE FULL" (8:12), "AND YOUR HERDS AND FLOCKS MULTIPLY" (8:13), "AND YOUR HEART BE LIFTED UP AND YOU FORGET THE LORD YOUR GOD" (8:14). The head row of piska 318 (לְפִי שֹׂבַע מוֹרְדִים "from satiety they rebel"; אֶלָּא מִתּוֹךְ מַאֲכָל וּמִשְׁתֶּה וּמִתּוֹךְ שַׁלְוָה "only out of food and drink and out of ease"; אִי אַתֶּם עֲתִידִים לִמְרֹד אֶלָּא מִתּוֹךְ אֲכִילָה "you will rebel only out of eating"): SATIETY BEFORE REBELLION — the Flood, the Tower, Sodom, the calf, then 31:20 in God's mouth and 8:12-14 in Moses' (the two seats that read this row at chapter 8 and at sitting 19; Genesis 11:1 the Babel ledger's seat): 31:20's twin by sense with 32:15 (the measure) here TAUGHT by the spine — the Link Review Law's teacher; THE EXPORT'S ABRIDGEMENTS — the Hebrew carries a copyist's Aramaic notes ("etc., the baraita as written in Devarim", "etc., the Mishnah") for three lacunae filled at piska 43:4-8 (the translator's bracketed notes), and cites Ezekiel 16:48 where 16:49 is the germane verse (the translator); the compile: satiety the rebellion's condition — a status the song names (DV32-06); the tape's own entries (Genesis 11:1, Exodus 32:6-8) RUN CITATIONS. REREAD WHOLE (chapter 8's reading — 8:12-14; sitting 19's — 31:20; the Genesis ledger gen_25_babel — Genesis 11:1).
- Sifrei Devarim 318:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:15. SO YOU FIND JOB'S SONS AND DAUGHTERS: PUNISHMENT CAME ON THEM ONLY OUT OF FOOD AND DRINK — "YOUR SONS AND DAUGHTERS WERE EATING AND DRINKING WINE IN THEIR ELDEST BROTHER'S HOUSE, AND BEHOLD A GREAT WIND" (Job 1:18); THE TEN TRIBES WERE EXILED ONLY OUT OF FOOD AND DRINK AND EASE — "WHO LIE ON BEDS OF IVORY" (Amos 6:4), "WHO DRINK WINE IN BOWLS" (6:6), "THEREFORE NOW THEY SHALL GO CAPTIVE AT THE HEAD OF THE CAPTIVES" (6:7); AND IN THE DAYS OF THE MESSIAH THEY WILL REBEL ONLY OUT OF EATING AND DRINKING — "JESHURUN GREW FAT AND KICKED AND FORSOOK GOD WHO MADE HIM". (שֶׁלֹּא גָלוּ אֶלָּא מִתּוֹךְ מַאֲכָל וּמִשְׁתֶּה וְשַׁלְוָה "they were exiled only out of food and drink and ease"; לִימוֹת הַמָּשִׁיחַ "in the days of the Messiah"): the rule carried to Job, the ten tribes and the last days; the Writings' and the Prophets' seats cited, never read; no law (DV32-06). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 318:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:15. A PARABLE OF ONE WHO HAD A CALF AND RUBBED IT AND CURRIED IT AND FED IT VETCH SO IT WOULD PLOW FOR HIM; WHEN THE CALF GREW ITS OWNER PUT THE YOKE ON IT, AND IT KICKED AND BROKE THE YOKE AND SNAPPED THE PEGS — "YOU HAVE BROKEN THE BARS OF WOOD, BUT YOU SHALL MAKE IN THEIR PLACE BARS OF IRON" (Jeremiah 28:13). (מָשָׁל לְאֶחָד שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ עֵגֶל "a parable of one who had a calf"; וְקִרְטֵעַ וְשִׁבֵּר אֶת הָעֹל "and it kicked and broke the yoke"): the kicking calf the verse's verb (ba'at — kicked); the Prophets' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-06). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 318:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:15. "YOU GREW FAT" — IN THE DAYS OF JEROBOAM; "YOU GREW THICK" — IN THE DAYS OF AHAB; "YOU BECAME GROSS" — ALL OF IT IN THE DAYS OF JEHU. (שָׁמַנְתָּ בִּימֵי יָרָבְעָם עָבִיתָ בִּימֵי אַחְאָב "you grew fat in the days of Jeroboam, you grew thick in the days of Ahab"; כָּשִׂיתָ הַכֹּל בִּימֵי יֵהוּא "you became gross — all of it in the days of Jehu"): THE THREE FATNESSES THE THREE KINGS OF ISRAEL — the parser's false eight (shamanta, "you grew fat") read by the spine as a verb dated to Jeroboam: the compile's guard confirmed by the shelf (DV32-06); the kings the Prophets' seats, cited, never read. Fresh.

- Sifrei Devarim 318:13 — MATERIAL. On 32:15. "THEY SACRIFICED TO DEMONS, NO-GODS" (32:17) — HAD THEY SERVED THE SUN AND THE MOON, THE STARS AND THE CONSTELLATIONS, THINGS THE WORLD NEEDS AND BENEFITS BY, THE JEALOUSY WOULD NOT BE DOUBLED; BUT THEY SERVE THINGS THAT DO THEM NO GOOD BUT HARM. "TO DEMONS" — WHAT IS A DEMON'S WAY? IT ENTERS A MAN AND OVERPOWERS HIM. (לֹא הָיְתָה קִנְאָה כְּפוּלָה "the jealousy would not be doubled"; מַה דַּרְכּוֹ שֶׁל שֵׁד "what is a demon's way"; נִכְנָס בָּאָדָם וְכוֹפֶה אוֹתוֹ "it enters a man and overpowers him"): the doubled jealousy (Onkelos's "demons in whom is no use" — the measure — the spine's "things that harm them"); the Sifra on Leviticus 17:7 the parallel, cited; no law (DV32-06). "GODS THEY KNEW NOT" — WHOM THE NATIONS OF THE WORLD DO NOT RECOGNIZE; "NEW ONES THAT CAME UP OF LATE" — WHENEVER ONE OF THE NATIONS SEES IT HE SAYS: THIS IS A JEWISH IMAGE; "AS MY HAND HAS FOUND THE KINGDOMS OF THE IDOLS, WHOSE IMAGES EXCEEDED JERUSALEM'S AND SAMARIA'S" (Isaiah 10:10) — IT TEACHES THAT JERUSALEM AND SAMARIA SUPPLIED THE MOLD FOR ALL WHO COME INTO THE WORLD. "OF THE ROCK THAT BEGOT YOU YOU WERE UNMINDFUL" (32:18) — THE HOLY ONE SAID TO THEM: YOU HAVE MADE ME AS A MALE SEEKING TO GIVE BIRTH: WERE A WOMAN SITTING ON THE BIRTHSTOOL WOULD SHE NOT BE IN PAIN — "THE CHILDREN ARE COME TO THE BIRTH AND THERE IS NO STRENGTH TO BRING FORTH" (2 Kings 19:3)? WERE THERE TWO IN HER WOMB — "AND THE CHILDREN STRUGGLED WITHIN HER" (Genesis 25:22)? WERE IT A MALE, WHOSE WAY IS NOT TO BEAR, SEEKING TO BEAR — WOULD THE PAIN NOT BE DOUBLED AND REDOUBLED — "ASK NOW AND SEE WHETHER A MAN TRAVAILS WITH CHILD" (Jeremiah 30:6)? Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 320:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:19. ANOTHER WORD: BEHOLD, I HAND THEM OVER INTO THE HAND OF THE FOUR KINGDOMS THAT THEY MAY ENSLAVE THEM. (הֲרֵינִי מוֹסְרָם בְּיַד אַרְבַּע מַלְכֻיּוֹת "behold, I hand them over into the hand of the four kingdoms"): the hiding as the handing over (the four kingdoms — 313:15, 317:4); no law (DV32-07). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 320:5 — MATERIAL. On 32:19. "I WILL SEE WHAT THEIR END SHALL BE" — I WILL KNOW WHAT THEIR END IS; "FOR THEY ARE A GENERATION OF OVERTURNINGS (tahpukhot)" — AN OVERTURNED GENERATION; NOT "A GENERATION THAT OVERTURNS" IS WRITTEN BUT "A GENERATION OF OVERTURNINGS": THEY ARE FICKLE, THEY ARE FAITHLESS. (דוֹר תַּהְפֹּךְ״ אֵין כָּתוּב כָּאן אֶלָּא "a generation that overturns is not written here but"; הֲפַכְפְּכָנִים הֵם פּוֹרְנִים הֵם "they are fickle, they are faithless"): the noun's plural read as habit; no law (DV32-07). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 320:6 — MATERIAL. On 32:19. "CHILDREN IN WHOM IS NO FAITHFULNESS" — YOU ARE CHILDREN IN WHOM THERE IS NO FAITH: YOU STOOD AT MOUNT SINAI AND SAID "ALL THAT THE LORD HAS SPOKEN WE WILL DO AND HEAR" (Exodus 24:7), AND I SAID "YOU ARE GODS" (Psalm 82:6); WHEN YOU SAID TO THE CALF "THESE ARE YOUR GODS, O ISRAEL" (Exodus 32:4), I SAID TO YOU "SURELY AS MAN YOU SHALL DIE" (Psalm 82:7); I BROUGHT YOU INTO THE LAND OF YOUR FATHERS AND BUILT YOU THE CHOSEN HOUSE AND SAID YOU WOULD NEVER BE EXILED FROM IT; WHEN YOU SAID "WE HAVE NO PORTION IN DAVID" (2 Samuel 20:1), I SAID "ISRAEL SHALL SURELY BE LED CAPTIVE OUT OF HIS LAND" (Amos 7:17). R. DOSTAI BEN YEHUDAH SAYS: DO NOT READ "NO FAITHFULNESS (emun) IN THEM" BUT "NO AMEN IN THEM" — THEY WOULD NOT ANSWER AMEN AFTER THE PROPHETS WHEN THEY BLESSED THEM: "TO PERFORM THE OATH WHICH I SWORE TO YOUR FATHERS, TO GIVE THEM A LAND FLOWING WITH MILK AND HONEY, AS THIS DAY" (Jeremiah 11:5) — AND NOT ONE OPENED HIS MOUTH TO ANSWER AMEN UNTIL JEREMIAH CAME AND ANSWERED: "AND I ANSWERED AND SAID: AMEN, O LORD". (בָּנִים אַתֶּם שֶׁאֵין בָּכֶם אֱמוּנָה "you are children in whom there is no faith"; כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֲמַרְתֶּם לָעֵגֶל "when you said to the calf"; אֶל תְּהִי קוֹרֵא לֹא אֵמֻן בָּם "do not read: no faithfulness in them, but no Amen in them"; עַד שֶׁבָּא יִרְמְיָה וְעָנָה אָמֵן "until Jeremiah came and answered Amen"): TWO FORFEITS — Sinai's "you are gods" lost at the calf, the Temple's permanence lost at "no portion in David" (the tape's own entries Exodus 24:7, 32:4 RUN CITATIONS; the Prophets' and the Writings' seats cited); R. DOSTAI'S THIRD "DO NOT READ" — no Amen after the prophets (the Amen of 306:37 refused); THE ENGLISH MISALIGNED — R. Dostai's teaching stands in this row's Hebrew but the export's English column carries it under 320:7 (the Hebrew governs the seat); the compile: no law (DV32-07). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 320:7 — MATERIAL. On 32:19. "THEY HAVE ROUSED ME TO JEALOUSY WITH A NO-GOD, PROVOKED ME WITH THEIR VANITIES" (32:21) — A MAN MAY SERVE AN IMAGE, A THING THAT SEES HIM; BUT THEY SERVE A REFLECTION, AND NOT A REFLECTION ONLY BUT THE VAPOR (hevel) THAT RISES FROM THE POT — "PROVOKED ME WITH THEIR VAPORS". (אֲבָל הֵם עוֹבְדִים לְבָבוּאָה "but they serve a reflection"; אֶלָּא לְהֶבֶל זֶה שֶׁעוֹלֶה מִן הַקְּדֵרָה "but the vapor that rises from the pot"): vanities read as steam (hevel both); the English of this row opens with 320:6's R. Dostai (the misalignment noted there); no law (DV32-07). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 320:8 — MATERIAL. On 32:19. "AND I WILL ROUSE THEM TO JEALOUSY WITH A NO-PEOPLE (belo am)" — DO NOT READ "WITH A NO-PEOPLE" BUT "WITH A MIXED PEOPLE (belui am)": THESE ARE THEY WHO COME FROM AMONG THE NATIONS AND KINGDOMS AND DRIVE THEM FROM THEIR HOUSES. (אֶלָּא בְּלוּי עָם "but: with a mixed people"; וּמוֹצִיאִים אוֹתָם מִתּוֹךְ בָּתֵּיהֶם "and drive them out of their houses"): the fourth "do not read" — the no-people the displaced; no law (DV32-07). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 320:9 — MATERIAL. On 32:19. ANOTHER WORD: THESE ARE THEY WHO COME FROM BARBARIA AND FROM MAURITANIA AND WALK NAKED IN THE MARKET — THERE IS NO MAN MORE DESPISED AND DEFECTIVE IN THE WORLD THAN ONE WHO WALKS NAKED IN THE MARKET. (אֵלּוּ הַבָּאִים מִבַּרְבַּרְיָא וּמִמַּרְטַנְיָא "these are they who come from Barbaria and from Mauritania"; אֶלָּא הַמְהַלֵּךְ עָרוֹם בַּשּׁוּק "than one who walks naked in the market"): the no-people named by their lands; no law (DV32-07). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 320:10 — MATERIAL. On 32:19. "WITH A VILE NATION I WILL PROVOKE THEM" — THESE ARE THE SECTARIANS (minim): "THE VILE MAN HAS SAID IN HIS HEART: THERE IS NO GOD" (Psalm 14:1). (אֵלּוּ הַמִּינִים "these are the sectarians"): the vile nation the deniers (the Writings' seat cited, never read); no law (DV32-07). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 320:11 — MATERIAL. On 32:19. "FOR A FIRE IS KINDLED IN MY NOSTRIL (af)" (32:22) — WHEN PUNISHMENT GOES OUT FROM BEFORE ME IT GOES OUT ONLY IN ANGER (af); AND WHENCE THAT ANGER IS WITHIN GEHENNA? "AND BURNS TO THE DEPTHS OF SHEOL". (אֵין יוֹצְאָה אֶלָּא בְּאַף "it goes out only in anger"; וּמִנַּיִן אַף בְּתוֹךְ גֵּיהִנֹּם "and whence that anger is within Gehenna"): the nostril anger, the fire Gehenna's (Onkelos: an east wind from before Him — the measure); no law (DV32-07). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 320:12 — MATERIAL. On 32:19. "AND DEVOURS THE EARTH WITH HER PRODUCE" — THIS IS THE LAND OF ISRAEL; "AND SETS ABLAZE THE FOUNDATIONS OF THE MOUNTAINS" — THIS IS JERUSALEM: "JERUSALEM, THE MOUNTAINS ARE ROUND ABOUT HER" (Psalm 125:2). (זוֹ אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל "this is the land of Israel"; זוֹ יְרוּשָׁלִַם "this is Jerusalem"): the earth the land, the mountains Jerusalem (the Writings' seat cited, never read); no law (DV32-07). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 320:13 — MATERIAL. On 32:19. ANOTHER WORD: "AND DEVOURS THE EARTH WITH HER PRODUCE" — THIS IS THE WORLD AND ITS FULLNESS; "AND SETS ABLAZE THE FOUNDATIONS OF THE MOUNTAINS" — THESE ARE THE FOUR KINGDOMS: "AND BEHOLD, FOUR CHARIOTS" (Zechariah 6:1). (זֶה הָעוֹלָם וּמְלוֹאוֹ "this is the world and its fullness"; אֵלּוּ אַרְבַּע מַלְכֻיּוֹת "these are the four kingdoms"): the last row of the piska — the fire on the kingdoms (the Prophets' seat cited, never read); no law (DV32-07). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. "I WILL HEAP EVILS UPON THEM" (32:23) — BEHOLD, I GATHER AND BRING UPON THEM ALL THE PUNISHMENTS TOGETHER AS ONE; ANOTHER WORD: I GATHER THEM ALL INTO A FORTRESS AND BRING UPON THEM ALL THE PUNISHMENTS AS ONE. The head row of piska 321 (הֲרֵינִי מַכְנִיס וּמֵבִיא עֲלֵיהֶם כָּל הַפֻּרְעָנֻיּוֹת כֻּלָּם "behold, I gather and bring upon them all the punishments"; לְתוֹךְ מְצוּדָה "into a fortress"): the heaping as gathering (Onkelos: I will gather evils — the measure); no law (DV32-08). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. ANOTHER WORD: NOT "I WILL END (asof) EVILS UPON THEM" IS WRITTEN BUT "I WILL HEAP (aspeh)" — THAT ALL THE PUNISHMENTS BE SPENT AND THEY NOT SPENT; AND SO "MY ARROWS I WILL SPEND (akhalleh) ON THEM" — NOT "MY ARROWS SHALL CONSUME THEM" IS WRITTEN BUT "MY ARROWS I WILL SPEND ON THEM": MY ARROWS ARE SPENT AND THEY ARE NOT SPENT. (שֶׁיְּהוּ כָּל פֻּרְעָנִיּוֹת כָּלוֹת וְהֵם אֵינָם כָּלִים "that all the punishments be spent and they not spent"; חִצַּי כָּלִים וְהֵם אֵינָם כָּלִים "My arrows are spent and they are not spent"): THE ARROWS SPENT, ISRAEL NOT — the reading Sifrei 43:24 (outside) carried to chapter 11; the verb's form (akhalleh, I will spend, not yokhelu, they will consume) the ink the argument rests on; the compile: the punishment bounded, the people's survival a status (DV32-08). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. ANOTHER WORD: "MY ARROWS I WILL SPEND ON THEM" — THESE ARE THE ARROWS OF FAMINE: "WHEN I SEND THE EVIL ARROWS OF FAMINE AGAINST THEM" (Ezekiel 5:16). (אֵלּוּ חִצֵּי רָעָב "these are the arrows of famine"): the arrows named (Onkelos: My plagues — the measure); the Prophets' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-08). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. "THE WASTING OF HUNGER AND THE DEVOURING OF THE FIERY BOLT" (32:24) — THAT THEY BE WASTED WITH HUNGER AND CAST OUT IN THE STREETS: "AND THE PEOPLE TO WHOM THEY PROPHESY SHALL BE CAST OUT IN THE STREETS OF JERUSALEM" (Jeremiah 14:16). (שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מֻזָּאִים בָּרָעָב וּמֻשְׁלָכִים בַּחוּצוֹת "that they be wasted with hunger and cast out in the streets"): THE SECOND MISPRINTED MARKER OF THE CHAPTER — the Hebrew row's marker reads 32:27 while its quoted words are 32:24's ("the wasting of hunger and the devouring of the fiery bolt"; the English cites 32:24) — the row seated at 32:24 by its own words (328's misprint the first, sitting 19's lesson 1); the Prophets' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-08). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:5 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. "AND BITTER DESTRUCTION (ketev meriri)" — IN PASSING YOU LEARN THAT WHOEVER HAS A DEMON IN HIM FOAMS (merir). (שֶׁכָּל מִי שֶׁהַשֵּׁד בּוֹ הוּא מֵרִיר "that whoever has a demon in him foams"): the demon of Onkelos's "evil spirits" (the measure) by the word's sound alone (the translator: "all we have is word-play"); no law (DV32-08). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:6 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. "AND THE TEETH OF BEASTS" — DO NOT READ "THE TEETH OF BEASTS (behemot) I WILL SEND UPON THEM" BUT "THE TOOTH OF BESTIALITY (behamut) I WILL SEND UPON THEM": THAT THEY BE HEATED AND GO ROUND AFTER EVERY TRANSGRESSION. (אַל תְּהִי קוֹרֵא "do not read"; שֶׁיְּהוּ מִתְחַמְּמִים וּמְחַזְּרִים עַל כָּל עֲבֵרוֹת "that they be heated and go round after every transgression"): the fifth "do not read" — the beast's tooth an appetite; no law (DV32-08). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:7 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. ANOTHER WORD: THAT ONE OF THEM BE HEATED AND DRAW HIMSELF AND RAISE A GROWTH AND DIE OF IT; ANOTHER WORD: "THE TEETH OF BEASTS I WILL SEND UPON THEM" — THAT HIS OWN BEAST BITE HIM AND RAISE A GROWTH AND HE DIE OF IT; THEY SAID: IT HAPPENED THAT EWES BIT AND KILLED. (שֶׁתְּהֵא בְּהֶמְתּוֹ נוֹשֶׁכֶתּוֹ "that his own beast bite him"; שֶׁרְּחֵלִים נוֹשְׁכִים וּמְמִיתִים "that ewes bit and killed"): the beast's tooth literal again — the ewe's bite (the English of this row repeats 321:8's text — a duplication in the export's English column); no law (DV32-08). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:8 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. "WITH THE VENOM OF CRAWLING THINGS OF THE DUST" — THAT THEY BE DRAGGED IN THE DUST; RABBI SAYS: THESE ARE SERPENTS, WHOSE DOMINION IS ONLY IN THE DUST. (שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מוּזְחָלִין בֶּעָפָר "that they be dragged in the dust"; אֵלּוּ עַכְנָאִים שֶׁאֵין שִׁלְטוֹנָם אֶלָּא בֶּעָפָר "these are serpents, whose dominion is only in the dust"): the crawlers named serpents (Onkelos the same — the measure); no law (DV32-08). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:9 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. "WITHOUT THE SWORD SHALL BEREAVE" (32:25) — FROM HERE THEY SAID: IN TIME OF WAR GATHER IN THE FOOT, IN TIME OF FAMINE SCATTER THE FOOT; "IF I GO OUT INTO THE FIELD, BEHOLD THE SLAIN OF THE SWORD, AND IF I ENTER THE CITY, BEHOLD THE SICKNESSES OF FAMINE" (Jeremiah 14:18), "HE THAT IS IN THE FIELD SHALL DIE BY THE SWORD, AND HE THAT IS IN THE CITY FAMINE AND PESTILENCE SHALL DEVOUR" (Ezekiel 7:15). (בִּשְׁעַת מִלְחָמָה כְּנֹס אֶת הָרֶגֶל "in time of war gather in the foot"; וּבִשְׁעַת רָעָב פַּזֵּר אֶת הָרֶגֶל "in time of famine scatter the foot"): A RULE FROM THE VERSE — "from here they said": the sages' counsel of war and famine (the Babylonian Talmud Bava Kamma 60b the testing shelf, cited); the compile: a rule of conduct with two conditions, the song its source — a candidate for the compile's case table (DV32-08); the Prophets' seats cited, never read. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:10 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. "AND IN THE CHAMBERS TERROR" — HE SAW THE SWORD COMING TO THE MARKET; COULD HE FLEE AND ESCAPE IT, THEN THE CHAMBERS OF HIS HEART BEAT ON HIM AND HE RAISES A GROWTH AND DIES OF IT. (חַדְרֵי לִבּוֹ נוֹקְפִים עָלָיו "the chambers of his heart beat on him"): the chambers the heart's (Onkelos: the terror of death — the measure); no law (DV32-08). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:11 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. ANOTHER WORD: "WITHOUT THE SWORD SHALL BEREAVE" — FOR WHAT THEY DID IN THE STREETS OF JERUSALEM: "FOR ACCORDING TO THE NUMBER OF YOUR CITIES WERE YOUR GODS, O JUDAH, AND ACCORDING TO THE NUMBER OF THE STREETS OF JERUSALEM YOU SET UP ALTARS TO THE SHAMEFUL THING" (Jeremiah 11:13); "AND IN THE CHAMBERS TERROR" — FOR WHAT THEY DID IN THE INNERMOST CHAMBERS: "HAVE YOU SEEN, SON OF MAN, WHAT THE ELDERS OF THE HOUSE OF ISRAEL DO IN THE DARK, EACH IN HIS CHAMBERS OF IMAGERY, SAYING: THE LORD DOES NOT SEE US" (Ezekiel 8:12). (עַל מַה שֶּׁעָשׂוּ בְּחוּצוֹת יְרוּשָׁלִַם "for what they did in the streets of Jerusalem"; עַל מַה שֶּׁעָשׂוּ בְּחַדְרֵי חֲדָרִים "for what they did in the innermost chambers"): the sword and the terror measure for measure — the street's sin and the chamber's; the Prophets' seats cited, never read; no law (DV32-08). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:12 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. "BOTH YOUNG MAN AND VIRGIN" — THESE I MIGHT HAVE SPARED, BUT "THE SUCKLING WITH THE MAN OF GRAY HAIRS"? — "BOTH HUSBAND AND WIFE SHALL BE TAKEN, THE ELDER WITH HIM THAT IS FULL OF DAYS" (Jeremiah 6:11). (הָא וִתְּרָה לְאֵלּוּ "these I might have spared"; אֶלָּא יוֹנֵק עִם אִישׁ שֵׂיבָה "but the suckling with the man of gray hairs"): the four ages read as the sweep no one escapes; the Prophets' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-08). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:13 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. ANOTHER WORD: "BOTH YOUNG MAN (bachur)" — YOU MADE ME STRETCH OUT MY HAND AGAINST MY CHOSEN (bechirai): "AND JOSHUA SON OF NUN, THE SERVANT OF MOSES FROM HIS YOUTH (mibchurav), ANSWERED" (Numbers 11:28). (אַתֶּם גְּרַמְתֶּם לִי לִשְׁלוֹחַ יָד בִּבְחִירַי "you made Me stretch out My hand against My chosen"): the young man read as the chosen by the root (Numbers 11:28 the tape's own entry — a RUN CITATION); no law (DV32-08). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 321:14 — MATERIAL. On 32:23. "BOTH VIRGIN" — IT TEACHES THEY WERE CLEAN OF SIN AS A VIRGIN WHO NEVER TASTED SIN; "SUCKLING" — THEY SUCKED WORDS OF TORAH AS A SUCKLING SUCKS MILK FROM HIS MOTHER'S BREASTS; "WITH THE MAN OF GRAY HAIRS (ish seivah)" — DO NOT READ "MAN OF GRAY HAIRS" BUT "MAN OF THE STUDY HOUSE (ish yeshivah)": ALL OF THEM FIT TO SIT IN THE STUDY HOUSE — "ALL OF THEM MIGHTY MEN, MAKERS OF WAR" (2 Kings 24:16): WHAT MIGHT DO MEN GOING INTO EXILE SHOW, AND WHAT WAR DO MEN BOUND IN FETTERS AND CHAINS MAKE? "MIGHTY" — MIGHTY IN TORAH ("BLESS THE LORD, HIS ANGELS, MIGHTY IN STRENGTH, WHO DO HIS WORD", Psalm 103:20); "MAKERS OF WAR" — WHO GAVE AND TOOK IN THE WAR OF TORAH ("THEREFORE IT IS SAID IN THE BOOK OF THE WARS OF THE LORD", Numbers 21:14); "AND THE CRAFTSMEN AND THE SMITHS A THOUSAND" (2 Kings 24:16) — CRAFTSMAN (charash): ONE SPEAKS AND ALL ARE SILENT (cheresh); SMITH (masger): ALL SIT BEFORE HIM AND LEARN FROM HIM — ONE OPENS AND ONE SHUTS, TO FULFIL "HE SHALL OPEN AND NONE SHALL SHUT, AND SHUT AND NONE SHALL OPEN" (Isaiah 22:22). (מְלַמֵּד שֶׁהָיוּ מְנֻקִּים מִן הַחֵטְא "it teaches they were clean of sin"; אֶלָּא ״אִישׁ יְשִׁיבָה״ "but: man of the study house"; אֵלּוּ גִּבּוֹרֵי תוֹרָה "these are the mighty in Torah"; אֶחָד פּוֹתֵחַ וְאֶחָד סוֹגֵר "one opens and one shuts"): THE EXILES OF JEHOIACHIN AS THE SAGES — the sixth "do not read" (yeshivah for seivah); 2 Kings 24:16's thousand craftsmen and smiths the study house's masters (the Prophets' and the Writings' seats cited, never read; Numbers 21:14 the tape's own entry — a RUN CITATION); the compile: no law (DV32-08). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 322:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:26. "I SAID: I WOULD MAKE AN END OF THEM (af'eihem)" (32:26) — I SAID IN MY ANGER (be'appi): WHERE ARE THEY (ayeh hem)? The head row of piska 322 (אָמַרְתִּי בְּאַפִּי אַיֵּה הֵם "I said in My anger: where are they"): the unique verb split into two words (anger and "where are they" — the translator names the assonance; Onkelos: My anger shall fall upon them — the measure); no law (DV32-09). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 322:2 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in gen_14_adam_line_ledger_2026-08-26.md). On 32:26. "I WOULD MAKE THEIR MEMORY CEASE FROM AMONG MEN (enosh)" — I SAID: LET THEM NOT BE IN THE WORLD; BUT WHAT SHALL I DO TO THEM? (אָמַרְתִּי לֹא יִהְיוּ בָּעוֹלָם "I said: let them not be in the world"; אֲבָל מָה אֶעֱשֶׂה לָהֶם "but what shall I do to them"): the counterfactual opened — the seat that read this row at the Genesis ledger of Adam's line (enosh — the man of Genesis 5:6's name, the translator); no law (DV32-09). REREAD WHOLE (the Genesis ledger gen_14_adam_line_ledger's reading).
- Sifrei Devarim 322:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:26. "IF IT HAD NOT BEEN THE LORD WHO WAS ON OUR SIDE WHEN MAN (adam) ROSE UP AGAINST US" (Psalm 124:1-2). (לוּלֵי ה׳ שֶׁהָיָה לָנוּ בְּקוּם עָלֵינוּ אָדָם "if it had not been the LORD who was for us when man rose up against us"): the answer to "what shall I do" a Psalm verse alone; THE MARKER GARBLED in the export ("Deuteronomy 32" and "Psalms 124:1-2" run together in one parenthesis) — the row is the Psalm's (the Writings' seat cited, never read); no law (DV32-09). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 322:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:26. ANOTHER WORD: I SAID IN MY ANGER, WHERE ARE THEY — "I WILL MAKE THEIR MEMORY CEASE FROM AMONG MEN", LET THEM NOT BE IN THE WORLD; BUT WHAT SHALL I DO — "EXCEPT THE LORD OF HOSTS HAD LEFT US A REMNANT" (Isaiah 1:9); ANOTHER WORD: "I SAID I WOULD MAKE AN END OF THEM" — WERE IT NOT AS IT SAYS "AND HE SAID HE WOULD DESTROY THEM, HAD NOT MOSES HIS CHOSEN STOOD IN THE BREACH BEFORE HIM" (Psalm 106:23). (אֲבָל מָה אֶעֱשֶׂה "but what shall I do"; ״לוּלֵי מֹשֶׁה בְחִירוֹ עָמַד בַּפֶּרֶץ לְפָנָיו״ "had not Moses His chosen stood in the breach before Him"): THE "WERE IT NOT" CHAIN (lulei — 32:27's word) — the remnant and Moses in the breach (Exodus 32:11-14 the tape's own entry — a RUN CITATION; the Prophets' and the Writings' seats cited); the compile: the decree stayed by intercession — the argument of the calf on the tape (DV32-09). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 322:5 — MATERIAL. On 32:26. "WERE IT NOT THAT I DREADED THE ENEMY'S PROVOCATION" (32:27) — WHO CAUSED THEM TO BE PUNISHED BY THESE? THE ANGER OF THE NATIONS GATHERED IN THEIR BELLIES.
