(מִי גָּרַם לָהֶם לִיפָּרַע מֵאֵלּוּ "who caused them to be punished by these"; כַּעַס אֻמּוֹת שֶׁהָיָה כָּנוּס בְּתוֹךְ מֵעֵיהֶם "the anger of the nations that was gathered in their bellies"): the enemy's anger gathered (Onkelos: the enemy's anger is gathered — the measure, the same reading); no law (DV32-09). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 322:6 — MATERIAL. On 32:26. "I DREADED (agur)" — AGUR IS ONLY GATHERED: "THE WORDS OF AGUR SON OF JAKEH" (Proverbs 30:1), "LET DEATH SEIZE UPON THEM, LET THEM GO DOWN ALIVE TO SHEOL, FOR EVILS ARE GATHERED (bimguram) WITHIN THEM" (Psalm 55:16). (אֵין אָגוּר אֶלָּא כָּנוּס "agur is only gathered"): the divine dread read away — the word means gathered (Onkelos the same); the Writings' seats cited, never read; no law (DV32-09). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 322:7 — MATERIAL. On 32:26. "LEST THEIR ADVERSARIES MISJUDGE (yenakkeru)" — IN THE HOUR OF ISRAEL'S DISTRESS THE NATIONS OF THE WORLD ESTRANGE THEM AND MAKE AS IF THEY NEVER KNEW THEM; SO WE FIND THAT WHEN THEY SOUGHT TO FLEE NORTHWARD THEY WERE NOT GATHERED IN BUT HANDED OVER — "THUS SAYS THE LORD: FOR THREE TRANSGRESSIONS OF TYRE, AND FOR FOUR, I WILL NOT TURN IT BACK, BECAUSE THEY DELIVERED UP A WHOLE CAPTIVITY TO EDOM" (Amos 1:9); SOUTHWARD — "FOR THREE TRANSGRESSIONS OF GAZA … THEY CARRIED AWAY A WHOLE CAPTIVITY TO DELIVER THEM TO EDOM" (Amos 1:6); EASTWARD — "FOR THREE TRANSGRESSIONS OF DAMASCUS" (Amos 1:3); WESTWARD — "THE BURDEN UPON ARABIA: IN THE FOREST OF ARABIA SHALL YOU LODGE, O CARAVANS OF DEDANITES" (Isaiah 21:13). (בִּשְׁעַת צָרָתָם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל "in the hour of Israel's distress"; אֻמּוֹת הָעוֹלָם מְנַכְּרִים אוֹתָם "the nations of the world estrange them"; שֶׁבִּקְשׁוּ לִבְרֹחַ כְּלַפֵּי צָפוֹן "they sought to flee northward"): THE FOUR DIRECTIONS CLOSED — Amos 1's three cities and Isaiah's Arabia the four exits barred (the Prophets' seats cited, never read); the verb yenakkeru read as estrange (n-k-r, the stranger); no law (DV32-09). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 322:8 — MATERIAL. On 32:26. IN THE HOUR OF ISRAEL'S PROSPERITY THE NATIONS OF THE WORLD FLATTER THEM AND MAKE THEMSELVES AS BROTHERS — SO ESAU SAID TO JACOB "I HAVE ENOUGH, MY BROTHER, LET WHAT YOU HAVE BE YOURS" (Genesis 33:9), AND HIRAM TO SOLOMON "WHAT CITIES ARE THESE WHICH YOU HAVE GIVEN ME, MY BROTHER?" (1 Kings 9:13); "LEST THEY SAY: OUR HAND IS EXALTED AND NOT THE LORD HAS WROUGHT ALL THIS" — AS THOSE FOOLS SAID "HAVE WE NOT TAKEN KARNAIM BY OUR OWN STRENGTH?" (Amos 6:13). (בִּשְׁעַת טוֹבָתָם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל "in the hour of Israel's prosperity"; וְעוֹשִׂים עַצְמָם כְּאִלּוּ הֵם אַחִים "and make themselves as if they were brothers"; כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמְרוּ אוֹתָם הַשּׁוֹטִים "as those fools said"): the brothers of good times (Genesis 33:9 the tape's own entry — a RUN CITATION; the Prophets' seats cited) and THE ENEMY'S BOAST quoted from Amos; no law (DV32-09). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 322:9 — MATERIAL. On 32:26. "FOR THEY ARE A NATION VOID OF COUNSEL" (32:28) — R. YEHUDAH EXPOUNDS IT OF ISRAEL, R. NEHEMIAH OF THE NATIONS OF THE WORLD. R. YEHUDAH OF ISRAEL: ISRAEL LOST THE GOOD COUNSEL GIVEN THEM, AND COUNSEL IS ONLY TORAH — "COUNSEL IS MINE, AND SOUND WISDOM" (Proverbs 8:14). (רַבִּי יְהוּדָה דּוֹרְשׁוֹ כְּלַפֵּי יִשְׂרָאֵל "R. Yehudah expounds it of Israel"; וְאֵין עֵצָה אֶלָּא תּוֹרָה "and counsel is only Torah"): THE NATION'S IDENTITY DISPUTED — Israel or the nations (the Hebrew's goy both, the translator's note at 311:1); Onkelos leaves it open (the measure); the Writings' seat cited, never read; no law (DV32-09). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 322:10 — MATERIAL. On 32:26. "AND THERE IS NO UNDERSTANDING IN THEM" — NOT ONE OF THEM LOOKS AND SAYS: YESTERDAY ONE OF US CHASED A THOUSAND OF THE NATIONS AND TWO PUT TEN THOUSAND TO FLIGHT, AND NOW ONE OF THE NATIONS CHASES A THOUSAND OF US AND TWO PUT TEN THOUSAND TO FLIGHT — "EXCEPT THEIR ROCK HAD SOLD THEM" (32:30). (אֶמֶשׁ אֶחָד מִמֶּנּוּ רוֹדֵף מִן הָאֻמּוֹת אֶלֶף "yesterday one of us chased a thousand of the nations"; אִם לֹא כִּי צוּרָם מְכָרָם "except their Rock had sold them"): THE JOINED THOUSAND READ BY THE SPINE — one, a thousand, two, ten thousand: four numbers, the proverb of 32:30 turned forward and back (the compile's guard against the parser's [1, 1002] confirmed by the shelf's own arithmetic — DV32-10); R. Yehudah's reading closed. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 322:11 — MATERIAL. On 32:26. R. NEHEMIAH EXPOUNDS IT OF THE NATIONS: THE NATIONS LOST THE SEVEN COMMANDMENTS I GAVE THEM. (אִבְּדוּ הָאֻמּוֹת שֶׁבַע מִצְוֹת שֶׁנָּתַתִּי לָהֶם "the nations lost the seven commandments I gave them"): THE SEVEN COMMANDMENTS of the nations named in the spine (the Tosefta Avodah Zarah 8:4 the testing shelf, cited — the Noahide seven; the compile: a law set on the nations, its seat outside the tape's Israel ledger, cited); no law here (DV32-09). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 322:12 — MATERIAL. On 32:26. "AND THERE IS NO UNDERSTANDING IN THEM" — NOT ONE OF THEM LOOKS AND SAYS: NOW ONE OF US CHASES A THOUSAND OF ISRAEL AND TWO PUT TEN THOUSAND TO FLIGHT; IN THE DAYS OF THE MESSIAH ONE OF ISRAEL WILL CHASE A THOUSAND OF US AND TWO PUT TEN THOUSAND TO FLIGHT — "EXCEPT THEIR ROCK HAD SOLD THEM". IT HAPPENED IN THE WAR IN JUDAH THAT A DECURION RAN AFTER AN ISRAELITE ON HORSEBACK TO KILL HIM AND DID NOT OVERTAKE HIM; BEFORE HE OVERTOOK HIM A SNAKE CAME OUT AND BIT HIM ON THE HEEL; HE SAID TO HIM: DO NOT THINK TO SAY THAT BECAUSE WE ARE MIGHTY THEY WERE GIVEN INTO OUR HAND — "EXCEPT THEIR ROCK HAD SOLD THEM". (לִימוֹת הַמָּשִׁיחַ אֶחָד מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל רוֹדֵף מִמֶּנּוּ אֶלֶף "in the days of the Messiah one of Israel will chase a thousand of us"; מַעֲשֶׂה בְּפוּלְמוֹס שֶׁבִּיהוּדָה "it happened in the war in Judah"; יָצָא נָחָשׁ וְנָשַׁךְ לוֹ עַל עֲקֵבוֹ "a snake came out and bit him on the heel"): the last row of the piska — R. Nehemiah's reading closed by the Roman's own confession (the war in Judah — Bar Kokhba's, the translator); 32:30's proverb the third time in the piska; no law (DV32-09, DV32-10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 323:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_04_vaetchanan_2026-09-16.md). On 32:29. "HAD THEY BEEN WISE THEY WOULD UNDERSTAND THIS" (32:29) — HAD ISRAEL LOOKED INTO THE WORDS OF TORAH THAT I GAVE THEM, NO NATION OR KINGDOM WOULD HAVE RULED THEM; AND "THIS" IS ONLY TORAH: "AND THIS IS THE TORAH WHICH MOSES SET" (4:44). ANOTHER WORD: HAD ISRAEL LOOKED INTO WHAT JACOB THEIR FATHER SAID TO THEM, NO NATION OR KINGDOM WOULD HAVE RULED THEM — AND WHAT DID HE SAY TO THEM? ACCEPT UPON YOU THE KINGDOM OF HEAVEN, OUTDO ONE ANOTHER IN THE FEAR OF HEAVEN, AND DEAL WITH ONE ANOTHER IN DEEDS OF KINDNESS. The head row of piska 323 (אִלּוּ הִסְתַּכְּלוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּדִבְרֵי תוֹרָה "had Israel looked into the words of Torah"; וְאֵין ״זֹאת״ אֶלָּא תּוֹרָה "and this is only Torah"; קַבְּלוּ עֲלֵיכֶם מַלְכוּת שָׁמַיִם "accept upon you the kingdom of Heaven"): THE COUNTERFACTUAL READ AS A CONDITION — the Torah looked into, no nation rules; the identity "this = the Torah" by 4:44 (chapter 4's own row, the ledger deu_04 — the book's "this is the Torah" the same word); Jacob's charge to his sons (the kingdom of Heaven, the fear, kindness — Genesis 49's deathbed on the tape, a RUN CITATION; the spine's wording its own); the compile: no law — the song's "had they been wise" the condition whose failure 32:30 states (DV32-10). REREAD WHOLE (chapter 4's reading — 4:44; the ledger deu_04).
- Sifrei Devarim 323:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:29. "HOW SHOULD ONE CHASE A THOUSAND" (32:30) — IF YOU DID NOT DO THE TORAH, HOW DO I DO YOUR PROMISE? FOR YOU ASKED THAT ONE OF YOU CHASE A THOUSAND OF THE NATIONS AND TWO PUT TEN THOUSAND TO FLIGHT — AND NOW ONE OF THE NATIONS CHASES A THOUSAND OF YOU AND TWO OF THEM PUT TEN THOUSAND TO FLIGHT. (אִם לֹא עֲשִׂיתֶם אֶת הַתּוֹרָה "if you did not do the Torah"; וְעַכְשָׁיו אֶחָד מִן הָאֻמּוֹת רוֹדֵף אֶלֶף מִכֶּם "and now one of the nations chases a thousand of you"): THE PROMISE CONDITIONED AND REVERSED — the four numbers (one, a thousand, two, ten thousand) turned as 322:10 and 322:12 turned them, THE JOINED-THOUSAND GUARD confirmed a third time (the parser's [1, 1002] refused); Leviticus 26:8's "five chase a hundred" the tape's promise (a RUN CITATION), Joshua 23:10 the Prophets' seat cited never read; the compile: the condition's two arms — the Torah done, the promise; undone, the reversal (DV32-10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 323:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:29. "EXCEPT THEIR ROCK HAD SOLD THEM AND THE LORD HAD DELIVERED THEM UP" — I DO NOT DELIVER YOU UP BY MYSELF BUT BY OTHERS; IT ONCE HAPPENED THAT THE FLIES DELIVERED THEM UP IN JUDAH. R. CHANINA OF TIBEIN SAYS: A PARABLE OF ONE WHO SAID TO HIS FELLOW, I SELL YOU A SLAVE FOR CASH — BUT I AM NOT SO: I SELL AND I DELIVER UP AT ONCE. [ANOTHER WORD: "AND THE LORD HAD DELIVERED THEM UP" — I HAND] YOU OVER AS UNCLEAN INTO THE HANDS OF THE CLEAN — OR [IS IT] AS CLEAN INTO THE HANDS OF THE UNCLEAN? ONLY THE UNCLEAN IS SHUT UP: "AND THE PRIEST SHALL SHUT UP THE PLAGUE SEVEN DAYS" (Leviticus 13:4). (אֵינִי מַסְגִּיר אֶתְכֶם עַל יְדֵי עַצְמִי "I do not deliver you up by myself"; שֶׁהַזְּבוּבִים מָסְרוּ אוֹתָם בִּיהוּדָה "that the flies delivered them up in Judah"; הָא אֵין מַסְגִּירִים אֶלָּא טָמֵא "only the unclean is shut up"): "DELIVERED UP" (hisgir, "shut up") READ BY THE LEPER'S LAW — the priest's seven days (Leviticus 13:4, the leprosy span compiled at Leviticus — a REFERENCE): the one delivered up is the unclean one; the surrogates (the flies in Judah — the translator finds no source); the parable's slave sold and handed over at once; the brackets the editor's supplement (the translator, after Finkelstein); the compile: no law — the punishment's agent named (DV32-10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 323:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:29. "FOR THEIR ROCK IS NOT AS OUR ROCK" (32:31) — NOT AS THE STRENGTH YOU GIVE US DO YOU GIVE THEM: WHEN YOU GIVE US THE STRENGTH WE DEAL WITH THEM BY THE MEASURE OF MERCY, AND WHEN YOU GIVE THEM THE STRENGTH THEY DEAL WITH US BY THE MEASURE OF CRUELTY — THEY KILL OF US, BURN OF US, CRUCIFY OF US. (אָנוּ מִתְנַהֲגִים עִמָּהֶם בְּמִדַּת רַחֲמִים "we deal with them by the measure of mercy"; הוֹרְגִים מִמֶּנּוּ וְשׂוֹרְפִים מִמֶּנּוּ וְצוֹלְבִים מִמֶּנּוּ "they kill of us, burn of us, crucify of us"): ISRAEL'S PLEA — "their rock" the nations' strength, the two measures (mercy, cruelty) the difference; the cross Rome's; the compile: no law (DV32-10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 323:5 — MATERIAL. On 32:29. "AND OUR ENEMIES ARE JUDGES" (32:31) — YOU WROTE IN THE TORAH THAT AN ENEMY NEITHER JUDGES NOR TESTIFIES: "AND HE WAS NOT HIS ENEMY" (Numbers 35:23) — HE MAY TESTIFY OF HIM; "NOR SOUGHT HIS HARM" — HE MAY JUDGE HIM; AND YOU HAVE SET OVER US ENEMIES AS WITNESSES AND JUDGES. (שֶׁאוֹיֵב לֹא דָּן וְלֹא מֵעִיד "that an enemy neither judges nor testifies"; וְאַתָּה מִנִּיתָ עָלֵינוּ אוֹיְבִים עֵדִים וְדַיָּנִים "and You set over us enemies as witnesses and judges"): THE COURT LAW READ INTO THE SONG A THIRD TIME (306:9 the two witnesses, 306:12 the agreeing witnesses, here THE ENEMY DISQUALIFIED) — Numbers 35:23's manslayer clause turned into the rule that an enemy neither judges nor testifies (Mishnah Sanhedrin 3:5 the answer sheet's row — the testing shelf not opened; the compiled manslayer span at Numbers a REFERENCE); the plea: the rule broken by the Judge Himself; the compile: a disqualification the court runner carries — the song's use a case (DV32-10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 323:6 — MATERIAL. On 32:29. "FOR THEIR VINE IS OF THE VINE OF SODOM" (32:32) — R. YEHUDAH EXPOUNDS IT OF ISRAEL, R. NECHEMIAH EXPOUNDS IT OF THE NATIONS OF THE WORLD. (רַבִּי יְהוּדָה דּוֹרְשׁוֹ כְּלַפֵּי יִשְׂרָאֵל "R. Yehudah expounds it of Israel"; רַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה דּוֹרְשׁוֹ כְּלַפֵּי אֻמּוֹת הָעוֹלָם "R. Nechemiah expounds it of the nations of the world"): THE TWO-READING FRAME OF 322 RETURNS (322:4 and 322:8 on the nation void of counsel; here on the vine); the rows 323:7-11 R. Yehudah's, 323:12-13 R. Nechemiah's (the translator: mirror images); no law (DV32-10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 323:7 — MATERIAL. On 32:29. R. YEHUDAH SAYS: ARE YOU OF THE VINE OF SODOM, OR OF THE PLANTING OF GOMORRAH? ARE YOU NOT OF A HOLY PLANTING: "AND I PLANTED YOU A NOBLE VINE, WHOLLY A SEED OF TRUTH" (Jeremiah 2:21). (וַהֲלֹא אֵי אַתֶּם אֶלָּא מִמַּטַּע קֹדֶשׁ "are you not of a holy planting"): the verse of Israel — a question answered by Jeremiah's noble vine (the Prophets' seat cited, never read); no law (DV32-10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 323:8 — MATERIAL. On 32:29. "THEIR GRAPES ARE GRAPES OF GALL" (32:32) — YOU ARE THE SONS OF THE FIRST ADAM, ON WHOM I DECREED DEATH, AND ON HIS OFFSPRING WHO COME AFTER HIM TO THE END OF ALL GENERATIONS. (בָּנָיו שֶׁל אָדָם הָרִאשׁוֹן אַתֶּם "you are the sons of the first Adam"; שֶׁקָּנַסְתִּי עָלָיו מִיתָה "on whom I decreed death"): GALL (rosh, "poison") READ BY THE FIRST (rishon) ADAM — the assonance the translator notes; death decreed on Adam and his line (Genesis 3:19 on the tape — a RUN CITATION); no law (DV32-10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 323:9 — MATERIAL. On 32:29. "CLUSTERS OF BITTERNESS ARE THEIRS" (32:32) — THE GREAT AMONG YOU, THEIR BITTERNESS IS SPREAD IN THEM LIKE A CLUSTER; AND "CLUSTER" IS ONLY A GREAT MAN: "THERE IS NO CLUSTER TO EAT, MY SOUL DESIRED THE FIRST-RIPE FIG" (Micah 7:1). (וְאֵין אֶשְׁכּוֹל אֶלָּא גָּדוֹל "and cluster is only a great man"): the cluster (eshkol, "a bunch of grapes") the leader — Mishnah Sotah 9:9's usage (the translator; the testing shelf not opened); no law (DV32-10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 323:10 — MATERIAL. On 32:29. "THEIR WINE IS THE VENOM OF SERPENTS" (32:33) — THE PIOUS AND THE HEAVEN-FEARING AMONG YOU, THEIR VENOM IS AS SERPENTS'; "AND THE CRUEL POISON OF ASPS" — THAT THE HEADS AMONG YOU ARE AS THIS ASP THAT IS CRUEL. (הַחֲסִידִים וְיִרְאֵי שָׁמַיִם שֶׁבָּכֶם חֲמָתָם כְּתַנִּינִים "the pious and heaven-fearing among you, their venom as serpents"): R. Yehudah's reading continued — the venom Israel's own pious, the cruelty its heads; no law (DV32-10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 323:11 — MATERIAL. On 32:29. ANOTHER WORD: "THEIR WINE IS THE VENOM OF SERPENTS" — THAT THE MODERATE AND THE SIN-FEARING AMONG YOU, THEIR VENOM IS AS SERPENTS'; "AND THE CRUEL POISON OF ASPS" — THE HEADS AMONG YOU ARE AS THIS ASP THAT IS CRUEL. (שֶׁהַמְּתוּנִים וְיִרְאֵי חֵטְא שֶׁבָּכֶם "the moderate and sin-fearing among you"): the doublet of 323:10 with "moderate and sin-fearing" for "pious and Heaven-fearing"; no law (DV32-10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 323:12 — MATERIAL. On 32:29. R. NECHEMIAH EXPOUNDS IT OF THE NATIONS OF THE WORLD: SURELY YOU ARE OF THE VINE OF SODOM, AND OF THE PLANTING OF GOMORRAH YOU ARE; YOU ARE THE DISCIPLES OF THE PRIMEVAL SERPENT WHO LED ADAM AND EVE ASTRAY. (תַּלְמִידָיו שֶׁל נָחָשׁ הַקַּדְמוֹנִי אַתֶּם "you are the disciples of the primeval serpent"): the nations — the serpent's disciples (Genesis 3's serpent on the tape, a RUN CITATION); no law (DV32-10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 323:13 — MATERIAL. On 32:29. "CLUSTERS OF BITTERNESS ARE THEIRS" — THAT THE GREAT AMONG YOU, THEIR BITTERNESS IS SPREAD IN THEM LIKE A SERPENT'S; "CLUSTERS OF BITTERNESS ARE THEIRS" — AND "CLUSTER" IS ONLY A GREAT MAN: "THERE IS NO CLUSTER TO EAT" (Micah 7:1). (מָרָתָם פְּרוּסָה בָהֶם כְּנָחָשׁ "their bitterness spread in them like a serpent"): the last row of the piska — R. Nechemiah's mirror of 323:9; 32:33's venom not reached in his reading (the export ends the piska here); no law (DV32-10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 324:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:34. "IS NOT THIS LAID UP IN STORE WITH ME" (32:34) — R. ELIEZER SON OF R. YOSE THE GALILEAN SAYS: A CUP THAT WAS STORED AND LACKING — MAY IT BE FADED? THE TEACHING SAYS "FOAMING" (Psalm 75:9); MAY IT BE ONLY HALF? THE TEACHING SAYS "FULL OF MIXTURE"; MAY IT NOT LACK EVEN ONE DROP? THE TEACHING SAYS "AND HE POURED OUT OF THIS" — OF THAT DROP DRANK THE GENERATION OF THE FLOOD, THE GENERATION OF THE DISPERSION, THE MEN OF SODOM, PHARAOH AND ALL HIS HOST, SISERA AND ALL HIS MULTITUDE, SENNACHERIB AND ALL HIS WINGS, NEBUCHADNEZZAR AND ALL HIS HOST; AND OF THAT DROP ALL WHO COME INTO THE WORLD ARE TO DRINK TO THE END OF ALL GENERATIONS. AND SO IT SAYS "AND THE LORD OF HOSTS WILL MAKE FOR ALL PEOPLES IN THIS MOUNTAIN A FEAST OF FAT THINGS, A FEAST OF LEES" (Isaiah 25:6) — FAT THINGS THAT ARE OF USE AND LEES THAT ARE NOT? THE TEACHING SAYS "FAT THINGS FULL OF MARROW, LEES WELL REFINED" — FAT THINGS WITH NOTHING IN THEM BUT DREGS. AND SO IT SAYS "BABYLON IS A GOLDEN CUP IN THE LORD'S HAND THAT MAKES ALL THE EARTH DRUNK" (Jeremiah 51:7) — AS GOLD BROKEN HAS A REMEDY, SO WHEN THE PUNISHMENT CEASES FROM THE NATIONS IT WILL RETURN TO THEM; AND WHEN IT REACHES ISRAEL WHAT DOES IT SAY? "YOU SHALL DRINK IT AND DRAIN IT AND GNAW ITS SHERDS" (Ezekiel 23:34) — AS EARTHENWARE BROKEN HAS NO REMEDY, SO WHEN THE PUNISHMENT CEASES FROM ISRAEL IT WILL NOT RETURN TO THEM. The head row of piska 324 (כּוֹס שֶׁהָיָה כָּמוּס וּמְחֻסָּר "a cup that was stored and lacking"; מֵאוֹתָהּ טִפָּה שָׁתוּ מִמֶּנָּה דּוֹר הַמַּבּוּל "of that drop drank the generation of the Flood"; כָּךְ כְּשֶׁתִּפָּסֵק הַפֻּרְעָנוּת מִן הָאֻמּוֹת "so when the punishment ceases from the nations"; אֵין עֲתִידָה לַחֲזֹר לָהֶם "it will not return to them"): THE STORE IS A CUP — Psalm 75:9 read clause by clause (foaming, full, poured); the seven drinkers (the Flood, the Dispersion, Sodom, Pharaoh on the tape — RUN CITATIONS; Sisera, Sennacherib, Nebuchadnezzar the Prophets' seats cited); the gold cup mended and the earthen cup not — the punishment returning to the nations and not to Israel; the Hebrew "stored" (kamus) against "mixture" (mesekh) the translator's assonance; the compile: no law — the store's content and its clock (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 324:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:34. "SEALED UP IN MY TREASURIES" (32:34) — AS THIS TREASURY IS SEALED AND GROWS NO FRUIT, SO THE DEEDS OF THE WICKED GROW NO FRUIT; IF YOU SAID SO THEY WOULD DESTROY THE WORLD — "WOE TO THE WICKED, EVIL, FOR THE RECOMPENSE OF HIS HANDS SHALL BE DONE TO HIM" (Isaiah 3:11); BUT THE DEEDS OF THE RIGHTEOUS MAKE FRUIT AND FRUIT OF FRUIT — "SAY OF THE RIGHTEOUS THAT IT IS WELL, FOR THEY SHALL EAT THE FRUIT OF THEIR DOINGS" (Isaiah 3:10). (כָּךְ מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם שֶׁל רְשָׁעִים אֵין מְגַדְּלִים פֵּרוֹת "so the deeds of the wicked grow no fruit"; עוֹשִׂים פֵּרוֹת וּפֵרֵי פֵרוֹת "make fruit and fruit of fruit"): THE FRUIT OF DEEDS — the wicked's fruitless, the righteous's bearing fruit of fruit (the translator: Avot deRabbi Natan A 40, Tosefta Peah 1:3 — the testing shelf not opened); no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 324:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:34. ANOTHER WORD: AS THIS TREASURY IS SEALED AND LACKS NOTHING, SO THE RIGHTEOUS TOOK NOTHING OF WHAT IS THEIRS IN THIS WORLD; WHENCE THAT THE RIGHTEOUS TOOK NOTHING OF THEIRS IN THIS WORLD? "HOW GREAT IS YOUR GOODNESS WHICH YOU HAVE LAID UP FOR THOSE WHO FEAR YOU" (Psalm 31:20); AND WHENCE THAT THE WICKED TOOK NOTHING OF THEIRS IN THIS WORLD? "IS NOT THIS LAID UP IN STORE WITH ME"; WHEN DO THESE AND THOSE TAKE? TOMORROW, WHEN REDEMPTION COMES TO ISRAEL — "VENGEANCE IS MINE AND RECOMPENSE". (צַדִּיקִים לֹא נָטְלוּ כְּלוּם "the righteous took nothing"; לְמָחָר כְּשֶׁתָּבוֹא גְּאֻלָּה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל "tomorrow when redemption comes to Israel"): BOTH REWARDS DEFERRED — the righteous's and the wicked's stores paid at "tomorrow", the redemption's day (32:35 read as the payment's clock); the compile: no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 325:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:35. "VENGEANCE IS MINE AND RECOMPENSE" (32:35) — I EXACT IT FROM THEM MYSELF, NOT BY AN ANGEL AND NOT BY A MESSENGER, AS IN THE MATTER SAID "NOW GO AND I WILL SEND YOU TO PHARAOH" (Exodus 3:10), AND IT SAYS "AND THE ANGEL OF THE LORD WENT OUT AND SMOTE IN THE CAMP OF ASSYRIA" (2 Kings 19:35). The head row of piska 325 (אֲנִי נִפְרָע מֵהֶם בְּעַצְמִי "I exact it from them Myself"; לֹא עַל יְדֵי מַלְאָךְ וְלֹא עַל יְדֵי "not by an angel and not by"): "MYSELF" AGAINST THE ANGEL AND THE MESSENGER — the two counter-cases named (Moses sent to Pharaoh, Exodus 3:10 on the tape a RUN CITATION; the angel smiting Assyria, the Prophets' seat cited): the vengeance of the song by no agent — the Passover night's formula in the spine's own words; the compile: no law — the agent of 32:35 (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 325:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:35. ANOTHER WORD: "VENGEANCE IS MINE AND I WILL PAY" IS NOT WRITTEN HERE, BUT "VENGEANCE IS MINE AND RECOMPENSE" — I PAY THE REWARD OF THE DEEDS THAT THEIR FATHERS DID BEFORE ME IN THIS WORLD; AND SO IT SAYS "I WILL NOT KEEP SILENCE, BUT I HAVE RECOMPENSED" (Isaiah 65:6), AND "I WILL RECOMPENSE FIRST THE DOUBLE OF THEIR INIQUITY AND THEIR SIN" (Jeremiah 16:18). (מְשַׁלֵּם אֲנִי שְׂכַר מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם שֶׁעָשׂוּ אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם "I pay the reward of the deeds their fathers did"): THE "IS NOT WRITTEN" FORM on the noun (shillem, "recompense") against the verb — the fathers' deeds repaid to the sons (the root sh-l-m, "to pay, make whole", the translator's thread to 336:2); no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 325:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:35. "AT THE TIME THEIR FOOT SLIPS" (32:35) — AS IN THE MATTER SAID "THE FOOT SHALL TREAD IT DOWN, THE FEET OF THE POOR, THE STEPS OF THE NEEDY" (Isaiah 26:6); "FOR THE DAY OF THEIR CALAMITY IS NEAR" — R. YOSE SAID: IF OF THOSE OF WHOM IT IS SAID "THE DAY OF THEIR CALAMITY IS NEAR" THE PUNISHMENTS COME CREEPING, THOSE OF WHOM IT IS SAID "AFTER MANY DAYS SHALL THEY BE VISITED" (Isaiah 24:22) — HOW MUCH MORE. (הֲרֵי פֻּרְעָנֻיּוֹת מְמַשְׁמְשׁוֹת וּבָאוֹת "the punishments come creeping"; עַל אַחַת כַּמָּה וְכַמָּה "how much more"): R. Yose's argument from the lighter to the weightier on the calamity's nearness (the Prophets' seats cited); no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 325:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:35. "AND THE THINGS TO COME UPON THEM MAKE HASTE" (32:35) — WHEN THE HOLY ONE BRINGS PUNISHMENT ON THE NATIONS HE SHAKES THE WORLD UPON THEM: "THEY FLY AS THE EAGLE THAT HASTES TO EAT" (Habakkuk 1:8), AND "THAT SAY: LET HIM MAKE SPEED, LET HIM HASTEN HIS WORK, THAT WE MAY SEE IT" (Isaiah 5:19); AND WHEN THE HOLY ONE BRINGS CHASTISEMENTS ON ISRAEL HE DOES NOT BRING THEM AT ONCE BUT WAITS — HOW? HE HANDS THEM TO THE FOUR KINGDOMS TO BE ENSLAVED BY THEM; AND SO IT SAYS "FEAR NOT, FOR I AM WITH YOU, SAYS THE LORD, TO SAVE YOU" (Jeremiah 1:8). (מַרְעִישׁ עֲלֵיהֶם אֶת הָעוֹלָם "He shakes the world upon them"; מוֹסְרָם לְאַרְבַּע מַלְכֻיּוֹת "He hands them to the four kingdoms"): THE TWO SPEEDS — haste for the nations, delay for Israel by the four kingdoms (320:4's kingdoms again); the last row of the piska; the translator: the editions vary on Jeremiah 1:8 and 30:11; no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 326:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in gen_15_flood_prologue_2026-08-26.md). On 32:36. "FOR THE LORD WILL JUDGE HIS PEOPLE" (32:36) — WHEN THE HOLY ONE JUDGES THE NATIONS IT IS JOY BEFORE HIM, AS IT IS SAID "FOR THE LORD WILL JUDGE HIS PEOPLE"; AND WHEN THE HOLY ONE JUDGES ISRAEL IT IS, AS IT WERE, REGRET BEFORE HIM, AS IT IS SAID "AND REPENT HIMSELF FOR HIS SERVANTS" — AND "REPENTING" IS ONLY REGRET: "FOR I REPENT THAT I MADE THEM" (Genesis 6:7), AND "I REPENT THAT I MADE SAUL KING" (1 Samuel 15:11). The head row of piska 326 (שִׂמְחָה הִיא לְפָנָיו "it is joy before Him"; כִּבְיָכוֹל תְּהוּת הִיא לְפָנָיו "as it were, regret before Him"; וְאֵין נֶחָמָה אֶלָּא תְּהוּת "and repenting is only regret"): THE VERSE PSALM 135:14 QUOTES WHOLE (the measure's find; the Writings' seat cited never read) read as two judgments — the nations' with joy, Israel's with regret; "repent" (nicham) fixed by Genesis 6:7's "I repent that I made them" — the flood prologue's own word, the Genesis ledger's row; the compile: no law — the verdict's two moods (DV32-11). REREAD WHOLE (the Genesis ledger gen_15_flood_prologue — Genesis 6:7).
- Sifrei Devarim 326:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:36. "WHEN HE SEES THAT THEIR POWER IS GONE" (32:36) — WHEN HE SEES THEIR DESTRUCTION IN THE CAPTIVITY IN WHICH THEY ALL WENT. (כְּשֶׁרוֹאֶה כְּלָיָה שֶׁלָּהֶם עַל הַשִּׁבְיָה "when He sees their destruction in the captivity"): "their power is gone" (azlat yad, "the hand is gone") read as the captivity's annihilation (the translator's assonances: gone / annihilation / all of them); no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 326:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:36. ANOTHER WORD: "WHEN HE SEES" — WHEN THEY HAVE DESPAIRED OF THE REDEMPTION. (כְּשֶׁנִּתְיָאֲשׁוּ מִן הַגְּאֻלָּה "when they have despaired of the redemption"): the second sign — despair; no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 326:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:36. ANOTHER WORD: "THAT THEIR POWER IS GONE AND THERE IS NONE SHUT UP OR LEFT" — WHEN HE SEES THAT THE SMALLEST COIN IS GONE FROM THE PURSE, AS IT IS SAID "AND WHEN THE BREAKING OF THE POWER OF THE HOLY PEOPLE IS ENDED, ALL THESE SHALL BE FINISHED" (Daniel 12:7). ANOTHER WORD: "WHEN HE SEES THAT THEIR POWER IS GONE" — WHEN HE SEES THAT THERE ARE NONE AMONG THEM WHO SEEK MERCY FOR THEM LIKE MOSES, AS IT IS SAID "AND HE SAID HE WOULD DESTROY THEM, HAD NOT MOSES HIS CHOSEN" (Psalm 106:23). (כְּשֶׁרוֹאֶה שֶׁתִּכְלֶה פְּרוּטָה מִן הַכִּיס "when He sees that the smallest coin is gone from the purse"; שֶׁמְּבַקְשִׁים עֲלֵיהֶם רַחֲמִים כְּמֹשֶׁה "who seek mercy for them like Moses"): the third and fourth signs — the last coin (perutah, "the smallest coin") gone, no one to plead like Moses (the calf's intercession, Exodus 32:11 on the tape — a RUN CITATION; Psalm 106:23 the Writings' seat cited); no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 326:5 — MATERIAL. On 32:36. ANOTHER WORD: "WHEN HE SEES" — WHEN HE SEES THAT THERE ARE NONE AMONG THEM WHO SEEK MERCY FOR THEM LIKE AARON, AS IT IS SAID "AND HE STOOD BETWEEN THE DEAD AND THE LIVING, AND THE PLAGUE WAS STAYED" (Numbers 17:13). (רַחֲמִים כְּאַהֲרֹן "mercy like Aaron"): Aaron's incense between the dead and the living (Numbers 17:13 on the tape — a RUN CITATION); no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 326:6 — MATERIAL. On 32:36. ANOTHER WORD: "WHEN HE SEES" — WHEN HE SEES THAT THERE ARE NONE AMONG THEM WHO SEEK MERCY FOR THEM LIKE PHINEHAS, AS IT IS SAID "THEN STOOD UP PHINEHAS AND PRAYED" (Psalm 106:30). (רַחֲמִים כְּפִינְחָס "mercy like Phinehas"): Phinehas's act (Numbers 25:7-8 on the tape — a RUN CITATION) read as prayer by the Psalm's "and prayed" (vayefalel; the Writings' seat cited); no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 326:7 — MATERIAL. On 32:36. ANOTHER WORD: "WHEN HE SEES THAT THEIR POWER IS GONE AND THERE IS NONE SHUT UP OR LEFT" — NONE SHUT UP AND NONE LEFT, AND THERE IS NO HELPER FOR ISRAEL. (וְאֵין עוֹזֵר לְיִשְׂרָאֵל "and there is no helper for Israel"): "none" (efes) doubled — the last row of the piska; no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 327:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:37. "AND HE SHALL SAY: WHERE ARE THEIR GODS" (32:37) — R. YEHUDAH EXPOUNDS IT OF ISRAEL, AND R. NECHEMIAH EXPOUNDS IT OF THE NATIONS OF THE WORLD. The head row of piska 327 (וְרַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה דּוֹרְשׁוֹ כְּלַפֵּי אֻמּוֹת הָעוֹלָם "and R. Nechemiah expounds it of the nations of the world"): the two-reading frame a third time (322, 323, 327); no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 327:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:37. R. YEHUDAH SAYS: ISRAEL ARE TO SAY TO THE NATIONS OF THE WORLD: WHERE ARE YOUR CONSULS AND YOUR GOVERNORS? (הֵיכָן הַפִּיטָקִים וְהֶגְמוֹנִים שֶׁלָּכֶם "where are your consuls and governors"): THE ROMAN LEXICON AGAIN (317:5's officers) — the last row of the piska: R. Yehudah's reading alone stands here, R. Nechemiah's follows as 328:2 (Titus); no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 328:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:35. "WHO ATE THE FAT OF THEIR SACRIFICES" (32:38) — THAT WE GAVE THEM PROVISIONS AND MADE THEM DONATIVES AND BROUGHT THEM UP SALARIES. The head row of piska 328 (שֶׁהָיִינוּ נוֹתְנִים לָהֶם אַפְסַנְיוֹת "that we gave them provisions"): THE MISPRINTED HEAD — the Hebrew marker reads 32:35 while the words are 32:38's (the English true; the row seated by its words — sitting 19's lesson 1, the ink's first-pass fall); the army's allowances in Hebrew letters (the translator: Rome's annona, donativa and salaria — "provisions, donatives, salt-money"); the sacrifices' fat the tax; no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 328:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:35. "LET THEM RISE UP AND HELP YOU" (32:38) — "LET THEM RISE UP AND HELP YOU" WITH THE FULL PLURAL IS NOT WRITTEN HERE, BUT "LET THEM RISE UP AND HELP YOU" WITH THE SUFFIX. R. NECHEMIAH SAYS: THIS IS THE WICKED TITUS, THE SON OF VESPASIAN'S WIFE, WHO ENTERED THE HOLY OF HOLIES AND CUT THE TWO CURTAINS WITH A SWORD AND SAID: IF HE IS GOD, LET HIM COME AND PROTEST. (זֶה טִיטוּס הָרָשָׁע "this is the wicked Titus"; אִם אֱלוֹהַּ הוּא יָבוֹא וְיִמְחֶה "if He is God let Him come and protest"): THE WRITTEN FORM'S DEFECTIVE SUFFIX (the translator: the letter vav omitted, a singular "let Him help you" possible — the ketiv the parser's form); Titus in the sanctuary (the translator: Avot deRabbi Natan B 7; the testing shelf not opened); no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 328:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:35. "WHO ATE THE FAT OF THEIR SACRIFICES" — HE SAID: MOSES LED THESE ASTRAY AND SAID TO THEM, BUILD YOU AN ALTAR AND OFFER ON IT BURNT OFFERINGS AND POUR ON IT LIBATIONS, AS IN THE MATTER SAID "THE ONE LAMB YOU SHALL OFFER IN THE MORNING AND THE SECOND LAMB YOU SHALL OFFER AT DUSK" (Numbers 28:4). (הַלָּלוּ מֹשֶׁה הִטְעָם "Moses led these astray"; בְּנוּ לָכֶם מִזְבֵּחַ "build you an altar"): the speaker unnamed in the Hebrew (the translator follows Hammer and Neusner: Titus); the daily offering (Numbers 28:4, compiled at Numbers — a REFERENCE) the mocker's example; no law (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 328:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:35. "LET THEM RISE UP AND HELP YOU, LET THEM BE YOUR PROTECTION" (32:38) — FOR EVERYTHING THE HOLY ONE PARDONS; FOR THE PROFANATION OF THE NAME HE EXACTS AT ONCE. (עַל הַכֹּל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מוֹחֵל "for everything the Holy One pardons"; עַל חִלּוּל הַשֵּׁם פּוֹרֵעַ מִיָּד "for the profanation of the Name He exacts at once"): A RULE FROM THE VERSE — the profanation of the Name punished at once, all else pardoned (the last row of the piska; Yoma 86a's divisions the testing shelf, not opened); the compile: a status the daemon may write — the Name profaned, the exaction immediate (DV32-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 329:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:39. "SEE NOW THAT I, I AM HE" (32:39) — THIS IS THE ANSWER TO THOSE WHO SAY THERE IS NO AUTHORITY IN HEAVEN; TO ONE WHO SAYS THERE ARE TWO AUTHORITIES IN HEAVEN THEY ANSWER AND SAY: IS IT NOT ALREADY WRITTEN "AND THERE IS NO GOD WITH ME"? OR IS IT AS ONE WHO HAS NO POWER TO KILL OR TO MAKE ALIVE, TO DO EVIL OR TO DO GOOD? THE TEACHING SAYS "SEE NOW THAT I, I AM HE; I KILL AND I MAKE ALIVE", AND IT SAYS "THUS SAYS THE LORD, THE KING OF ISRAEL AND HIS REDEEMER, THE LORD OF HOSTS: I AM THE FIRST AND I AM THE LAST, AND BESIDE ME THERE IS NO GOD" (Isaiah 44:6). The head row of piska 329 (זוֹ תְּשׁוּבָה לָאוֹמְרִים אֵין רָשׁוּת בַּשָּׁמַיִם "this is the answer to those who say there is no authority in Heaven"; הָאוֹמֵר שְׁתֵּי רָשׁוּיוֹת בַּשָּׁמַיִם "one who says there are two authorities in Heaven"): THE TWO POWERS REFUTED by the doubled "I" and by "no god with Me" (the translator: Mekhilta Shirah 4's parallel); the compile: the unity the tape carries from 6:4 — a status reaffirmed, no new law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 329:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:39. ANOTHER WORD: "I KILL AND I MAKE ALIVE" (32:39) — THIS IS ONE OF FOUR PROMISES THAT WERE GIVEN THEM AS A HINT OF THE RESURRECTION OF THE DEAD. (זֶה אֶחָד מֵאַרְבַּע הַבְטָחוֹת "this is one of four promises"; רֶמֶז לִתְחִיַּת הַמֵּתִים "a hint of the resurrection of the dead"): the four listed in 329:3; no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 329:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:39. "I KILL AND I MAKE ALIVE"; "LET MY SOUL DIE THE DEATH OF THE UPRIGHT" (Numbers 23:10); "LET REUBEN LIVE AND NOT DIE" (33:6); "HE WILL REVIVE US AFTER TWO DAYS" (Hosea 6:2) — I MIGHT HEAR DEATH IN ONE AND LIFE IN ANOTHER; THE TEACHING SAYS "I HAVE WOUNDED AND I HEAL" — AS THE WOUND AND THE HEALING ARE IN ONE, SO DEATH AND LIFE ARE IN ONE. (שׁוֹמֵעַ אֲנִי מִיתָה בְּאֶחָד וְחַיִּים בְּאֶחָד "I might hear death in one and life in another"; כָּךְ מִיתָה וְחַיִּים בְּאֶחָד "so death and life are in one"): THE FOUR PROMISES — Balaam's (Numbers 23:10 on the tape, a RUN CITATION), the blessing's 33:6 (THE NEXT CHAPTER'S — the seat cited, the piska not opened), Hosea's (the Prophets' seat cited), the song's; "wounded and heal" the analogy that puts death and life in one body; the Hebrew marker "Deuteronomy 33" without the verse; no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 329:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:39. "AND THERE IS NONE THAT DELIVERS OUT OF MY HAND" (32:39) — FATHERS DO NOT DELIVER SONS: ABRAHAM DOES NOT DELIVER ISHMAEL, NOR ISAAC ESAU; I HAVE ONLY FATHERS WHO DO NOT DELIVER SONS — BROTHERS FOR BROTHERS, WHENCE? THE TEACHING SAYS "NO MAN CAN BY ANY MEANS REDEEM HIS BROTHER" (Psalm 49:8) — ISAAC DOES NOT DELIVER ISHMAEL, NOR JACOB ESAU; AND EVEN IF A MAN GIVES ALL THE MONEY IN THE WORLD THEY DO NOT GIVE HIM HIS RANSOM, AS IT IS SAID "NO MAN CAN BY ANY MEANS REDEEM HIS BROTHER … FOR THE REDEMPTION OF THEIR SOUL IS COSTLY" — THIS SOUL IS PRECIOUS: WHEN A MAN SINS WITH IT THERE IS NO PAYMENT FOR IT. (אֵין אָבוֹת מַצִּילִים אֶת הַבָּנִים "fathers do not deliver sons"; אַחִים אֶת אַחִים מִנַּיִן "brothers for brothers, whence"; יְקָרָה הִיא נֶפֶשׁ זוֹ "this soul is precious"): NO RANSOM FOR A SOUL — the fathers and brothers named from the tape (Abraham and Ishmael, Isaac and Esau, Jacob and Esau — RUN CITATIONS); the ransom (kofer, "a payment for a life") of Exodus 21:30 compiled at Exodus — a REFERENCE, here denied for the soul; the last row of the piska; the compile: no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 330:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:40. "FOR I LIFT UP MY HAND TO HEAVEN" (32:40) — WHEN THE HOLY ONE CREATED THE WORLD HE CREATED IT ONLY BY A WORD, AND HE CREATED IT ONLY BY AN OATH; AND WHO CAUSED HIM TO SWEAR? THOSE LACKING FAITH CAUSED HIM TO SWEAR, AS IT IS SAID "AND HE LIFTED UP HIS HAND TO THEM, TO OVERTHROW THEM IN THE WILDERNESS" (Psalm 106:26), AND "I HAVE LIFTED UP MY HAND TO THE NATIONS" (Ezekiel 36:7). The head row of piska 330 (לֹא בְרָאוֹ אֶלָּא בְּמַאֲמָר "He created it only by a word"; מְחֻסְּרֵי אֲמָנָה הֵם גָּרְמוּ לוֹ לִשָּׁבַע "those lacking faith caused Him to swear"): THE LIFTED HAND IS THE OATH — the spies' generation's oath (Numbers 14:28-30 on the tape, a RUN CITATION; Psalm 106:26 the Writings' seat cited) the cause; the world by utterance (the translator: Mishnah Avot 5:1's ten utterances — the testing shelf not opened); Onkelos's "the house of My Shekhinah in heaven" (the measure) the same lifted hand; the compile: no law — the oath's formula (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 330:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:40. "AND SAY: AS I LIVE FOR EVER" (32:40) — NOT AS THE MEASURE OF FLESH AND BLOOD IS THE MEASURE OF THE HOLY ONE: THE MEASURE OF FLESH AND BLOOD — A GOVERNOR ENTERS HIS PROVINCE; IF HE CAN EXACT FROM HIS PREDECESSOR HE EXACTS, AND IF NOT HE CANNOT EXACT; BUT HE WHO SPOKE AND THE WORLD WAS IS NOT SO — IF HE DOES NOT EXACT FROM THE LIVING HE EXACTS FROM THE DEAD, IF HE DOES NOT EXACT IN THIS WORLD HE EXACTS IN THE WORLD TO COME. (שֶׁלֹּא כְּמִדַּת בָּשָׂר וָדָם מִדַּת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ "not as the measure of flesh and blood is the measure of the Holy One"; אֵינוֹ נִפְרָע מִן הַחַיִּים נִפְרָע מִן הַמֵּתִים "if He does not exact from the living He exacts from the dead"): "AS I LIVE FOR EVER" — the governor's term against the eternal Judge (the lexicon of 317:5 — the governor, the province); the exaction from the dead and in the world to come; the last row of the piska; no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 331:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:41. "IF I WHET MY GLITTERING SWORD" (32:41) — WHEN PUNISHMENT GOES OUT FROM BEFORE ME IT IS SWIFT AS LIGHTNING; EVEN SO — "AND MY HAND TAKE HOLD ON JUDGMENT". The head row of piska 331 (קַלָּה הִיא כַּבָּרָק "it is swift as lightning"): "glittering" (barak, "lightning") the pun; judgment held even in haste (the translator: Mekhilta Shirah 4); no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 331:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:41. "I WILL RENDER VENGEANCE TO MY ADVERSARIES" — THAT IS ONE; "AND WILL RECOMPENSE THOSE WHO HATE ME" — THAT IS TWO. (הֲרֵי אֶחָד "that is one"; הֲרֵי שְׁנַיִם "that is two"): the two vengeances counted (333:3 names them — the blood and the violence); the translator: the small-type text an interpolation (Finkelstein); no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 331:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:41. ANOTHER WORD: "I WILL RENDER VENGEANCE TO MY ADVERSARIES" — THESE ARE THE CUTHEANS (THE SAMARITANS), AS IT IS SAID "AND THE ADVERSARIES OF JUDAH AND BENJAMIN HEARD" (Ezra 4:1). (אֵלּוּ כּוּתִיִּים "these are the Cutheans"): the Samaritans (kutim, "the Cutheans") — the Writings' seat Ezra 4:1 cited, never read; no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 331:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:41. "AND WILL RECOMPENSE THOSE WHO HATE ME" — THESE ARE THE SECTARIANS; AND SO IT SAYS "DO I NOT HATE THEM, O LORD, WHO HATE YOU, AND STRIVE WITH THOSE WHO RISE UP AGAINST YOU" (Psalm 139:21). (אֵלּוּ הַמִּינִים "these are the sectarians"): the sectarians (minim, "the sectarians") — 320:8's again; the last row of the piska; the translator: Avot deRabbi Natan A 16; no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 332:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:42. "I WILL MAKE MY ARROWS DRUNK WITH BLOOD" (32:42) — AND CAN ARROWS BE DRUNK WITH BLOOD? RATHER, I MAKE OTHERS DRUNK WITH WHAT MY ARROWS DO. The head row of piska 332 (וְכִי אִפְשָׁר לָהֶם לְחִצִּים שֶׁיִּשְׁתַּכְּרוּ מִדָּם "can arrows be drunk with blood"): the figure resolved by the causative — "I make others drunk"; no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 332:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:42. "AND MY SWORD SHALL DEVOUR FLESH" (32:42) — AND CAN A SWORD DEVOUR FLESH? RATHER, I FEED OTHERS WITH WHAT MY SWORD DOES; AND SO IT SAYS "AND YOU, SON OF MAN, SAY TO THE BIRDS OF EVERY WING" (Ezekiel 39:17), AND IT SAYS "AND YOU SHALL EAT FAT TILL YOU ARE FULL AND DRINK BLOOD", AND "YOU SHALL EAT THE FLESH OF THE MIGHTY", AND "YOU SHALL BE FILLED AT MY TABLE WITH HORSES AND CHARIOTS", AND IT SAYS "THE SWORD OF THE LORD IS FILLED WITH BLOOD, IT IS MADE FAT WITH FATNESS" (Isaiah 34:6) — WHY? "FOR THE LORD HAS A SACRIFICE IN BOZRAH AND A GREAT SLAUGHTER IN THE LAND OF EDOM". (הֲרֵינִי מַאֲכִיל אֲחֵרִים "I feed others"; ״כִּי זֶבַח לַה׳ בְּבָצְרָה "for the LORD has a sacrifice in Bozrah"): the feast of Gog (Ezekiel 39:17-20) and Edom's slaughter (Isaiah 34:6) the sword's eaters — the Prophets' seats cited, never read; the assonance sacrifice / slaughter (zevach / tevach) the translator's; no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 332:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:42. "WITH THE BLOOD OF THE SLAIN AND THE CAPTIVES" (32:42) — FOR WHAT THEY DID TO THE SLAIN OF MY PEOPLE; AND SO IT SAYS "OH THAT MY HEAD WERE WATERS AND MY EYES A FOUNTAIN OF TEARS, THAT I MIGHT WEEP DAY AND NIGHT FOR THE SLAIN OF THE DAUGHTER OF MY PEOPLE" (Jeremiah 8:23). (מִמַּה שֶׁעָשׂוּ בְּחַלְלֵי עַמִּי "for what they did to the slain of My people"): the slain (chalal, "one pierced") of the verse the nations' victims; no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 332:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:42. "AND THE CAPTIVES" — FOR WHAT THEY DID TO THE CAPTIVES OF MY PEOPLE; AND SO IT SAYS "AND THEY SHALL TAKE THEM CAPTIVE WHOSE CAPTIVES THEY WERE, AND SHALL RULE OVER THEIR OPPRESSORS" (Isaiah 14:2). (מִמַּה שֶּׁעָשׂוּ בִּשְׁבִי עַמִּי "for what they did to the captives of My people"): the captives' turn (the Prophets' seat cited); no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 332:5 — MATERIAL. On 32:42. "FROM THE HEAD OF THE LEADERS OF THE ENEMY" (32:42) — WHEN THE HOLY ONE BRINGS PUNISHMENT ON THE NATIONS HE BRINGS UPON THEM NOT THEIRS ALONE BUT THEIRS AND THEIR FATHERS' FROM NIMROD ONWARD; WHEN HE BRINGS GOOD ON ISRAEL HE BRINGS UPON THEM THEIRS AND THEIR FATHERS' FROM ABRAHAM ONWARD. (שֶׁלָּהֶם וְשֶׁל אֲבוֹתֵיהֶם מִנִּמְרוֹד וָאֵילָךְ "theirs and their fathers from Nimrod onward"; מֵאַבְרָהָם וָאֵילָךְ "from Abraham onward"): NIMROD AND ABRAHAM THE TWO HEADS of the two ledgers — the fathers' deeds reckoned to the sons both ways (Genesis 10:8 and 12:1 on the tape, RUN CITATIONS); no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 332:6 — MATERIAL. On 32:42. ANOTHER WORD: "FROM THE HEAD OF THE LEADERS OF THE ENEMY" — WHY DID THEY SEE FIT TO HANG ALL THE PUNISHMENTS ON PHARAOH'S HEAD? BECAUSE HE WAS THE FIRST TO ENSLAVE ISRAEL. (מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהָיָה תְּחִלָּה לְשִׁעְבּוּדָם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל "because he was the first to enslave Israel"): "leaders" (par'ot) read as Pharaoh (par'oh) — Exodus 1:8-11 on the tape a RUN CITATION; the last row of the piska; no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 333:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:43. "SING ALOUD, O NATIONS, OF HIS PEOPLE" (32:43) — TOMORROW, WHEN THE HOLY ONE BRINGS REDEMPTION TO ISRAEL, THE NATIONS OF THE WORLD TREMBLE BEFORE HIM; AND THIS IS NOT THEIR FIRST TIME, FOR THEY TREMBLED OF OLD, AS IN THE MATTER SAID "THE PEOPLES HEARD, THEY TREMBLE" (Exodus 15:14). The head row of piska 333 (אֻמּוֹת הָעוֹלָם מִתְרַגְּזִים לְפָנָיו "the nations of the world tremble before Him"; שֶׁכְּבָר רָגְזוּ מִקֶּדֶם "for they trembled of old"): THE TWO SONGS JOINED — the Song at the Sea (Exodus 15:14 on the tape, a RUN CITATION) the first trembling, the redemption's the second (the translator: Mekhilta Shirah 9); no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 333:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:43. ANOTHER WORD: "SING ALOUD, O NATIONS, OF HIS PEOPLE" — THE NATIONS OF THE WORLD ARE TO PRAISE BEFORE ISRAEL, AS IT IS SAID "SING ALOUD, O NATIONS, OF HIS PEOPLE"; AND EVEN HEAVEN AND EARTH, AS IT IS SAID "SING, O HEAVENS, FOR THE LORD HAS DONE IT; SHOUT, YOU LOWER PARTS OF THE EARTH" (Isaiah 44:23); WHENCE EVEN THE MOUNTAINS AND HILLS? "THE MOUNTAINS AND THE HILLS SHALL BREAK FORTH BEFORE YOU INTO SINGING" (Isaiah 55:12); WHENCE EVEN THE TREES? "AND ALL THE TREES OF THE FIELD SHALL CLAP THEIR HANDS" (Isaiah 55:12); WHENCE EVEN THE FATHERS AND THE MOTHERS? "LET THE INHABITANTS OF THE ROCK SING, LET THEM SHOUT FROM THE TOP OF THE MOUNTAINS" (Isaiah 42:11). (עֲתִידִים אֻמּוֹת הָעוֹלָם לִהְיוֹת מְקַלְּסִים לִפְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל "the nations of the world are to praise before Israel"; מִנַּיִן אַף אָבוֹת וְאִמָּהוֹת "whence even the fathers and mothers"): THE WITNESSES OF 32:1 CALLED TO THE PRAISE — heaven and earth (the song's frame closing on its openers), then the mountains, the trees, the fathers and mothers ("the inhabitants of the rock" — the tombs); the Prophets' seats cited; the English's Isaiah 43:11 a misprint for the Hebrew's 42:11; no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 333:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:43. "FOR HE WILL AVENGE THE BLOOD OF HIS SERVANTS, AND RENDER VENGEANCE TO HIS ADVERSARIES" (32:43) — TWO VENGEANCES: VENGEANCE FOR THE BLOOD AND VENGEANCE FOR THE VIOLENCE; WHENCE THAT ALL THE VIOLENCE THE NATIONS OF THE WORLD DID TO ISRAEL IS RECKONED TO THEM AS IF THEY SHED INNOCENT BLOOD? AS IT IS SAID "I WILL GATHER ALL THE NATIONS AND BRING THEM DOWN TO THE VALLEY OF JEHOSHAPHAT, AND PLEAD WITH THEM THERE FOR MY PEOPLE ISRAEL" (Joel 4:2), "EGYPT SHALL BE A DESOLATION AND EDOM A DESOLATE WILDERNESS, FOR THE VIOLENCE AGAINST THE CHILDREN OF JUDAH, BECAUSE THEY SHED INNOCENT BLOOD IN THEIR LAND" (Joel 4:19); AT THAT HOUR, "JUDAH SHALL DWELL FOR EVER AND JERUSALEM FROM GENERATION TO GENERATION, AND I WILL HOLD THEIR BLOOD INNOCENT WHICH I HAVE NOT HELD INNOCENT, AND THE LORD DWELLS IN ZION" (Joel 4:20-21). (שְׁתֵּי נְקָמוֹת נָקָם עַל הַדָּם וְנָקָם עַל "two vengeances: vengeance for the blood and vengeance for"; כְּאִלּוּ דָּם נָקִי שָׁפְכוּ "as if they shed innocent blood"): VIOLENCE RECKONED AS BLOODSHED — the two vengeances of 331:2 named; the Prophets' seats cited, never read; the compile: no law — the equation a rule of reckoning, the tape's "innocent blood" formula (19:10, 21:8) a REFERENCE (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 333:4 — MATERIAL. On 32:43. "AND HIS LAND MAKES ATONEMENT FOR HIS PEOPLE" (32:43) — WHENCE THAT THE KILLING OF ISRAEL BY THE NATIONS OF THE WORLD IS AN ATONEMENT FOR THEM IN THE WORLD TO COME? AS IT IS SAID "A PSALM OF ASAPH: O GOD, THE NATIONS HAVE COME INTO YOUR INHERITANCE … THEY HAVE GIVEN THE DEAD BODIES OF YOUR SERVANTS … THEY HAVE SHED THEIR BLOOD LIKE WATER" (Psalm 79:1-3). ANOTHER WORD: WHENCE DO YOU SAY THAT THE DESCENT OF THE WICKED TO GEHENNA (THE PLACE OF PUNISHMENT) IS AN ATONEMENT FOR THEM? "I HAVE GIVEN EGYPT AS YOUR RANSOM, ETHIOPIA AND SEBA FOR YOU; SINCE YOU ARE PRECIOUS IN MY SIGHT AND HONORED, AND I HAVE LOVED YOU" (Isaiah 43:3-4). R. MEIR USED TO SAY: WHOEVER DWELLS IN THE LAND OF ISRAEL, THE LAND OF ISRAEL ATONES FOR HIM, AS IT IS SAID "THE PEOPLE WHO DWELL IN IT ARE FORGIVEN INIQUITY" (Isaiah 33:24) — STILL THE MATTER HANGS ON WHAT IS NOT SETTLED: WE DO NOT KNOW WHETHER THEY UNLOAD THEIR INIQUITIES ON IT OR BEAR THEIR INIQUITIES ON IT; WHEN IT SAYS "AND HIS LAND MAKES ATONEMENT FOR HIS PEOPLE" — THEY UNLOAD THEIR INIQUITIES ON IT AND DO NOT BEAR THEIR INIQUITIES ON IT. AND SO R. MEIR USED TO SAY: WHOEVER DWELLS IN THE LAND OF ISRAEL AND READS THE SHEMA (HEAR, O ISRAEL) MORNING AND EVENING AND SPEAKS THE HOLY TONGUE IS A SON OF THE WORLD TO COME. (כָּל הַיּוֹשֵׁב בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל מְכַפֶּרֶת "whoever dwells in the land of Israel, the land of Israel atones"; וְקוֹרֵא קְרִיאַת שְׁמַע שַׁחֲרִית וְעַרְבִית "and reads the Shema morning and evening"; וּמְדַבֵּר בִּלְשׁוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ "and speaks the holy tongue"): A RULE FROM THE VERSE — THE LAND ATONES (R. Meir), its condition settled by the verse (the iniquities unloaded, not borne); the Shema's morning and evening (6:7's "when you lie down and when you rise", compiled at chapter 6 — a REFERENCE; Mishnah Berakhot 1:1 the answer sheet's clock, not opened) and the holy tongue the world to come's conditions; the death by the nations an atonement, Gehenna the wicked's; the compile: a status — dwelling in the land, the Shema twice, the tongue (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 333:5 — MATERIAL. On 32:43. YOU SAY: GREAT IS THIS SONG, FOR IT HAS IN IT THE PRESENT, AND IT HAS IN IT THE PAST, AND IT HAS IN IT THE FUTURE TO COME, AND IT HAS IN IT THIS WORLD, AND IT HAS IN IT THE WORLD TO COME. (גְּדוֹלָה שִׁירָה זוֹ "great is this song"; וְיֵשׁ בָּהּ לֶעָתִיד לָבוֹא "and it has in it the future to come"): THE SONG'S THREE TIMES AND TWO WORLDS — the spine's own verdict on the song's tense: the past the tape's (the desert, the eagle, the honey), the present the charge's, the future a POINTER (the vengeance, the nations' song) — THE RETELLING RULES' three kinds in the tradition's words; the last row of the song's piskaot (306-333 on 32:1-43); no law (DV32-12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 334:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_29_31_nitzavim_vayelech_2026-09-27.md). On 32:44. "AND MOSES CAME" (32:44) — IT IS SAID HERE "AND MOSES CAME" AND IT IS SAID THERE "AND MOSES WENT" (31:1); ONE CANNOT SAY "AND MOSES CAME" SINCE "AND MOSES WENT" IS ALREADY SAID, AND ONE CANNOT SAY "AND MOSES WENT" SINCE "AND MOSES CAME" IS ALREADY SAID — SAY FROM NOW: HIS SUCCESSOR CAME, AND THE AUTHORITY WAS GIVEN INTO ANOTHER'S HAND. The head row of piska 334 (נֶאֱמַר כָּאן וַיָּבֹא מֹשֶׁה "it is said here: and Moses came"; בָּא דְּיוֹתִיכוֹס שֶׁלּוֹ "his successor came"; וְהָרְשׁוּת נִתְּנָה בְּיַד אַחֵר "and the authority was given into another's hand"): 31:1'S "AND MOSES WENT" — sitting 19's row (the marker's day, the ledger deu_29_31) read against 32:44's "came": the successor (diadochos, "the one who takes over" — the Greek in Hebrew letters; the translator) arrives and the authority passes; the compile: the aftermath's frame — Joshua's authority the status the tape set at 31:14-23's commission, no new law (DV32A-01). REREAD WHOLE (sitting 19's reading — 31:1; the ledger deu_29_31).
- Sifrei Devarim 334:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:44. "AND SPOKE ALL THE WORDS OF THIS SONG IN THE EARS OF THE PEOPLE" (32:44) — THIS TEACHES THAT HE SANK THEM INTO THEIR EARS. (מְלַמֵּד שֶׁהָיָה מְשַׁקְּעָם בְּאָזְנֵיהֶם "this teaches that he sank them into their ears"): the manner of the speaking; no law (DV32A-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 334:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:44. "HE AND HOSHEA SON OF NUN" (32:44) — WHY DO I NEED IT? IS IT NOT ALREADY SAID "AND MOSES CALLED HOSHEA SON OF NUN JOSHUA" (Numbers 13:16)? WHAT DOES THE TEACHING SAY BY "HE AND HOSHEA SON OF NUN"? TO MAKE KNOWN JOSHUA'S RIGHTEOUSNESS — I MIGHT HEAR THAT HIS MIND GREW PROUD OVER HIM WHEN HE WAS APPOINTED TO AUTHORITY; THE TEACHING SAYS "HE AND HOSHEA SON OF NUN": HOSHEA IN HIS RIGHTEOUSNESS — THOUGH APPOINTED LEADER OVER ISRAEL, HE IS HOSHEA IN HIS RIGHTEOUSNESS. LIKEWISE YOU SAY "AND JOSEPH WAS IN EGYPT" (Exodus 1:5) — DO WE NOT KNOW THAT JOSEPH WAS IN EGYPT? RATHER, TO MAKE KNOWN JOSEPH'S RIGHTEOUSNESS, WHO SHEPHERDED HIS FATHER'S FLOCK, AND THOUGH APPOINTED KING IN EGYPT HE IS JOSEPH IN HIS RIGHTEOUSNESS. LIKEWISE "AND DAVID WAS THE YOUNGEST" (1 Samuel 17:14) — DO WE NOT KNOW THAT DAVID WAS THE YOUNGEST? RATHER, TO MAKE KNOWN TO YOU DAVID'S RIGHTEOUSNESS, WHO SHEPHERDED HIS FATHER'S FLOCK, AND THOUGH APPOINTED KING OVER ISRAEL HE IS DAVID IN HIS LITTLENESS. (לְהוֹדִיעַ צִדְקוֹ שֶׁל יְהוֹשֻׁעַ "to make known Joshua's righteousness"; הוּא הוֹשֵׁעַ בְּצִדְקוֹ "he is Hoshea in his righteousness"; הוּא דָּוִד בְּקָטְנוֹ "he is David in his littleness"): THE OLD NAME KEPT — the measure's kin (Numbers 13:16, the spies' renaming on the tape: shelach.spies' joshua_name a RUN CITATION) read as humility unchanged by office; Joseph and David the parallels (Exodus 1:5 on the tape; the Prophets' seat cited); the last row of the piska; no law (DV32A-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 335:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:46. "AND HE SAID TO THEM: SET YOUR HEART TO ALL THE WORDS WHICH I TESTIFY AGAINST YOU THIS DAY" (32:46) — A MAN MUST HAVE HIS HEART, HIS EYES AND HIS EARS DIRECTED TO THE WORDS OF TORAH; AND SO IT SAYS "SON OF MAN, SEE WITH YOUR EYES AND HEAR WITH YOUR EARS ALL THAT I SAY TO YOU, AND SET YOUR HEART TO THE ENTRANCE OF THE HOUSE" (Ezekiel 44:5); AN ARGUMENT FROM THE LIGHTER: IF THE TEMPLE, WHICH IS SEEN WITH THE EYES AND MEASURED BY THE HAND, NEEDS A MAN'S HEART, EYES AND EARS DIRECTED, THE WORDS OF TORAH, WHICH ARE LIKE MOUNTAINS HANGING BY A HAIR — HOW MUCH MORE. The head row of piska 335 (צָרִיךְ אָדָם לִהְיוֹת לִבּוֹ וְעֵינָיו וְאָזְנָיו מְכֻוָּנִים "a man must have his heart, eyes and ears directed"; כַּהֲרָרִים הַתְּלוּיִים בִּשְׂעָרָה "like mountains hanging by a hair"): THE CHARGE'S MANNER — heart, eyes and ears; "mountains hanging by a hair" Mishnah Chagigah 1:8's phrase for the laws with scant Scripture (317:3 named it; the testing shelf not opened); the Hebrew marker Ezekiel 40:4 for words that are 44:5's (the English's citation; the Prophets' seat cited); the compile: no law — the charge's attention (DV32A-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 335:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:46. "WHICH YOU SHALL COMMAND YOUR CHILDREN TO OBSERVE" (32:46) — HE SAID TO THEM: I MUST BE GRATEFUL TO YOU THAT YOU WILL UPHOLD THE TORAH AFTER ME; YOU TOO MUST BE GRATEFUL TO YOUR CHILDREN THAT THEY WILL UPHOLD THE TORAH AFTER YOU. IT HAPPENED THAT OUR MASTER CAME FROM LAODICEA, AND R. YOSE SON OF R. YEHUDAH AND R. ELAZAR SON OF YEHUDAH ENTERED AND SAT BEFORE HIM; HE SAID TO THEM: DRAW NEAR — I MUST BE GRATEFUL TO YOU THAT YOU WILL UPHOLD THE TORAH AFTER ME; YOU TOO MUST BE GRATEFUL TO YOUR CHILDREN THAT THEY WILL UPHOLD THE TORAH AFTER YOU. IF MOSES WERE NOT GREAT, AND HAD OTHERS NOT RECEIVED THE TORAH THROUGH HIM, IT WOULD NOT HAVE BEEN WORTH IT — [WE], HOW MUCH MORE. THEREFORE IT IS SAID "WHICH YOU SHALL COMMAND YOUR CHILDREN". (צָרִיךְ אֲנִי לְהַחֲזִיק לָכֶם טוֹבָה "I must be grateful to you"; מַעֲשֶׂה שֶׁבָּא רַבֵּינוּ מִלָּדִיקְיָא "it happened that our Master came from Laodicea"): THE CHAIN OF TEACHING — the teacher's gratitude to the disciples who carry the Torah on (R. Yehudah the Patriarch; the translator: "sons" for disciples, piska 34); the last row of the piska; no law (DV32A-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 336:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:47. "FOR IT IS NO EMPTY THING FROM YOU" (32:47) — YOU HAVE NO EMPTY THING IN THE TORAH WHICH, IF YOU EXPOUND IT, HAS NO GIVING OF REWARD IN THIS WORLD WHILE THE PRINCIPAL STANDS FOR HIM FOR THE WORLD TO COME. KNOW THAT IT IS SO: THEY SAID, WHY IS IT WRITTEN "AND LOTAN'S SISTER WAS TIMNA" (Genesis 36:22), "AND TIMNA WAS A CONCUBINE" (Genesis 36:12)? BECAUSE SHE SAID: I AM NOT WORTHY TO BE HIS WIFE, LET ME BE HIS CONCUBINE — AND ALL THIS WHY? TO MAKE KNOWN THE LOVE OF ABRAHAM OUR FATHER, THAT THERE WERE THOSE WHO WOULD NOT HAVE KINGSHIP AND RULE AND RAN TO CLEAVE TO HIM; AND IS IT NOT AN ARGUMENT FROM THE LIGHTER? IF ESAU, WHO HAD IN HIS HAND ONLY ONE COMMANDMENT, THAT HE HONORED HIS FATHER, KINGS AND RULERS RAN TO CLEAVE TO HIM — HOW MUCH MORE WOULD THEY RUN TO CLEAVE TO JACOB THE RIGHTEOUS, WHO UPHELD THE WHOLE TORAH, AS IT IS SAID "AND JACOB WAS A PERFECT MAN, DWELLING IN TENTS" (Genesis 25:27). The head row of piska 336 (אֵין לְךָ דָּבָר רֵיקָם בַּתּוֹרָה "you have no empty thing in the Torah"; וְהַקֶּרֶן קַיֶּמֶת לוֹ לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא "and the principal stands for him for the world to come"; לְהוֹדִיעֲךָ חִבָּתוֹ שֶׁל אַבְרָהָם אָבִינוּ "to make known the love of Abraham our father"): THE FRUIT AND THE PRINCIPAL — Mishnah Peah 1:1's frame inside the spine (the translator: also Tosefta Peah 1:1-2; the testing shelf not opened); Timna's two verses on the tape (Genesis 36:12, 36:22 — the Genesis ledgers' RUN CITATIONS) the proof that no verse is empty; Esau's one commandment (the honoring of his father) against Jacob's whole Torah (312:1's "perfect"); no law (DV32A-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 336:2 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_22_25_ki_teitzei_2026-09-25.md). On 32:47. "AND THROUGH THIS THING YOU SHALL PROLONG DAYS" (32:47) — THIS IS ONE OF THE THINGS WHOSE DOER EATS THEIR FRUIT IN THIS WORLD AND HAS LENGTH OF DAYS IN THE WORLD TO COME; AND IT IS EXPLICIT HERE OF TORAH STUDY; OF HONORING FATHER AND MOTHER, WHENCE? THE TEACHING SAYS "HONOR YOUR FATHER AND YOUR MOTHER, THAT YOUR DAYS MAY BE LONG" (Exodus 20:12); OF SENDING AWAY THE NEST IT IS WRITTEN "YOU SHALL SURELY LET THE MOTHER GO AND TAKE THE YOUNG FOR YOURSELF, THAT IT MAY BE WELL WITH YOU AND YOU MAY PROLONG DAYS" (22:7); OF BRINGING PEACE IT IS WRITTEN "AND ALL YOUR CHILDREN SHALL BE TAUGHT OF THE LORD, AND GREAT SHALL BE THE PEACE OF YOUR CHILDREN" (Isaiah 54:13). (וּמְפֹרָשׁ כָּאן בְּתַלְמוּד תּוֹרָה "and it is explicit here of Torah study"; בְּשִׁלּוּחַ הַקֵּן כָּתוּב "of sending away the nest it is written"; בַּהֲבָאַת שָׁלוֹם כָּתוּב "of bringing peace it is written"): THE ANSWER SHEET INSIDE THE SPINE — Mishnah Peah 1:1's list (honoring parents, bringing peace, Torah study) each given its verse, with the nest added (the translator's note: the Sifrei weaves a companion to the Mishnah); 22:7's nest — the ledger deu_22_25's row, the nest law compiled at 17b (persons_poor_court — a REFERENCE); Exodus 20:12 on the tape a RUN CITATION; the last row of the piska; the compile: the reward's two arms — the fruit here, the days there (DV32A-02). REREAD WHOLE (chapters 22-25's reading — 22:7; the ledger deu_22_25).
- Sifrei Devarim 337:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in gen_17_boarding_2026-08-25.md). On 32:48. "AND THE LORD SPOKE TO MOSES IN THE SELFSAME DAY" (32:48) — IN THREE PLACES "IN THE SELFSAME DAY" IS SAID. IT IS SAID "IN THE SELFSAME DAY" OF NOAH (Genesis 7:13) — THIS TEACHES THAT NOAH'S GENERATION WERE SAYING: BY SUCH-AND-SUCH, IF WE FEEL HIM AT IT WE WILL NOT LET HIM, AND MORE — WE WILL TAKE AXES AND HATCHETS AND SPLIT THE ARK ON HIM; THE HOLY ONE SAID: I BRING HIM IN AT MIDDAY, AND WHOEVER HAS POWER TO PROTEST, LET HIM COME AND PROTEST. AND WHY DID IT SEE FIT TO SAY OF EGYPT "IN THE SELFSAME DAY ALL THE HOSTS OF THE LORD WENT OUT" (Exodus 12:41)? BECAUSE THE EGYPTIANS WERE SAYING: BY SUCH-AND-SUCH, IF WE FEEL THEM AT IT WE WILL NOT LET THEM, AND MORE — WE WILL TAKE SWORDS AND BLADES AND KILL THEM; THE HOLY ONE SAID: I BRING THEM OUT AT MIDDAY, AND WHOEVER HAS POWER TO PROTEST, LET HIM COME AND PROTEST. AND WHY DID IT SEE FIT TO SAY HERE "IN THE SELFSAME DAY"? BECAUSE ISRAEL WERE SAYING: BY SUCH-AND-SUCH, IF WE FEEL HIM AT IT WE WILL NOT LET HIM — THE MAN WHO BROUGHT US OUT OF EGYPT AND SPLIT THE SEA FOR US AND BROUGHT DOWN THE MANNA FOR US AND BROUGHT THE QUAIL FOR US AND DID MIRACLES AND MIGHTY DEEDS FOR US, WE WILL NOT LET HIM; THE HOLY ONE SAID: I BRING HIM INTO THE CAVE AT MIDDAY, AND WHOEVER HAS POWER TO PROTEST, LET HIM COME AND PROTEST. THEREFORE IT IS SAID "AND THE LORD SPOKE TO MOSES IN THE SELFSAME DAY, SAYING". The one row of piska 337 (בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה מְקוֹמוֹת נֶאֱמַר בְּעֶצֶם הַיּוֹם הַזֶּה "in three places: in the selfsame day is said"; הֲרֵינִי מַכְנִיסוֹ בַּחֲצִי הַיּוֹם "I bring him in at midday"; הֲרֵינִי מַכְנִיסוֹ לַמְּעָרָה בַּחֲצִי הַיּוֹם "I bring him into the cave at midday"): THE MEASURE'S KIN TAUGHT — 32:48 with Genesis 7:13 (the ark's boarding, the Genesis ledger's row) and Exodus 12:41 (the exodus's day on the tape, a RUN CITATION; the measure found 12:17, the spine's own seat 12:41 — the English cites 12:17): the eleven "selfsame day" of the Torah narrowed to the three where a protest is dared, "at midday" the answer; the cave the burial's (34:6 ahead — cited, not read); the compile: the aftermath's day the marker's (40, 12, 7) at 20b, the divine frame's one "saying" (DV32A-03). REREAD WHOLE (the Genesis ledger gen_17_boarding — Genesis 7:13).
- Sifrei Devarim 338:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:49. "GO UP INTO THIS MOUNTAIN OF ABARIM" (32:49) — IT IS AN ASCENT FOR YOU AND NOT A DESCENT. The head row of piska 338 (עֲלִיָּה הִיא לְךָ וְאֵינָהּ יְרִידָה "it is an ascent for you and not a descent"): death as ascent; no law (DV32A-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 338:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:49. "THIS MOUNTAIN OF ABARIM" (32:49) — WHICH IS CALLED BY FOUR NAMES: THE MOUNTAIN OF ABARIM, MOUNT NEBO, HOR THE MOUNTAIN, THE TOP OF PISGAH; AND WHY DO THEY CALL IT MOUNT NEBO? BECAUSE THREE PROPHETS WHO DIED NOT BY THE HAND OF TRANSGRESSION ARE BURIED IN IT, AND THESE ARE THEY: MOSES, AARON AND MIRIAM. (שֶׁנִּקְרָא אַרְבָּעָה שֵׁמוֹת "which is called by four names"; מֹשֶׁה אַהֲרֹן וּמִרְיָם "Moses, Aaron and Miriam"): THE FOUR NAMES — Abarim, Nebo, Hor, Pisgah one mountain (the translator: Abarim read as "four", arba; Nebo as "prophets", nevi'im); the spine's own identification of Hor the mountain (Aaron's, Numbers 20:22-28 on the tape) with Nebo — the teacher's link, carried as the shelf gives it; the three who died without transgression's hand (Miriam's death Numbers 20:1 on the tape — RUN CITATIONS); no law (DV32A-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 338:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:49. "WHICH IS IN THE LAND OF MOAB" (32:49) — THIS TEACHES THAT HE SHOWED HIM THE CHAIN OF KINGS WHO ARE TO ARISE FROM RUTH THE MOABITESS; "WHICH IS OVER AGAINST JERICHO" — THIS TEACHES THAT HE SHOWED HIM THE CHAIN OF PROPHETS WHO ARE TO ARISE FROM RAHAB THE HARLOT; "AND SEE ALL THE LAND OF CANAAN" — R. ELIEZER SAYS: THE FINGER OF THE HOLY ONE WAS A GUIDE FOR MOSES AND SHOWED HIM ALL THE CITIES OF THE LAND OF ISRAEL: TO HERE EPHRAIM'S BORDER, TO HERE MANASSEH'S BORDER; R. YEHOSHUA SAYS: MOSES HIMSELF SAW IT — HOW? HE GAVE STRENGTH TO MOSES' EYES AND HE SAW FROM THE END OF THE WORLD TO ITS END. (שַׁלְשֶׁלֶת הַמְּלָכִים שֶׁעֲתִידִים לַעֲמֹד מֵרוּת הַמּוֹאֲבִיָּה "the chain of kings who are to arise from Ruth the Moabitess"; אֶצְבָּעוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא "the finger of the Holy One"; מֹשֶׁה בְּעַצְמוֹ רָאָה אוֹתָהּ "Moses himself saw it"): THE SIGHT OF THE LAND OPENED TO ITS HISTORY — Ruth's kings and Rahab's prophets (the Writings' and the Prophets' seats cited, never read); the guide (metatron, "a guide" — the translator: not the named angel); the eyes strengthened; the last row of the piska; the compile: 34:1-4's sight AHEAD — cited, not read (DV32A-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 339:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:50. "AND DIE IN THE MOUNTAIN WHERE YOU GO UP" (32:50) — HE SAID BEFORE HIM: MASTER OF THE WORLD, WHY DO I DIE? IS IT NOT BETTER THAT THEY SAY "MOSES IS GOOD" FROM SIGHT THAN THAT THEY SAY "MOSES IS GOOD" FROM HEARING? IS IT NOT BETTER THAT THEY SAY "THIS IS MOSES WHO BROUGHT US OUT OF EGYPT AND SPLIT THE SEA FOR US AND BROUGHT DOWN THE MANNA FOR US AND DID MIRACLES AND MIGHTY DEEDS FOR US" THAN THAT THEY SAY "SO AND SO WAS MOSES, SO AND SO MOSES DID"? HE SAID TO HIM: ENOUGH, MOSES — IT IS A DECREE FROM BEFORE ME, EQUAL FOR EVERY MAN, AS IT IS SAID "THIS IS THE LAW: WHEN A MAN DIES IN A TENT" (Numbers 19:14), AND IT SAYS "AND THIS IS THE LAW OF MAN, O LORD GOD" (2 Samuel 7:19). THE MINISTERING ANGELS SAID BEFORE THE HOLY ONE: MASTER OF THE WORLD, WHY DID THE FIRST ADAM DIE? HE SAID TO THEM: BECAUSE HE DID NOT DO MY COMMANDS. THEY SAID BEFORE HIM: BUT MOSES DID YOUR COMMANDS! HE SAID TO THEM: IT IS A DECREE FROM BEFORE ME, EQUAL FOR EVERY MAN, AS IT IS SAID "THIS IS THE LAW: WHEN A MAN DIES IN A TENT". The head row of piska 339 (רִבּוֹנוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם לָמָּה אֲנִי מֵת "Master of the world, why do I die"; גְּזֵרָה הִיא מִלְּפָנַי שֶׁהִיא שָׁוָה בְּכָל אָדָם "it is a decree from before Me, equal for every man"; וַהֲרֵי מֹשֶׁה עָשָׂה פִּקּוּדֶיךָ "but Moses did Your commands"): THE LAW OF THE TENT READ AS DEATH'S DECREE — Numbers 19:14 (the corpse's uncleanness, compiled at Numbers — a REFERENCE) taken for its first words, "a man dies", the decree equal for all; Moses' plea (sight against hearing — 337:1's list of his deeds again; the translator); the angels' question on the first Adam (Genesis 3:19 on the tape, a RUN CITATION); the compile: no law — Moses' death the tape's event ahead at 34:5, the three gifts' daemon owed (DV32A-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 339:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:50. "AND BE GATHERED TO YOUR PEOPLE" (32:50) — TO ABRAHAM, ISAAC AND JACOB, TO AMRAM AND KOHATH, TO MIRIAM AND AARON YOUR BROTHER. (אֵצֶל אַבְרָהָם יִצְחָק וְיַעֲקֹב "to Abraham, Isaac and Jacob"; אֵצֶל עַמְרָם וּקְהָת "to Amram and Kohath"): "YOUR PEOPLE" NAMED — the fathers, the Levite line (Exodus 6:18-20 on the tape, RUN CITATIONS), the brother and sister; the gathering formula's referent (the measure: "be gathered to your people" once in this form); no law (DV32A-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 339:3 — MATERIAL. On 32:50. "AS AARON YOUR BROTHER DIED" (32:50) — A DEATH YOU COVETED; AND WHENCE DID MOSES COVET AARON'S DEATH? WHEN THE HOLY ONE SAID TO HIM "TAKE AARON AND ELEAZAR HIS SON" (Numbers 20:25), "AND STRIP AARON OF HIS GARMENTS" (Numbers 20:26) — THESE ARE THE PRIESTLY GARMENTS — CLOTHE ELEAZAR IN THEM, AND SO THE SECOND AND SO THE THIRD; HE SAID TO HIM: ENTER THE CAVE — AND HE ENTERED; GO UP ON THE BIER — AND HE WENT UP; STRETCH OUT YOUR HANDS — AND HE STRETCHED; STRETCH OUT YOUR FEET — AND HE STRETCHED; CLOSE YOUR MOUTH — AND HE CLOSED; SHUT YOUR EYES — AND HE SHUT; AT THAT HOUR MOSES SAID: HAPPY IS HE WHO DIES THIS DEATH! THEREFORE IT IS SAID "AS AARON YOUR BROTHER DIED" — A DEATH YOU COVETED. (מִיתָה שֶׁחָמַדְתָּ לָהּ "a death you coveted"; הִכָּנֵס לַמְּעָרָה וְנִכְנַס "enter the cave, and he entered"; אַשְׁרֵי מִי שֶׁמֵּת בְּמִיתָה זוֹ "happy is he who dies this death"): AARON'S DEATH RETOLD — Numbers 20:25-28 on the tape (the garments passed to Eleazar; a RUN CITATION) filled with the cave, the bier and the six commands (the translator: Avot deRabbi Natan A 12) — a RETELLING, the readback's reference row, never a second act; the last row of the piska; no law (DV32A-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 340:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:51. "BECAUSE YOU TRESPASSED AGAINST ME" (32:51) — YOU CAUSED TRESPASS AGAINST ME; "BECAUSE YOU DID NOT SANCTIFY ME" — YOU CAUSED ME NOT TO BE SANCTIFIED; "AS YOU REBELLED AGAINST MY WORD" (Numbers 27:14) — YOU CAUSED REBELLION AGAINST MY WORD. The head row of piska 340 (אַתֶּם גְּרַמְתֶּם לִמְעֹל בִּי "you caused trespass against Me"; אַתֶּם גְּרַמְתֶּם שֶׁלֹּא לְקַדֵּשׁ אוֹתִי "you caused Me not to be sanctified"): THE CAUSATIVE READING THRICE — Meribah's sin (Numbers 20:12-13 on the tape, RUN CITATIONS) as Israel's causing; the third clause the kin's own seat (Numbers 27:14, the commission's wording — the Hebrew marker names it inside 32:51's exposition); the compile: the sentence's reason clause (DV32A-04). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 340:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:51. THE HOLY ONE SAID TO MOSES: DID I NOT SAY TO YOU "WHAT IS THAT IN YOUR HAND" (Exodus 4:2), "CAST IT ON THE GROUND" (Exodus 4:3) — AND YOU CAST IT; THE SIGNS THAT WERE IN YOUR HAND YOU DID NOT DELAY — THIS LIGHT THING YOU SHOULD HAVE DELAYED. AND WHENCE THAT HE DID NOT DEPART FROM THE WORLD UNTIL THE HOLY ONE BOUND IT UP FOR HIM IN HIS WINGS? AS IT IS SAID "THEREFORE YOU SHALL NOT BRING THIS ASSEMBLY" (Numbers 20:12). (״מַה אוֹתוֹת שֶׁבְּיָדְךָ לֹא עִכַּבְתָּה "the signs in your hand you did not delay"; עַד שֶׁצְּרָרָהּ לוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בִּכְנָפָיו "until the Holy One bound it up for him in His wings"): THE STAFF'S SIGN AGAINST THE ROCK'S WORD (Exodus 4:2-3 on the tape, a RUN CITATION) — the obedience at the bush the measure of the failure at the rock; the bundling in the wings (the translator's surmise: the Land as the world to come); the last row of the piska; no law (DV32A-04). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 341:1 — MATERIAL. On 32:52. "FOR YOU SHALL SEE THE LAND BEFORE YOU, BUT YOU SHALL NOT GO THERE" (32:52) — IT IS SAID HERE "YOU SHALL NOT GO THERE" AND IT IS SAID THERE "YOU SHALL NOT CROSS THERE" (34:4); ONE CANNOT SAY "YOU SHALL NOT CROSS THERE" SINCE "YOU SHALL NOT GO THERE" IS ALREADY SAID, AND ONE CANNOT SAY "YOU SHALL NOT GO THERE" SINCE "YOU SHALL NOT CROSS THERE" IS ALREADY SAID. MOSES SAID BEFORE THE HOLY ONE: IF I DO NOT ENTER IT AS A KING, LET ME ENTER IT AS A COMMONER; IF I DO NOT ENTER IT ALIVE, LET ME ENTER IT DEAD. THE HOLY ONE SAID TO HIM: "YOU SHALL NOT GO THERE", "YOU SHALL NOT CROSS THERE" — NOT AS KING AND NOT AS COMMONER, NOT ALIVE AND NOT DEAD. The one row of piska 341 (אִם אֵינִי נִכְנָס לָהּ מֶלֶךְ אֶכָּנֵס לָהּ "if I do not enter it as a king, let me enter it"; לֹא מֶלֶךְ וְלֹא הֶדְיוֹט לֹא חַי וְלֹא "not as king and not as commoner, not alive and not"): THE SPINE'S LAST ROW ON THE CHAPTER — 357:27's twin (the outside row read at RUN 2 from the death's piska: 34:4 with 32:52, the same teaching from the other seat); the two negatives read as four (king, commoner, alive, dead — the translator: Mekhilta Amalek 2, Sifrei Numbers 135); the compile: the sentence's two negatives, the seeing granted and the entering denied (DV32A-04). Fresh.

## Sifrei Devarim rows citing chapter 32 from outside the spine (11 rows found by the union of both files' citations over the dump, each read whole in both files; 8 read before and reread whole; 3 fresh from the piskaot ahead; none excluded)
- Sifrei Devarim 1:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_01_03_devarim_2026-09-15.md, deu_29_31_nitzavim_vayelech_2026-09-27.md). On 1:1; cites 32:1, 32:15. "THESE ARE THE WORDS MOSES SPOKE" (1:1) — DID MOSES PROPHESY ONLY THESE? DID HE NOT WRITE THE WHOLE TORAH — "AND MOSES WROTE THIS LAW" (31:9)! WHAT THEN IS "THESE ARE THE WORDS"? IT TEACHES THEY WERE WORDS OF REBUKE — "AND JESHURUN GREW FAT AND KICKED" (32:15). The book's first row read now for ITS PROOF TEXT (מְלַמֵּד שֶׁהָיוּ דִּבְרֵי תוֹכָחוֹת "it teaches they were words of rebuke"; ״וַיִּשְׁמַן יְשֻׁרוּן וַיִּבְעָט״ "and Jeshurun grew fat and kicked"): the whole book's words are rebuke BECAUSE the song says Jeshurun kicked — 32:15 the seat that names the book's genre; the translator's note sends the verse to piska 318 (the spine's own row, RUN 3). The marker for 31:9 reads 31:22 (sitting 19's finding; the misprint is 31:9's, not this chapter's). The compile: no law — the song's verse a status on the book's speech (DV32-06). REREAD WHOLE (chapter 1's reading, and sitting 19's for 31:9).
- Sifrei Devarim 37:11 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_01_03_devarim_2026-09-15.md, deu_11_ekev_reeh_2026-09-20.md). On 11:10; cites 32:49. "GO UP TO THIS MOUNTAIN OF THE ABARIM, MOUNT NEBO" (32:49) AND ELSEWHERE "GO UP TO THE TOP OF PISGAH" (3:27) — ONE PLACE CALLED BY THREE NAMES; WHAT NEED HAVE THE WORLD'S CREATURES OF THIS? THREE KINGDOMS QUARRELED OVER IT, EACH SAYING: LET IT BE CALLED BY MY NAME — IF OVER THE REFUSE OF THE MOUNTAINS OF THE LAND OF ISRAEL THREE KINGDOMS QUARREL, HOW MUCH MORE THE LAND'S PRAISE. (נִמְצָא קָרוּי שְׁלֹשָׁה שֵׁמוֹת "it turns out it is called three names"; שָׁלֹשׁ מַלְכֻיּוֹת מִתְכַּתְּשׁוֹת עָלָיו "three kingdoms contending over it"): THE MOUNTAIN'S THREE NAMES — Abarim, Nebo, Pisgah — one seat; the a-fortiori (qal vachomer, I1) from the mountain to the land's praise the row's move (the compile: a place identity, the Abarim = Nebo = Pisgah, a REFERENCE the ink itself makes at 32:49 and 34:1; 3:27's Pisgah on the tape). REREAD WHOLE (chapters 1-3's reading and chapter 11's).
- Sifrei Devarim 39:11 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_11_ekev_reeh_2026-09-20.md). On 11:11; cites 32:2. ANOTHER WORD: AS RAINS ARE FOR BLESSING SO DEWS ARE FOR BLESSING — "MAY GOD GIVE YOU OF THE DEW OF HEAVEN" (Genesis 27:28), "MY DOCTRINE SHALL DROP AS THE RAIN" (32:2), "I WILL BE AS THE DEW TO ISRAEL" (Hosea 14:6), "THE REMNANT OF JACOB SHALL BE AS DEW FROM THE LORD" (Micah 5:6). (מָה גְּשָׁמִים לִבְרָכָה אַף טְלָלִים לִבְרָכָה "as rains for blessing so dews for blessing"): 32:2 cited among the dew verses in chapter 11's rain piska — the song's rain and dew both blessings (the spine's 306:17 makes the dew the one all rejoice in); the Prophets' seats cited, never read. The compile: no law (DV32-01). REREAD WHOLE (chapter 11's reading).
- Sifrei Devarim 43:24 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_11_ekev_reeh_2026-09-20.md). On 11:15; cites 32:23. "AND THE LORD'S ANGER BE KINDLED AGAINST YOU" (11:17) — SHALL I HEAR THAT THEY PERISH FROM THE WORLD? IT SAYS "AND HE SHUT UP THE HEAVENS": KINDS OF PUNISHMENT ARE SPENT, NOT THEY; AND SO IT SAYS "MY ARROWS I WILL SPEND ON THEM" (32:23) — MY ARROWS ARE SPENT AND THEY ARE NOT SPENT; AND "THE LORD HAS SPENT HIS FURY" (Lamentations 4:11). (מִינֵי פֻּרְעָנִיּוֹת הֵם כָּלִים "kinds of punishments are spent"; וְהֵם אֵינָם כָּלִים "and they are not spent"): 32:23 READ AS A PROMISE — the verb 'spend' (kallah) turned from Israel to the arrows: the plagues end, the people do not (the spine's 321 the seat, RUN 3). - Sifrei Devarim 48:2 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_04_vaetchanan_2026-09-16.md, deu_11_ekev_reeh_2026-09-20.md). On 11:22; cites 32:47. R. ISHMAEL SAID: "ONLY TAKE HEED TO YOURSELF AND KEEP YOUR SOUL DILIGENTLY" (4:9) — A PARABLE OF A KING WHO CAUGHT A BIRD AND GAVE IT TO HIS SERVANT: GUARD THIS BIRD FOR MY SON; IF YOU LOSE IT, DO NOT THINK YOU LOST A BIRD WORTH A PENNY — IT IS AS IF YOU LOST YOUR LIFE; AND SO IT SAYS "FOR IT IS NO EMPTY THING FOR YOU" (32:47) — THE THING YOU CALL EMPTY, "IT IS YOUR LIFE". (כְּאִלּוּ נַפְשְׁךָ אִבַּדְתָּ "as if you lost your life"; שֶׁאַתֶּם אוֹמְרִים רֵיק הוּא "that you say is empty"): 32:47 THE PROOF that the Torah's smallest word is one's life — the bird's parable (Avot de-Rabbi Natan A 24 the parallel, cited); the compile: the charge's promise (it is your life) a status, no law (DV32A-02). - Sifrei Devarim 48:10 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_08_ekev_2026-09-18.md, deu_11_ekev_reeh_2026-09-20.md). On 11:22; cites 32:47. SHIMON BEN MENASYA: "TOO" INCLUDES HIS FATHER IN HEAVEN. (לְפִי שֶׁאָמַר מֹשֶׁה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל דְּבָרִים קָשִׁים תְּחִלָּה "because Moses spoke hard words to Israel first"; חָזַר וְאָמַר לָהֶם דִּבְרֵי נִחוּמִים "he turned and spoke to them words of consolation"): THE SONG'S HARDEST VERSES (32:24-25) named as the reason for the blessing — the row read here for its two quotations of this chapter, the head (33:1) the next chapter's and the piska not opened (READ THEN COMPILE PER PORTION); the HEADLESS piska 342 opens with 33:1's citation inside the row (the dump's head None). - Sifrei Devarim 346:2 — MATERIAL. On 33:5; cites 32:3. "TOGETHER THE TRIBES OF ISRAEL" (33:5) — WHEN THEY ARE ONE BAND, NOT WHEN THEY ARE BANDS AND BANDS; "HE BUILDS HIS UPPER CHAMBERS IN THE HEAVENS AND HIS BAND HE FOUNDED ON THE EARTH" (Amos 9:6); R. SHIMON BEN YOCHAI: ONE WHO BROUGHT TWO SHIPS, BOUND THEM WITH ANCHORS AND IRON AND BUILT PALACES ON THEM — WHILE THE SHIPS ARE BOUND THE PALACES STAND; SO ISRAEL DOING HIS WILL BUILDS HIS CHAMBERS IN THE HEAVENS; "THIS IS MY GOD AND I WILL GLORIFY HIM" (Exodus 15:2) — WHEN I ACKNOWLEDGE HIM HE IS GLORIOUS; LIKEWISE "FOR I WILL PROCLAIM THE NAME OF THE LORD" (32:3) — WHEN I CALL ON HIS NAME HE IS GREAT, AND IF NOT, AS IT WERE …; "YOU ARE MY WITNESSES, SAYS THE LORD, AND I AM GOD" (Isaiah 43:12); "TO YOU I LIFT MY EYES, WHO SIT IN THE HEAVENS" (Psalm 123:1). (יַחַד שִׁבְטֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל כְּשֶׁהֵם עֲשׂוּיִם אֲגֻדָּה אַחַת "together the tribes of Israel — when they are one band"; קוֹרֵא בִּשְׁמוֹ הוּא גָּדוֹל "when I call on His name He is great"): 32:3 READ AS A CONDITION — His greatness proclaimed depends on the caller (the 'as it were', kivyakhol, guarding the theology, the phrase abbreviated in the manuscripts per the translator); the row from the blessing's piska on 33:5 read here for its teaching on 32:3, the piska not opened. Mishnah Avot 3:6 the parallel (the testing shelf, cited). The compile: no law (DV32-01); the Prophets' and the Writings' seats cited, never read. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 355:6 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in gen_22_covenant_bow_2026-08-25.md). On 33:20; cites 32:49. "FOR THERE THE PORTION OF THE LAWGIVER IS HIDDEN" (33:20) — THIS IS MOSES' BURIAL PLACE, GIVEN IN GAD'S PORTION; BUT DID HE NOT DIE IN REUBEN'S — "GO UP TO THIS MOUNTAIN OF THE ABARIM, MOUNT NEBO" (32:49), AND NEBO IS REUBEN'S (Numbers 32:37-38)! IT TEACHES MOSES WAS CARRIED ON THE WINGS OF THE SHEKHINAH FOUR MIL FROM REUBEN'S PORTION TO GAD'S, THE MINISTERING ANGELS LAMENTING: HE ENTERS IN PEACE, HE RESTS ON HIS BED; AND THIS IS ONE OF THE THINGS CREATED ON THE EVE OF THE SABBATH AT TWILIGHT — THE RAINBOW, THE MANNA, THE WELL, THE WRITING AND THE SCRIPT AND THE TABLETS, THE MOUTH OF THE ASS, THE MOUTH OF THE EARTH, MOSES' GRAVE, THE CAVE OF MOSES AND ELIJAH, AARON'S STAFF WITH ITS ALMONDS AND BLOSSOMS; SOME SAY ADAM'S GARMENTS; SOME SAY THE TUNICS AND THE DEMONS; R. YOSHIYAH IN HIS FATHER'S NAME: THE RAM AND THE SHAMIR; R. NEHEMIAH: THE FIRE AND THE MULE; R. YEHUDAH: THE TONGS — TONGS ARE MADE WITH TONGS, THE FIRST TONGS WERE THEY NOT A CREATION? THEY SAID: ONE COULD MAKE THEM IN A MOLD — SO NOT A CREATION. (זוֹ קְבוּרָתוֹ שֶׁל מֹשֶׁה "this is Moses' burial place"; וְאֵין נְבוֹ אֶלָּא חֶלְקוֹ שֶׁל רְאוּבֵן "and Nebo is only Reuben's portion"; בְּעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת "on the eve of the Sabbath at twilight"): 32:49 THE PROOF that Moses died in Reuben's land — the mountain placed by Numbers 32:38 (the tape's own entry, a RUN CITATION); the grave among the twilight creations (Mishnah Avot 5:6 the parallel, cited — the testing shelf; the rainbow the seat that read this row at the Genesis ledger of the covenant's bow). The row from the blessing's piska on 33:20 read here for 32:49, the piska not opened. The compile: the burial a tape event at 34:6 (ahead, cited); no law here (DV32A-03). REREAD WHOLE (the Genesis ledger gen_22_covenant_bow's reading — the rainbow among the twilight creations).
- Sifrei Devarim 356:5 — MATERIAL. On 33:27; cites 32:12. "ALONE" (33:28) — NOT LIKE THE "ALONE" MOSES SAID, "THE LORD ALONE LED HIM" (32:12), NOR LIKE THE "ALONE" JEREMIAH SAID, "BECAUSE OF YOUR HAND I SAT ALONE" (Jeremiah 15:17), BUT LIKE THE "ALONE" THAT WICKED MAN SAID: "BEHOLD, A PEOPLE THAT DWELLS ALONE" (Numbers 23:9). (אִם אֵינִי נִכְנָס בָּהּ חַי "if I do not enter it alive"; לֹא מֶלֶךְ וְלֹא הֶדְיוֹט "not king nor commoner"; לֹא חַי וְלֹא מֵת "not alive nor dead"): 32:52'S "NOT COME" AND 34:4'S "NOT CROSS" READ AS TWO DECREES against two pleas — the second verse closes the door the first left (the kin the ink found: 32:52 and 34:4 share 'and there you shall not'); the row from the last piska on 34:1-5 read here for 32:52, the piska not opened (34:4 ahead cited, never read; the translator sends the theme to piska 341:1, the spine's own row on 32:52 — RUN 4). "יִמְצָאֵהוּ בְּאֶרֶץ מִדְבָּר" (He found him in a desert land — one of the eleven verses with no two-token kin (the ink)) "וּבְתֹהוּ יְלֵל יְשִׁמֹן" (and in the waste, a howling wilderness — tohu of Genesis 1:2) "יְסֹֽבְבֶנְהוּ יְבוֹנְנֵהוּ יִצְּרֶנְהוּ כְּאִישׁוֹן עֵינֽוֹ" (He compassed him, cared for him, kept him as the little man (pupil) of His eye — ONCE in this form) סַפִּיק צָרְכֵּיהוֹן בְּאַרְעָא מַדְבְּרָא (He SUPPLIED THEIR NEEDS in the desert land — finding rendered providing) אַשְׁרִנּוּן סְחוֹר סְחוֹר לְשְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ (He made them dwell round about HIS SHEKHINAH — compassing rendered the camp around the Presence) אַלֵּפִנּוּן פִּתְגָּמֵי אוֹרַיְתָא (He TAUGHT THEM THE WORDS OF THE TORAH — caring rendered teaching: THE DESERT THE TORAH'S SCHOOL (eleven Hebrew words to twenty Aramaic — the measure)) נְטָרִנּוּן כְּבָבַת עֵינֵיהוֹן (He guarded them as the pupil of their eyes) Onkelos writes the meaning at its fullest in the chapter: the wilderness the school, the camp the Shekhinah's ring, the eye theirs. "כְּנֶשֶׁר יָעִיר קִנּוֹ" (as an eagle stirs up her nest — 'as an eagle' once in the Torah, eleven in the Bible (the ink); Exodus 19:4's eagles' wings the twin (one shared token)) "עַל גּוֹזָלָיו יְרַחֵף" (hovers over her young — Genesis 1:2's hovering (merachefet) in another form) "יִפְרֹשׂ כְּנָפָיו יִקָּחֵהוּ יִשָּׂאֵהוּ עַל אֶבְרָתֽוֹ" (spreads her wings, takes him, bears him on her pinion) כְּנִשְׁרָא דִּמְחִישׁ לְקִנֵּיהּ (as an eagle that HASTENS to its nest — stirring rendered hastening) מְקַבֶּלְהוֹן מְנַטֶּלְהוֹן עַל תְּקוֹף אֱבָרוֹהִי (receives them, carries them on THE STRENGTH of its pinions — the pinion given its strength) The eagle the tradition's figure for the exodus (Exodus 19:4 the kin, compiled at the mountain). - Onkelos Deut 32:12 — MATERIAL. THE LORD ALONE DID LEAD HIM, AND THERE WAS NO STRANGE GOD WITH HIM. "יְהוָה בָּדָד יַנְחֶנּוּ" (the LORD ALONE led him — badad; Sifrei 356:5 (outside, fresh) sets it against Jeremiah 15:17's and Numbers 23:9's alone) "וְאֵין עִמּוֹ אֵל נֵכָֽר" (and no strange god with Him — El the second in the song (32:4, 12, 18, 21 — the ink)) יְיָ בִּלְחוֹדוֹהִי עֲתִיד לְאַשְׁרָיוּתְהוֹן (the LORD alone IS DESTINED TO MAKE THEM DWELL — the past made the future) בְּעָלְמָא דְּהוּא עָתִיד לְאִתְחַדָּתָא (in the world that is to be RENEWED — THE WORLD TO COME written into the wilderness (seven Hebrew words to thirteen Aramaic — the measure)) וְלָא יִתְקַיַּם קֳדָמוֹהִי פָּלְחַן טַעֲוָן (and no service of idols shall stand before Him) Onkelos writes the meaning: the verse a promise of the renewed world, not a memory of the desert — the translator's one eschatology in the chapter. - Onkelos Deut 32:13 — MATERIAL. HE MADE HIM RIDE ON THE HIGH PLACES OF THE EARTH AND HE ATE THE FRUITAGE OF THE FIELD; HE MADE HIM SUCK HONEY OUT OF THE CRAG AND OIL OUT OF THE FLINTY ROCK. "לוּלֵי כַּעַס אוֹיֵב אָגוּר" (were it not that I feared the enemy's provocation — the divine 'I fear' (agur)) "פֶּֽן יְנַכְּרוּ צָרֵימוֹ" (lest their adversaries misjudge — 'lest' twice in the verse, the chapter's two (the ink)) "פֶּֽן יֹֽאמְרוּ יָדֵינוּ רָמָה" (lest they say: our hand is high — 9:28's 'lest they say' the song's kin inside the book) "וְלֹא יְהוָה פָּעַל כָּל זֹֽאת" (and not the LORD has done all this) אִלּוּלָפוֹן רָגְזָא דְסָנְאָה כְּנִישׁ (were it not that the enemy's anger is GATHERED — the dread dropped from God) דִּילְמָא יִתְרַבְרַב בְּעֵל דְּבָבָא (lest the adversary BOAST — misjudging rendered boasting) וְלָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ הֲוַת כָּל דָּא (and not from before the LORD was all this — from before (min kodam) again) Onkelos writes the meaning: God does not fear; the enemy's anger is stored.
