- THE WORDS: the Name SEVEN times bare, once with lamed (32:6), once with vav (32:30); THE SONG NEVER SAYS "THE LORD YOUR GOD" — the book's refrain absent from its song; El at 32:4, 12, 18, 21; Eloah (the rare singular) at 32:15 and 32:17; Elohim at 32:17, 32:39; the Most High 32:8; the names Bashan, Jeshurun, Sheol, HOSHEA son of Nun (32:44 — the old name of Numbers 13:16), Nebo, the Abarim, Moab, Jericho, Canaan, Aaron and Hor, Kadesh and Zin; vengeance thrice (32:35, 41, 43); the negations 12; "for" (ki) nineteen — the song's own connective; "if" at 32:30 and 32:41; "lest" twice at 32:27; "saying" 32:48 alone; the seven "?" glosses of the store all the pronoun "I" (32:39 thrice, 32:40, 32:46, 32:49, 32:52 — the tail's display patch, sitting 19's precedent).
- ONKELOS OVER THE CHAPTER (asserted in the ink; read at the rows): the Rock RENDERED "the Mighty One" (takifa) at 32:4, 13, 14, 15, 18, 27, 30, 37 — twenty-two in the book; 32:4 doubled fourteen to nineteen; 32:10 THE DESERT THE TORAH'S SCHOOL — eleven to twenty; 32:12 THE WORLD TO COME — the world that is to be renewed and no service of idols before Him (the age at 32:7, 12, 40, 33:6, 33:27); 32:14 the curds and the milk the spoil of their kings; Jeshurun "Israel" (32:15); the demons "in whom there is no use" (32:17); "I will remove My Shekhinah from them" (32:20 — 31:17's and 31:18's rendering; 320:3's Hebrew); "the house of My Shekhinah in heaven" for the lifted hand (32:40); the vine of Sodom "the punishment of the people of Sodom" (32:32); "vengeance is Mine" "at the time they go into exile" (32:35); the fat of the sacrifices (32:38); the Torah into 32:6 and 32:46 beside 32:10; Meribah "the waters of the strife of Rekem" (32:51, with 33:8); the selfsame day "in the middle of this day" (32:48); the song (tushbachta, "praise") at 32:44 with 31:22, 31:30; 32:30's Aramaic carries a parenthesized variant uncut.

## The finds (this ledger's crowns)
- THE SPINE IN FORCE — thirty-six piskaot on one chapter, 249 rows read whole over four runs by the split's byte plan (306 alone 95,532 bytes; the slices 306 / 307-316 / 317-322 / 323-338 / 339-341), thirty rows read before over twenty-four ledgers (twelve of them 306's — the Genesis ledgers cite the song's first piska on the creation and the flood) REREAD WHOLE and marked by computation; zero cut misses on 312 rows in the whole-spine check's first run; the reread marks equal to the computed set at every run.
- THE WITNESSES AND THE COURT LAW — 306:9 reads the two witnesses of 17:6-7 into heaven and earth, 306:12 the witnesses who must agree, 323:5 THE ENEMY DISQUALIFIED as witness and judge from Numbers 35:23 ("and You have set over us enemies as witnesses and judges"): the court's compiled rules cited by the song's own reading three times; 306:14 the witnesses' commandments (the calendar the heavens', the land's gifts the earth's), 306:15 the song the Holy One's witness beside Moses' two (sitting 19's row reread), 333:2 the witnesses called back at the song's close to sing (the heavens, the earth, the mountains, the trees, the fathers and mothers).
- THE MEASURE'S TWINS TAUGHT — three of five twins by sense now carry the Link Review Law's teacher: Isaiah 1:2 with 32:1 (306:11-12), 31:20 with 32:15 (318:1 — the Holy One's words, then Moses' 8:12-14), Exodus 19:4 with 32:11 (314:3); and the frame's kin taught at 337:1 (the selfsame day — Genesis 7:13, Exodus 12:41, 32:48: the three seats where a protest was dared, "at midday" the answer) and 334:1 (31:1's "went" against 32:44's "came" — the successor); Leviticus 26:22 and Genesis 11:8 UNTAUGHT — kin by sense, no link of our own.
- THE SPINE SPEAKS ONKELOS'S WORDS — 320:3 "behold, I remove My Shekhinah from among them" is 32:20's Aramaic in Hebrew; 32:10's desert the Torah's school in both (313:1-15); 32:12's world to come in Onkelos and 333:5's "this world and the world to come" in the spine; the Rock "the Mighty One" and 307's Rock; the translator and the spine one reading of the hidden face and the song's tense.
- THE PARSER'S TWO GUARDS CONFIRMED BY THE SHELF — 318:4-5 read shamanta ("you grew fat", the false eight) as a verb dated to Jeroboam and Ahaz; 322:10, 322:12 and 323:2 read 32:30 as FOUR numbers (one, a thousand, two, ten thousand) turned forward and back — the joined thousand [1, 1002] refused by the tradition's own arithmetic; 311:5's seventy against a hundred and forty and 315:3's conversion (25,000 reeds = 75 mil) the spine's own number lines; the compile (20b) guards both.
- THE COMPILER LAW IN THE TRADITION'S WORDS — 317:2 lists the offering classes under 32:13-14 (the first fruits' baskets, the oil and wine libations, the sin, burnt, peace, thanksgiving and guilt offerings, the meal offerings — a REFERENCE list of the compiled spans), 317:3 the Torah's forms with the middot named among the dishes and the laws "the body of the Torah" (Mishnah Chagigah 1:8; 335:1's mountains hanging by a hair), 313:8 the middot seen in each of the ten utterances; 336:2 THE ANSWER SHEET INSIDE THE SPINE — Mishnah Peah 1:1's list each given its verse with the nest (22:7) added.
- THE RULES FROM THE VERSES — 321:9 "in war gather in the foot, in famine scatter it" (Bava Kamma 60b); 328:4 THE NAME PROFANED PUNISHED AT ONCE, all else pardoned; 333:4 THE LAND ATONES (R. Meir — the iniquities unloaded, not borne; the Shema morning and evening and the holy tongue the world to come's conditions); 322:11 the nations' seven commandments; 329:4 no father delivers a son nor brother a brother, the soul beyond ransom (Exodus 21:30's kofer denied); 323:3 "delivered up" read by the leper's "shut up" (Leviticus 13:4) — the compile's statuses and cases.
- THE SONG'S THREE TIMES AND TWO WORLDS (333:5) — the spine's own verdict on the song's tense: the past the tape's (the desert, the eagle, the honey — RUN CITATIONS), the present the charge's, the future a POINTER (the vengeance, the nations' song, the resurrection's four hints at 329:2-3) — THE RETELLING RULES' three kinds in the tradition's words; 339:3 Aaron's death RETOLD with the cave and the bier (the readback's reference row, never a second act).
- SIX "DO NOT READ" REVOWELINGS (318:10 the Rock spurned him; 318:16 the fathers paid them no mind — Cain's gift; 320:6 no Amen in them; 320:8 a mixed people; 321:6 a taste for bestiality; 321:14 the man of the study house) and 328:2's defective suffix ("let Him help you" possible) — readings the parser cannot make, carried as the shelf's alternatives; 338:2's Abarim read as four and Nebo as prophets.
- THE FEAST OF 32:14 DATED AND DIVIDED — Solomon's and the ten tribes' days (317:1), the altar's classes (317:2), the Torah's forms (317:3), the four kingdoms' oppressors (317:4), Rome's officers (317:5 — the Roman lexicon in Hebrew letters: governors, tribunes, centurions, beneficiarii, senators, matrons; 309:1's councilman and prefect; 327:2's consuls and governors; 330:2's governor and province; 328:1's provisions, donatives and salt-money), the future wheat (317:6-7); the compile: the song's abundance a retelling ahead of the tape, no law.
- THE HIDDEN FACE THE THIRD TIME — 32:20 with 31:17-18: Onkelos's Shekhinah removed at all three, 320:3's Hebrew the same; 320:4 the children still children, 320:5-13 the fickle generation, no Amen after the prophets until Jeremiah, the sectarians, the fire from Gehenna (the place of punishment), the four kingdoms — a STATUS on Israel the compile installs at 31:17 and reaffirms here.
- THE VINE OF SODOM AND THE CUP IN STORE — 323:6-13 R. Yehudah of Israel and R. Nechemiah of the nations (the two-reading frame thrice: 322, 323, 327); 324:1 Psalm 75:9's cup drunk by seven generations and hosts, the golden cup mended and the earthen not; 324:2-3 the deeds' fruit and both rewards deferred to the redemption; 325:1 "Myself, not an angel nor a messenger"; 326:1 joy over the nations and regret over Israel (Genesis 6:7's "I repent" — the flood prologue's row reread); 326:2-7 the seven signs of the hand gone.
- THE FRAME — 334:1 the successor came and the authority passed (diadochos, "the one who takes over"), 334:3 HOSHEA IN HIS RIGHTEOUSNESS with Joseph and David (the old name kept — the measure's Numbers 13:16 kin taught), 335:1 heart, eyes and ears, 335:2 the teacher's gratitude to the disciples, 336:1 no empty verse (Timna's line), 337:1 the selfsame day, 338:1-3 the ascent, the four names, Ruth's kings and Rahab's prophets, the finger of the Holy One; 339:1 the law of the tent (Numbers 19:14) read as death's decree equal for every man, the angels' question on the first Adam; 340:1 "you caused them" thrice; 341:1 not as king nor commoner, not alive nor dead (357:27's twin from the other seat).
- THE EXPORT'S FAULTS AT THE READ — 328's head misprinted (32:35 for 32:38 — the Hebrew marker; the ink's first-pass fall), 321:4's marker 32:27 for 32:24's words, 322:3's marker garbled, 335:1's Ezekiel 40:4 for 44:5's words, 340:1's Numbers 27:14 inside 32:51's exposition (the kin's own seat); 306:29's English a stray fragment; the English column misaligned at 320:6-7 and duplicated at 321:7-8; English citations misprinted at 307:9, 313:1, 318:8, 318:15-16, 319:1, 319:3, 333:2 (Isaiah 43:11 for 42:11), 337:1 (Exodus 12:17 for 12:41); 318:1's copyist's Aramaic notes for three abridgements filled at piska 43 and Ezekiel 16:48 for 16:49; 32:30's Aramaic with a parenthesized variant uncut; the store's qere at 32:13 — the Hebrew's markers govern every seat, the rows seated by their own words.
- THE POINTERS AHEAD, OWED — chapter 33 (33:5, 33:6 at 329:3, 33:8, 33:19, 33:26-27; the piskaot 342-357 whose rows 342:1, 346:2, 355:6, 356:5, 357:27 were read here for their teaching on this chapter), chapter 34 (34:1-4 the sight at 338:3, 34:4 at 341:1, 34:5-6 the death and the cave at 337:1 and 339:1 — the three gifts' daemon owed), Joshua 23:10, Psalm 135:14, Isaiah 1:2, and the rows' seats — Isaiah 3:10-11, 5:19, 14:2, 24:22, 25:6, 26:6, 33:24, 34:6, 42:11, 43:3-4, 44:6, 44:23, 54:13, 55:12, 65:6; Jeremiah 1:8, 2:21, 8:23, 16:18, 30:11, 51:7; Ezekiel 23:34, 36:7, 39:17-20, 44:5; Hosea 6:2; Joel 4:2, 4:19-21; Micah 7:1; Habakkuk 1:8; Psalms 31:20, 49:8-9, 75:9, 79:1-3, 106:23, 106:26, 106:30, 139:21; Daniel 12:7; Ezra 4:1; 1 Samuel 15:11, 17:14-15; 2 Samuel 7:19; 2 Kings 19:35; and the earlier runs' seats in the map's design.
